# Honkai: Star Rail 98

What *Honkai: Star Rail* could have looked like as a 1999 PC CD-ROM JRPG. The combat follows the modern game: an action-value timeline, a shared Skill Point pool, Ultimates that fire out of turn, elemental Weakness Break and Techniques used on the map. Three systems come from *Persona 2* (1999): **Contact** (talking to enemies), **Fusion** (combined attacks by two party members) and **Rumors** (a rumor you spread becomes true). The story follows the version 1.0 Trailblaze Missions from the Herta Space Station to Jarilo-VI and ends with the Cocolia fight and the departure of the Express. Its dialogue is adapted from the original missions and told through dialogue scenes on painted backdrops and 29 letterboxed cinematics that stand in for the original mission animations. Version 1.3 adds the Simulated Universe, Herta's machine of runs through four worlds with blessings, curios and Path Resonance, and Planar Ornaments, two relic slots that only the Simulated Universe fills. The game was written from scratch for the Win 98 desktop and shares no code with the other games.

- Launch it from the desktop, the Start menu, the Run command (`starrail`, `hsr`, `hsr98`, `honkai`, `хонкай`), the Steam client (after downloading it there) or `C:\Games\StarRail98\STARRAIL.EXE`. The game is free: it can be taken in Steam or downloaded from its official site `http://hsr.hoyoverse.com` (see «Official site» below).
- Run it standalone at `hsr98.html`. The page exposes `window.hsrui`.
- Copy the game out of the project with `npm run build:hsr98` (`node tools/hsr98-standalone.cjs [folder]`, default `dist/HonkaiStarRail98`, about 25 MB). The folder needs nothing from Win 98 and no server: open `index.html` by double-click. The tool copies the scripts listed in `hsr98.html` and `css/apps/hsr98.css` unchanged into `game/`, packs every `content/music/` file that the scripts name into `music/<name>.js` (base64), and puts `game/files.js` first. That file loads a pack with a `<script>` tag when the game fetches a `content/music/` URL, because `fetch` does not work for `file://` pages. Run the tool again after changing the game. It wipes only a folder that it made itself. Saves live in that page's `localStorage`, apart from the saves of the Win 98 copy. `npm run test:hsr98-standalone` checks the copy.
- The copy has touch controls (`tools/hsr98-standalone/touch.js` and `touch.css`, loaded after the game). They appear on touch screens and hide when a real keyboard key is pressed. Each button sends the same `keydown`/`keyup` that the keyboard sends, so the game code is unchanged. Left: a D-pad with key repeat. Right: A (Enter), B (Esc), Q, E and a row of keys for the current mode (field: F, M, Tab, Shift held as «Шаг», 1–4; battle: T, R, V, Shift «»», 1–4; screens: Tab, F, X, A, V, Shift, PgUp, PgDn). Top left: «Клавиши» opens a panel with every key the game reads, F7 (+jade), F8 (debug menu, which needs a started game) and «Скрыть» (brings back «Пульт»). A tap on the picture becomes a mouse click. A vertical swipe becomes wheel scrolling.
- Build an Android APK with `npm run build:hsr98-apk` (`node tools/hsr98-apk.cjs [file]`, default `dist/StarRail98.apk`, about 16 MB, package `com.win98clone.hsr98`, minSdk 24, targetSdk 34, landscape). It needs no Gradle. The tool takes the SDK from `ANDROID_HOME` or from the newest Unity editor's AndroidPlayer (SDK and OpenJDK). It builds the copy into `assets/www`, compiles `tools/hsr98-standalone/android/MainActivity.java` (a full-screen WebView), runs aapt2, d8, zipalign and apksigner, and signs with `~/.android/hsr98.keystore` (or `HSR98_KEYSTORE`), which it creates on the first run. Keep that file: an update installs over the old app only when the same key signs it. Assets are added with `aapt add`, because aapt2 `-A` on Win writes `\` into the paths. The WebView serves them at `https://appassets.androidplatform.net/`, so saves stay in that origin's `localStorage`. The system Back button sends Esc. Pausing the app suspends the game's AudioContext.
- Run the tests with `npm run test:hsr98`, which runs `node tests/hsr98.test.js` (about 30 s, ~61 700 checks). The suite checks the data and maps, validates every script line (speakers, labels, menus, gender forms, no em dashes), simulates boss fights, plays the whole story headless, drives every UI screen against a stub canvas, draws every shot of every cinematic and every backdrop, builds the art of every enemy, map, map object and battle backdrop, checks the software 3D rasterizer plays the Warp cinematic for every rarity, and plays the Ultimate cutscene of every character with all, one or no targets, then checks in a battle that the cutscene holds the damage until it ends, follows the battle speed, skips on Esc and obeys the three Settings modes. The same is checked for the Fusion cutscene of every Fusion (both lines shown, the team beat present exactly when the Fusion helps the team), and in a battle with March 7th, Dan Heng and the Trailblazer. It also renders the Eidolon picture of every character at every stage, plays the assembly of new pieces and checks that every fragment text fits the panel, and plays the Warp intro of every character the Warp can give (both ticket colours, every frame from the Path sign to the card, the line for both Trailblazers), checks that its sounds play once and in order, that Enter jumps to the card without a burst of skipped sounds and that the card shows the line and the duplicate result.

## Controls

| Where | Keys |
|---|---|
| Map | WASD / arrows move (Shift walks), Space / Enter talk or use, **F** attack (the fight starts with your first strike, or you break a crate), **E** Technique of the leader (costs 1 Technique Point), **1–4** change the leader, **M** navigation map, Esc / Tab open the menu |
| Dialogue | Enter / Space / Esc show the whole line, then the next one; **Tab** (or the «Пропустить» plate above the dialogue box) opens the scene recap and skips the scene |
| Battle | **Q** picks Basic ATK (+1 SP), **E** picks the Skill (−1 SP), **Space** or **Enter** casts the picked action (pressing Q or E again, or clicking the target again, also casts it; Esc drops a picked Skill back to Basic ATK), ←/→ change the enemy target or, for an ally skill, the ally, **1–4** Ultimate of that slot when its ring glows (it plays a short cutscene; Enter, Esc or a click skips it), **T** Contact, **R** Fusion, **V** auto battle, **X** or the «»1» button top right switches the battle speed ×1 → ×2 → ×3 (saved as Settings → «Скорость боя»), ←/→ target, Shift doubles the speed, Esc pauses or retreats |
| Menus | Arrows, Enter, Esc; Q/E switch characters or inventory sections; the mouse works everywhere |
| Information | Enter moves the focus to the text, ←→ switch the pages «Досье / История / Голос», ↑↓ scroll by two rows, PageUp/PageDown by a screen, Q/E switch characters, Esc returns to the section list; the mouse wheel and the scroll bar work without the focus |
| Traces | Enter moves the focus onto the Traces tree, the arrows walk between nodes, Enter upgrades the selected node (a node that spends an Echo of War material or Tracks of Destiny asks first), Esc leaves the tree; a click selects, a second click upgrades |
| Omni-Synthesizer | Q/E switch synthesis and exchange, the arrows pick a recipe, A/D change the amount, Tab picks another material to give in an exchange, Enter crafts |
| Eidolons | Enter moves the focus to the constellation, the arrows pick an Eidolon, **V** (or «Смотреть картину») opens the picture full screen, Q/E there switch characters; any key or click skips the assembly of new pieces |
| Phone | Arrows move over the tiles; → from the right column (or ← from the left one) moves to the side panel with Mail, Settings, Save, Load, Controls and «В титры», ↑/↓ inside it |
| Synthesis | Q/E tab, arrows choose, A/D or Home/End quantity, Enter synthesize, F filter; exchange: Tab picks the material, R auto-add; relics: Enter set → slots → crafting window, R resin for Remains |
| Interastral Guide | Q/E section, I rules, arrows choose, Enter claim / enter / open; Daily Training F claim all; Survival Index C change target, G Traces, V relics; Forgotten Hall B shop, F star rewards |
| Supply | Tab cycles «Магазин», «Магазин обликов» and «Питомцы», Q/E switch the currency tab, arrows pick a lot, Enter opens the purchase window (←→ ±1, ↑↓ ±10, Enter buy, Esc cancel); in the outfit shop ↑/↓ pick an outfit, Enter buys or puts it on, F (or the «Все наряды» button) opens a grid of every outfit: arrows move, Enter puts the picked one on the showcase, Esc or F closes |
| Pets | In Supply → «Питомцы» Enter buys the pet, then takes it along or leaves it on the Express, ←→ pick a pet when there are several. In the field Space on the tile in front of the pet pets it or wakes it up |
| Outfits | In the character section «Наряды» Enter moves the focus to the costume card, ←→ switch outfits (the figure tries them on), Enter puts the outfit on or opens the Supply showcase when it is not owned, Q/E switch characters, Esc returns |
| Events | Q/E event, arrows reward, Enter claim, F claim everything open, Esc back |
| Mail | ↑/↓ letter, Enter claim the attachment, F claim all, Del delete read letters (asks first), Esc back |
| Messages | In the contact list ↑/↓ contact, Enter or → open the chat. In the chat Enter shows the next message at once, ↑/↓ pick an answer (or scroll when there is none), Enter sends it, PgUp/PgDn scroll the history, ← or Tab back to the list, Esc close |
| Warp | Q/E banner tab (event, Signal Search, Convene, Guild, standard), Tab channel, ↑/↓ featured hero, ←/→ ×1 or ×10, Enter warp, H history (Tab there switches the banner); while the Warp plays Enter goes on, Esc or the corner button skips to the results |
| Navigation map | ↑/↓ area, ←/→ next marker (left to right), Enter marker card and its action (Teleport, Show area), Q/E or −/+ zoom, Tab Star Rail map, M or Esc close |
| Debug | **F7** grants 1600 Stellar Jade (`TUNING.debugJade`) anywhere after the title screen, including open menus and battles |
| Debug menu | **F8** opens or closes the grant menu (`hsr-ui-debug.js`) anywhere after the title screen, also in battle. Tabs (Q/E or 1–5): Characters (level, Eidolon, «Выдать всех»), Light Cones (superimposition S1–S5, copies, «Все конусы»), Relics (set, slot, main stat, up to four distinct substats, level +0…+15, minimum/average/maximum rolls), Items (every item plus Stellar Jade and credits in preset amounts). ↑↓ picks a field, ←→ changes it (Shift for bigger steps), Enter grants. The lists on the left have a scroll bar (arrows, track click pages, the thumb drags) and scroll with the mouse wheel without changing the selection; moving the selection with the keys scrolls it into view. Each tab and each scene mode keeps its own scroll position (`debugState.views`). The bar is `ui.scrollBar`, switched on by the `scroll` option of `ui.list` (`{ top, sel }` object); the wheel goes through `ui.wheelZone`, dragging through `ui.drag`. Game side: `debugChar`, `debugCone`, `debugRelic`, values from `relicMain`/`relicSub` shared with `rollRelic`. Tab 5 «Сцены» is a viewer that never touches the save: the first row picks the mode (ultimate cutscene, Fusion cutscene, Warp intro, Eidolon picture), the list and the second row pick the character or the Fusion, a looping silent preview sits above the fields. Ultimates: targets as in battle, all three foes, one foe or none; «Смотреть» plays the real `H.Ults.Show` with sound, «Все подряд» plays all 21 in turn (Enter/Esc skip one, Backspace stops, ←→ jump). Fusions: the same targets row and buttons, the list shows both partners and whether the Fusion is already in the Archive; watching one does not add it there. Warp: the full Warp (train cinematic, then the intro) or only the intro, event or standard ticket colour, result tag new / Eidolon +1 / credits; it builds a `WarpShow` from a fake result, so no jade, pity or history changes. Eidolon pictures: stages E0 (black and white), E1 (colour), E3 (decor moves), E6 (scene and hero live); «Смотреть» opens the full-screen picture (←→ stage, Q/E character), «Сборка» and Enter play the assembly from the previous stage (from E0 it builds the whole picture to E6). A debug screen pauses the screens under it and the battle, so a cinematic below does not play on. Shots: `debugult`, `debugfus`, `debugfusview`, `debugwarp`, `debugeid`, `debugeidview`, `debugeidasm`, `debugultview`, `debugcard` |

## Modules (`js/apps/hsr98/`)

| File | Contents |
|---|---|
| `hsr-core` | RNG, elements, Paths, tuning table (`TUNING`), level growth and EXP curves |
| `hsr-data` | 21 character kits (Trailblazer in two Paths, March 7th, Dan Heng, Himeko, Welt, Natasha, Serval, Asta, Arlan, Herta, Gepard, Bronya, Seele, Sampo, Hook, Pela, the event 5★ Ruan Mei and the standard 5★ Bailu, Yanqing and Clara), 12 Fusions, Contact profiles, 27 enemies and bosses, items, Light Cones, relic sets, shops, the Warp pool, the two banners (`BANNERS`), the event rewards (`COMET_GIFT`, `TRAIL_WILL`), the letters (`MAIL`) and speakers |
| `hsr-battle` | Pure battle engine without DOM: timeline, damage and break formulas, DoTs, shields, taunt, talents, follow-ups, enemy AI with charge and lock-on patterns, summons, waves, Contact, Fusion, auto battle. It emits an event list that the UI plays back |
| `hsr-inaba` | Persona 4 collab kits and battle verbs (`H.INABA`): the six Investigation Team kits (`KITS`, merged into `CHARS` with temporary looks), five Fusions (`FUSIONS`, merged into `H.FUSIONS`), three signature Light Cones (`CONES`, merged into `H.CONES`), Yu's Personas (`PERSONAS`), Contact tag names for Rise's scan, and the `Battle` mixin: «Ещё раз!», All-Out Attack (`aoaReady`, `allOut`), Persona switch (`setPersona`, `autoPersona`), stitches (`stitch`, `stitchPartner`, `stitchEcho`), analysis and enemy intent (`analyze`, `planNext`, `intentOf`, `riseWarn`), Yosuke's follow-up (`yosukeStrike`), Yukiko's Burn healing (`burnHeal`), the Techniques and fx kinds `freeSkill`, `stitch`, `stitchAll`, `analyze`, `wave`, `execute`, `allOut`. Loads right after `hsr-battle` everywhere |
| `hsr-world` | Tile legend (`X` furniture block, `:` checker floor, `;` runner), 37 story maps (the Express is three cars: `express`, `express_cabin`, `express_party`, listed in `EXPRESS_AREAS`; the Herta station has eight: `hs_control`, `hs_office`, `hs_base`, `hs_storage`, `hs_storage2`, `hs_supply`, `hs_seclusion`, `hs_incubator`; Jarilo-VI has 25, see «Jarilo-VI maps» below) plus 6 Simulated Universe domain maps, story encounters, rumors, Calyx domains, destinations, worlds with their area groups and area descriptions (`WORLDS`, `EXPRESS_AREA`) |
| `hsr-story` | Script DSL with the whole Russian script. A line is `'speaker\|text'`; `'speaker=Имя\|text'` shows another name (an empty speaker, as in `'=???\|…'`, shows the name plate without a bust); `{он\|она}` in text and choice labels picks the form for the chosen Trailblazer and `{name}` inserts the name. Steps: `#label`, `{jump}`, `{if, jump}`, `{flag}`, `{battle}`, `{goto}`, `{choice}`, `{menu, exit}` (a question list whose asked items disappear until only the exit is left), `{cg}` (backdrop), `{cine}` (cinematic), `{ui}`, `{join}`, `{give}`, `{path}`, `{sfx}`, `{music}`, `{tut}`, `{obj, at}`, `{save}`, `{credits}`, `{recap, text}` (the start of a scene: its title and a short summary for the skip window and the Archive), `{take}` (removes items or credits), `{guest: [keys], solo|add, lvl}` and `{guest: null}` (a temporary team: guests get temporary roster entries, the real team waits in `state.guest` and comes back unchanged), `{quest, step}` and `{quest, tick}` (repeats the current step with its counter), `{lift: [map, mark]}` (rides the Space Elevator to a zone), `{rate: true}` (the Rating Pistol: the player picks a team member, the score lines from `Game.rateLines` are spliced into the running script), `{choice, title}` (a titled answer window), `{day: key}` (stores today in `state.days[key]`) and `{day: key, jump}` (jumps when a stored day exists and today is another day), `{party: id, jump}` (jumps when the character is in the team), `{rnd: [labels]}` (jumps to a random label); `{party}` also takes a list, and `lead: true` checks only the team leader. Repulsion bridges: marks `{type: 'rbridge', color: 'orange'|'blue', tile: '%'}`; orange burns while `hs_bridge` is set and `rb_blue` is not, blue while `rb_blue` is set; consoles flip `rb_blue`. Unearthly Marvel: marks `{type: 'marvel', group, tile: '%'}` light up when stepped on (`state.marvel`); when a group is lit the flag `mv_<map>_<group>` is set and marks `{type: 'span', group, tile: '%'}` stop blocking and draw a light bridge. A `prop` mark with `doc: '<id>'` opens the document window for `H.DOCS[id]` and sets `doc_<id>`; an `exit` mark with `lift: [[map, mark, need, lockText], ...]` is a Space Elevator that asks for the zone (closed routes show their lock text) and counts for quest routing. An `npc` mark with `wander: n` walks at random within n tiles of its spot (Peppy). Conditions (`if`, `need`, `open`, mark `if`/`always`) read flags and also mission steps: `?id` is an active mission, `?id=step` a mission at that step |
| `hsr-game` | Game state and rules: map loading, tile movement, roaming enemies, first strike and ambush, chests, crates, anchors, the script runner, rewards, EXP, relic rolls, auto-equip, Warp with pity per banner (`warp(n, ban)`, `pityOf`, `defaultBanner`), events (`cometTick`, `cometOpen`, `claimComet`, `claimWill`, `claimEvent`, `eventReady`), mail (`mailbox`, `readMail`, `claimMail`, `claimAllMail`, `deleteReadMail`), `alerts()` for the red badges, shops, rumors, Calyx, travel and saves. The clock is `Date.now()` unless a test passes `clock` to the constructor. Navigation data: `H.mapLayout(id)` parses any map without loading it, `navPoints(id)` lists anchors, landing points, exits, shops, rumor sellers, terminals and the objective marker, `objTarget()` reads the `at` of the current objective, `routeExit(from, to)` finds the exit toward another area, `teleportFor(point)` and `chestCount(id)` |
| `hsr-su-data` | Simulated Universe data in `H.SU`: the seven Paths of version 1.0 (`PATHS`: Aeon, colour, Path buff, Resonance text), 56 blessings (`BLESSINGS`, 8 per Path: three 1★, three 2★, two 3★, each with base and enhanced text and an effect list), 23 curios (`CURIOS`, seven of them sealing waxes, two of them bad clocks), the domain types (`DOMAINS`), the four worlds (`WORLDS`: level, ornament level, set, domain plan, enemy pools, boss waves, unlock condition, `atk`/`bossAtk`/`bossHp` multipliers), the six domain maps (`su_combat_a`, `su_combat_b`, `su_elite`, `su_event`, `su_respite`, `su_boss`, flagged `su: true`) and the tuning table `TUN` |
| `hsr-su-battle` | The battle side of a run: `H.SU.compile(run)` turns the Path, blessings and curios into stat buffs, enemy debuffs and event hooks; the `Battle` mixin applies them (`suStart`, `suFoe`, `bless(on, ctx)` with a re-entry guard, Grit, Critical Boost, Dissociation, Dew, Aftertaste, the once-per-battle survive) and plays the Path Resonance (`resonate`, gauge `su.res`, +5 per unit turn, 100 to fire) |
| `hsr-su-story` | Event domains (`EVENTS`: 14 events of kinds `event`, `trade` and `encounter`, each with a frame art key and a script), all run scripts (`SCRIPTS`: Herta's puppet, the tutorial lines, seven Aeon meetings with lines from the official 1.0 Russian text, the event scripts) and the Aeon meeting per world (`WORLDS[].aeons`) |
| `hsr-su` | The run itself as a `Game` mixin: `suStart`, domain generation (`suEnter`: layout, enemy groups, up to three portals), encounters with the world multipliers, the end of a battle (fragments, curio effects, Aeon meetings, the blessing or curio choice queue), offers (`suOffer`, `suCurioOffer`), Herta's shop (`suShop`, `suShopBuy`), enhancement, the script ops `{ su: … }` and conditions `{ suIf }`, rewards (`suComplete`, waiting claims, `suClaim`), failure and exit back to the office |
| `hsr-gfx` | Battle backdrops (sky, horizon and a perspective floor made from the map theme's floor texture) and element icons. `drawCG` hands dialogue backdrops to `H.Cine.still` |
| `hsr-tiles` | Map art: 12 themes (with `office` and `sim`) of 16×16 tiles drawn at 2×, autotiled walls (top and face), contact shadows, props, one prerendered layer per map with animated cells (water, lamps, consoles, crystals, windows), pixel map objects (chests, Space Anchors, crates, levers, barriers, exit arrows) and floor textures for battles, and the run objects: portals coloured by the next domain, event frames, healing capsules and Technique orbs |
| `hsr-foes` | Enemy art for all 28 enemies (battle sprite, field symbol and portrait icon from one drawing), idle frames, auras and family colours |
| `hsr-art` | Character pixel art from one look spec per character: field sprites (18×26, drawn at 2×), busts (any size, 64 for dialogue at 2×) and full-body figures (48×96, drawn at 2–3×; `figureCanvas(look, 2)` paints a 96×192 figure for the Warp reveal) |
| `hsr-icons` | Item, currency and relic icons, stat and menu glyphs, Path and element emblems (see «Path and element emblems»), map marker glyphs (`mapIcon`: anchor on/off, landing point, exit, locked exit, shop, warp, Simulator, navigation console, rumors, objective, player arrow, chest, padlock), rarity cells; the Remembrance and Elation glyphs, 23 curio icons (`curioIcon`, waxes in their Path colour), 14 event frame pictures (`suEventIcon`), the Cosmic Fragment icon (`frag`) and the quest marker (`quest`) |
| `hsr-relic-art` | Relic piece art (`G.RELIC_PIECE[set][slot]`): the four pieces of each of the 11 Cavern sets and the sphere and rope of the 3 Planar sets, drawn after the original pieces (hairpin, ring, alloy leg, grappling hook and so on) with per-set palettes (`PB`, `MK`, `KN`, `TH`, `FS`, `GL`, `BD`, `EG`, `GN`, `WL`, `CH`, `PL`) and helpers for boots, feathers, orbs and curves. Loads right after `hsr-icons`; a set without art falls back to the old slot silhouette in `SET_COL` |
| `hsr-cones` | Light Cone card art (scenes of 38 cones here, the rest in the scene modules of their versions) and the card frame |
| `hsr-midi` | Standard MIDI file player: `H.Midi.parse` (format 0/1, running status, tempo map, `loopStart`/`loopEnd` markers), `H.Midi.load` (fetch and cache) and `H.Midi.Player`, a small General MIDI synth on WebAudio (piano, string ensembles, solo and low strings, horns, a brighter brass section, electric, synth and sub bass, saw lead, distortion guitar with a channel waveshaper, voice, a synth drum whose pitch falls an octave, reverse cymbal, drum kit with kick, bass drum boom, snare, hats, six toms and cymbals), with CC 7/10/11/74/91, a convolution reverb and a 1.2 s lookahead scheduler that plays the intro once and then repeats the loop |
| `hsr-audio` | WebAudio chiptune sequencer (10 tracks, `sim` plays in Simulated Universe domains), the MIDI tracks (`menu`, `battle`, `boss`, `order`, `svarog`, `doomsday`, `gepard`, `cocolia`; each falls back to a chiptune if its file does not load) and sound effects, including the Warp cues (`ticket`, `whoosh`, `rumble`, `brake`, `steam`, `glint`) and the intro cues `shutter` (March 7th) and `strum` (Serval) |
| `hsr-ui` | 640×480 logical canvas core (render presets scale the buffer): window, plate, bar, rail, keycap and list primitives, title, field HUD, dialogue with busts, choice, hints, name choice, anchor, memory card saves, settings, credits |
| `hsr-train` | Art of the Express cars (`H.Train`), used by `hsr-tiles` for maps with `car`: its own floors (dark stone, marble aisle, carpet, checker, teal runner, carpeted stairs), 2-tile wall faces per car (navy and gold in the Общий вагон, wood fans over navy in the Спальный вагон, plum with a lit strip in the Вагон для вечеринок), window panes with a parallax view of the parked world (`map.outside`: the Herta station or Jarilo-VI), furniture as y-sorted sprites from the `decor` list (`DECOR`: kind, footprint, height, painter, lights), wall and floor decor painted into the layer (`LAYER`), light glows and hanging lanterns (`drawFx`). Also the guest book and photo wall screens (`UI.openBook`). Loads after `hsr-ui` |
| `hsr-station` | Art of the Herta Space Station (`H.Station`), used by `hsr-tiles` for maps with `zone`: palettes of the six zones (`ZONES`: control, base, storage, supply, seclusion, office), floors (panels, lit glass, accent walkway, grating, carpet, stairs, doorways), 2-tile wall faces with light strips, windows and `~` voids that show the panorama `blue` (stars and the Blue planet) or a lit shaft (`def.void: 'shaft'`), glass railings along voids, furniture sprites from `decor` (`DECOR`: planters, consoles, terminals, capsules, curio cases, the Storage Zone lift column, the spire of the Master Control Zone, the docked Express, vats, cryopods, note stands, the trolley, the neon arches of Herta's Office), painted decor (`LAYER`: station emblem, hex decals, screen walls, lift pads, signs). A decor entry may carry a condition as its fifth field (the Express appears at the platform after the Doomsday Beast). The layer has the same contract as `H.Train.buildLayer` (`deco`, `glass`, `lights`, `sprites`), so `H.Train.drawWindows/drawSprite/drawFx` draw it. Also the document window (`UI.openDoc`: paper, book or terminal look, scrolling with the arrows and the wheel). Loads after `hsr-train` |
| `hsr-jarilo` | Art of Jarilo-VI (`H.Jarilo`), used by `hsr-tiles` for maps with `land` (`city`, `plains`, `backwater`, `cavern`, `rivet`, `settle`, `corridor`, `hill`, `pathspace`, `pillars` and the interiors `hotel`, `workshop`, `fort`, `florist`, `clinic`, `club`, `grandhotel`, `museum`, `cable`): palettes per land (`LANDS`), slate roofs with snow caps and eaves, stone or brick facades up to `storeys` rows (windows run down a whole column when one facade tile of it is a window), rock cliffs with snow caps, cobbles, sidewalks, plaza paving, tram and mine rails, stairs, frozen river ice. Prop tiles (trees, lamps with Belobog banners, crates, crystals, fences) become y-sorted sprites; `DECOR` adds mailboxes, trash cans, benches, Geomarrow heaters, cars, the Ding-Ding Tram, Czech hedgehogs, sandbags, Fragmentum shards and the Everwinter Monument, `LAYER` paints signs, banners, posters, bracket lamps and manholes. Same layer contract as `hsr-station` |
| `hsr-magflow` | The Magflow Link puzzle (`H.Magflow`, `UI.openMagflow`): a flat-top hex board, `PUZZLES[id]` lists the board size, the power node, the end circuits, the solution paths and the fixed modules (red frame); masks are built from the paths, the other modules get random pipes, every non-fixed module starts turned. Arrows pick a module, Enter/E or a left click turns it clockwise, Q or a right click back. Script step `{ magflow: id, fail: label }` waits for the puzzle; solving sets the flag `id`, leaving jumps to `fail` or ends the script. Prop `obj: 'magflow'` (with `magflow: id` to light it when solved) draws the controller |
| `hsr-jarilo-story` | All 22 Jarilo-VI missions of the chapter and the 1.4 continuation «Рынок будущего», rebuilt by the plan in `HSR-JARILO.md` and merged into `H.SCRIPTS`. Save migration for the rebuilt maps lives in `hsr-game.js` (`JARILO_STEPS`, `state.jv`). Loads after `hsr-seclusion` |
| `hsr-jarilo-life` | Life of the Jarilo-VI areas: investigations, small NPC scripts, shops (Lynx) and the readables of the original in `H.DOCS` (Vache's lab records and letter) |
| `hsr-museum` | The History and Culture Museum: the 82 exhibit investigations with the texts of the original, the projector topics, the portrait captions in `H.DOCS`, Eris, Uldan, Boris and the booth attendant |
| `hsr-boulder` | Life of Boulder Town and its interiors: residents (Gertie, Fidora and her robot, Antonia, Tanya, the cable car operator, Elaine, Turner and his food stall, Maxime, Alina, Joshua, Scott, the Mysterious Kid, the Treasure Chest Magician), dumpsters and mailboxes with the jokes of the original, the eating championship, Scott's code box, the clinic books, the Cheap Room rating card, and the readables in `H.DOCS` |
| `hsr-carts` | The mine cart test of the Great Mine: nine track puzzles over three real days (`PUZZLES`, `day`), the rules (carts roll until a stop, a blocker, an ore pile that loads or unloads, a turnout set the wrong way or another cart; three-way junctions are turnouts), a breadth-first solver used by the tests and the `openCarts` screen (cursor, Enter picks a cart or flips a turnout, arrows push, R resets). Script step `{carts: id, fail}` works like `{magflow}` |
| `hsr-rivet` | Rivet Town life: the speakers Eric, Perkins (a `beetlebot` look) and the weak voice, the window on the south plaza and the anxious woman («Из другого мира»: the Rivet Town Warp Trotter drops the ribbon through the enemy `loot` field, the window gives the music box, the woman fades into the Fragmentum), the two Magflow caches, doors, barricades and the Rivet Town readables (`rv_*`, the Musicians of Belobog IV–VI). The mission «Прошлое накроет снежной лавиной» lives in `hsr-jarilo-story.js` (`riv_*`) |
| `hsr-settle` | Robot Settlement life: the speakers Eunice and the scout robots (`beetlebot` look), Eunice's rap, Eric looking for Bronya, the east Magflow cache and the settlement readables (`st_*`). The mission «Гнить или гореть» lives in `hsr-jarilo-story.js` (`rot_*`): certification quiz robots, two gates, Perkins in the Rivet Town storage, Blaze Out of Space at the Tempering Workshop, the lift, the energy supply device, Svarog in two rounds (`svarog1` ends at 70%, `svarog2` starts the boss at 80% with the encounter fields `hpStart` and `allyBuff`), the memory archive and Natasha |
| `hsr-restricted` | Restricted Zone life: the speaker Dunn, his talk after the story, the «Moral Higher Ground» barrel, two Magflow caches and the zone readables (`rz_*`). The missions «Очистка внешней тьмы», «Не сотвори кумира», «Молодая гвардия» and «Отставить разговоры в строю» live in `hsr-jarilo-story.js` (`ret_*`, `rz_*`) |
| `hsr-corridor` | Corridor of Fading Echoes extras: two Magflow caches (`cor_mf*`) and the corridor readables (`cor_*`: two unsent letters, the Crystal Daily sample article, the ancient Belobog poem). The mission «Звёзды - это замёрзшие игрушки» lives in `hsr-jarilo-story.js` (`cor_*`) |
| `hsr-hill` | Everwinter Hill readable «Сирил: Сирил Недоучка» (`hill_cyrille`). The finale missions «А зори здесь...», «Возвращение», «Опасное болото», «Время не ждёт, мой друг» and «Тишина галактики» live in `hsr-jarilo-story.js` (`dawn_*`, `ret_*`, `swamp_*`, `time_*`, `gal_*`) |
| `hsr-pillars` | Pillars of Creation life: Fersman, Thea, Antonia, Benjamin and Pela with the project scraps, the Engine of Creation team (three design questions), the frozen railing, the frozen trash can, the barrels and other inspections (`pl_*`), the readables `pl_ipc_manual`, `pl_tabloid`, `pl_proposal`, and Bronya's chat invitation to the Sun Festival (`fm_invite`). The 1.4 continuation «Рынок будущего» (Предисловие, I, II) lives in `hsr-jarilo-story.js` (`fm_*`) |
| `hsr-3d` | Software 3D rasterizer `H.R3` for the Warp cinematic, the cinematics and the title scene: a 320×240 frame buffer drawn at 2×, z-buffer, near-plane clipping, flat and Gouraud lighting with point lights, affine texture mapping, integer vertex snapping, additive and alpha blending, glow sprites, streaks, light shafts and a 15-bit ordered dither on output. Mesh builders: quad, box, cylinder, ellipsoid (with a planar face texture), loft, grid, merge |
| `hsr-pompom` | Pom-Pom's low-poly 3D model with a simple rig and a painted face texture, used only by the Warp cinematic: `H.PomPom.draw(R, pose)` puts it into a scene. The dialogue bust, the full figure and the field sprite are hand-drawn 2D art in `hsr-art` (`pomArt`, `spritePom`) |
| `hsr-warp` | The Warp: `H.WarpShow` plays the cinematic, the item reveals (Light Cones in 3D, characters through `H.WarpIntro`) and the results rack; low-poly models of the pass, the ticket console, the Astral Express (locomotive and cars), the platform and door, and Pom-Pom with a simple rig |
| `hsr-title` | The title screen in real-time 3D (`H.TitleScene`, drawn by `UI.drawTitle` under the logo and commands): the Warp's Express models run on a curved Star Rail (gold rails, violet light ribbon, glowing sleepers and stardust that stream back at the train's speed) past a rotating Jarilo-VI (procedural 512×256 ice, sea and cloud textures on two UV spheres, lit limb), tumbling asteroids, a small Herta Space Station, stars and nebulae that parallax with a slow camera sway, and a comet every 13 s. The camera, planet and station are placed in screen space so the train stays clear of the logo and the command column. About 4 ms per frame in Edge; the textures are built once on the first frame (~150 ms) |
| `hsr-cine` | Cinematics and dialogue backdrops. Scenes are painted into a 320×240 buffer, dithered to 15-bit colour and shown at 2× with letterbox bars and subtitles. The kit has painted locations (`BG`: space with the Herta station or a planet, station rooms, the Express parlor, snow plains and Belobog's walls, Belobog streets by day, night and sunset, Qlipoth Fort, Goethe Hotel, the Fragmentum alley, Underworld town, clinic and hotel, the Great Mine, Rivet Town, the robot settlement, Neverwinter workshop, the front line, Everwinter Hill and its summit, Path Space with Nanook's eye or Qlipoth's amber, storybook panels), effects (glows, snow, embers, smoke, sparks, glitch, shards, hex shield, Stellaron, Express side view, drones, capsules, the Engine of Creation) and actors from the character, bust, sprite and enemy art (`S.fig`, `S.bust`, `S.sprite`, `S.foe`, look variants such as closed eyes or an open mouth). `closeup()` builds a talking-head shot, `STILLS` are the dialogue backdrops, `Player` plays a cinematic (Enter next shot, Esc skip) |
| `hsr-cines` | The 29 cinematic shot lists (`c_attack` … `c_leave`): each shot has a duration, a draw function, subtitles `[time, 'speaker\|text']` and sound cues. The Express shots reuse the Warp's 3D models through `H.WarpKit` |
| `hsr-warp-intro` | Warp intros (`H.WarpIntro`): the Path sign of every Path (`PATH_FX`: background, the way the emblem leaves, the flash colour and the sound), the intro scene of every character the Warp can give (`SHOWS[id]`: duration `d`, sound cues, the card `line`, `act(S, P)`), the card pose (`CARD`), `render` (sign or scene into the 320×240 buffer, dithered), `overlay` (letterbox, Path name), `line`, `cues`, `length`. Uses the cinematic kit of `hsr-cine` and the props of `hsr-ults` (`H.Ults.Art`) |
| `hsr-signal` | Signal Search scene `H.SignalShow` (same interface as `H.WarpShow`: `tick`, `draw`, `render`, `enter`, `skip`, `done`): shots `power` (a CRT in a stack turns on), `dial` (a 3D rotary dial on its screen), `glitch` (static and the best rank letter), `pyramid` (the TV pyramid lights one screen per result: S gold, A purple, B blue); then per item a hop to its TV, a turntable stage for Light Cones («КОНУС» and the rank letter) or a spotlight stage for agents (silhouette, flash, 3D words «АГЕНТ S» and «КОНТРАКТ ЗАКЛЮЧЁН», for rank S a close-up with the bust and the Eidolon picture on a TV); results grid «ИТОГИ ПОИСКА». Meshes are cached by unit and screen texture. Loads after `hsr-warp-intro` |
| `hsr-signal-tv` | Live TV channel on the Signal Search close-up (`H.SignalTV`): per character 3 to 5 programmes from their lore (`SHOWS[id]` = `key`, `ch` OSD channel or `''`, `len`, `light` room tint, `fx` {noise, ghost, chroma, wobble, track, hum}, `paint(g, t, X)` on 320×240 with `X.look`), downscaled to one 160×120 texture updated in place, CRT pass (scanlines, vignette, antenna ghost, chroma shift, VHS tracking band and tear, hum bar), power-on line, channel switch = static plus vertical roll, OSD drawn after the CRT pass. `closeScene` uses it when `has(id)`, else the Eidolon picture. Miyabi: Hollow news (06), fan club VHS (AV), video rental ad (21), letter to her mother (no OSD, cleanest signal). Yanagi: «Знакомьтесь» interview with the unlaughed joke (03), her calming art film «Чайник» on a sepia tape (AV), overtime on the office camera with Soukaku stealing a bean bun (12), the Mother's Day desk in carnations (no OSD). Welt: «Арахато» anime shown by mimicry, worst picture (08), March's home video of a tiny black hole in the parlor car (AV), his pencil line test of the Express rescuing the drifting ship (24), the album with a sketch of his home world (no OSD). Himeko: black coffee ad with Dan Heng's «Неплохо.» (05), educational channel lecture «Динамика межзвёздной навигации» with a chalk gravity assist (02), baggage x-ray of her suitcase with the saw and the runaway satellite moving (11), the night shore with meteors «один... два... три...» and the wrecked train (no OSD). Bronya: Sun festival speech «Я хочу вернуть празднику его исконный смысл» (01), Silvermane bulletin map with the squad marker 10 → 9 after the Fragmentum ambush (03), Underworld tape «СМЕНА 14 ЧАСОВ» with miners (AV), young Bronya on the stone bench while the steel disc fades into a clear sky and a butterfly comes (no OSD). Gepard: victory parade, Cocolia gives him the glowing bracers under fireworks (04), Serval live concert with Gepard humming in a picture-in-picture (07), kids show «УРОК ДЛЯ ЮНЫХ СТРАЖЕЙ» «Больше заниматься спортом и правильно питаться» (09), his wilted window flowers, one blooms, note «Полей! — С.» (no OSD). Clara: Hook's «КРОТЫ» news, hide-and-seek, Svarog under a big coat (06), family photo countdown with Perkins, Timmy and uncle Toby «СЕМЬЯ!» (AV), Svarog's red log view of the scrapyard «ПЛАЧ: 3 Ч 07 МИН» (13), Svarog in the red coat and hat holding sleeping Clara (no OSD; Svarog via `H.Ults.Art.svarog`). Yanqing: star fleet review with orbiting flying swords (15), Artisanship Commission teleshop «КУПИ ДВА!» with Остроцвет, Тальник, Кроволист and a wallet running to zero (22), amber Cloud Knights archive dossier «РОДИТЕЛИ: НЕИЗВЕСТНО», «ПОРУЧИТЕЛЬ: ГЕНЕРАЛ ЦЗИН ЮАНЬ» (31), his room with swords in pairs and the general's note «На еду.» (no OSD). Bailu: «ПРИЁМ У БАЙЛУ» with prescription cards «Пейте больше воды», «Высыпайтесь» (10), teletext page P128 with Jing Yuan's case history and a food prescription (TXT), «САД ГУРМАНОВ» ad for the stretching «небесная тянучка» while she climbs the wall and mentors grab her (27), Bailu under water with a dragon swimming in the deep (no OSD). Every 5★ that Signal Search can give has a channel. 4★ agents get 3 programmes and the same close-up (`SignalShow.closeAt`: 5★ at 2.4 s, 4★ with a channel at 1.6 s, others none; bust and card line from `H.WarpIntro.line`). March 7 joined the Signal Search 4★ pool through `BANNERS.sig.extra4` (the Star Rail Warp pool is unchanged): Pom-Pom fashion show «МОДА ЭКСПРЕССА» with a new hat per planet (17), her tape «КТО Я?» with versions 12, 41, 67 (AV), a polaroid of the crew developing «не забыть!» (no OSD). Kazuma (Signal cameo 4★): Axel news «УРОЖАЙ КАПУСТЫ УЛЕТАЕТ!» with winged cabbages and his net (13), rock-paper-scissors show where he beats Aqua every round, «Нечестно!» (22), his old room in Japan with the sealed first-edition online game box and the green tracksuit on the chair (no OSD). Sampo: «КРИСТАЛЬНЫЕ ВЕДОМОСТИ» live from Fountain Square, Brügel interviews a fuming «Синее мошенничество» victim «Этот синеволосый гад!..» while Sampo waves behind the fountain (05), Silvermane wanted notice cycling his disguises, «синие волосы (или парик)», «У вас дома есть парики?» (01), an Underworld candle table with bread split into seven days and medicine «для Н.» (no OSD). Arlan: station show «НАЙДИ ПЕППИ», a magnifier finds her by the tail, «ПОБЕДА АРЛАНА: 9 ИЗ 9» (19), security quad split where camera 4 catches him throwing the electronic frisbee to Peppy at 02:47 (04), the boy's hand reaching to the sick puppy on a blanket in Asta's house until she lifts her head and licks it (no OSD). Dan Heng: Express database screen linking Ярило-VI, «Герта», Стелларон, Лофу and Экспресс (16), spaceport split-flap board «ЛОФУ — ОТМЕНЁН», «КУДА УГОДНО — ПОСАДКА» while he walks through the crowd (28), dawn in the archive, he sleeps on the floor and Pom-Pom peeks in (no OSD). Asta: «СВЕТСКАЯ ХРОНИКА» with three relatives correcting her one after another «Нельзя так!», «Скажи иначе!», «Нет, вот так!» (07), telescope «Ньюл Иман» locking onto a protostellar cloud with a camera inset of her watching (33), young Asta on the mansion balcony with her telescope and a shooting star, the party lit behind her (no OSD). Herta: Genius Society No. 83 interview, «Что побудило вас создать этот оазис?» — «Мне стало некуда складывать вещи» (83), six puppets answering six meetings at once «Нет, спасибо.», «Не-а.», «Скучно.» (9), «Глава 2» of her manuscript at night, a line written and crossed out (no OSD). Serval: «НЕЙВЕРВИНТЕР» repair ad, heater, cash register and recorder get fixed one by one, «Свободна в любое время!» (14), night rehearsal tape of «Механическая горячка» with Pela on keys and the drums under her note «НЕ ТРОГАТЬ!» (AV), her workbench at night: a toolbox powers up into Gepard's shield emitter next to an old photo from the Silvermane days (no OSD). Natasha: medical graduation «С ОТЛИЧИЕМ», «Любая больница возьмёт тебя» — «Я хочу вернуться в Подземье» (18), clinic-nursery tape where she stitches her teddy bear for the kids (AV), the night ward with sleeping children and her letter «Дорогие мама и папа! ... Я нужна людям.» (no OSD). Hook: kids show «ХУК ВЕЛИКИЙ МРАК» on top of the retired mining drill «Разящий железный кулак!» (42), her diary page «Погода: холодень.» where «приборок» and «проборок» get crossed out until «пробирок» (AV), a salamander roasting on stones like her mother loved, a drawing of the once-a-year surface flower on the rock (no OSD). Pela: recruitment tape «НАБОР В РАЗВЕДКУ» where she cites «Глава V, пункт 23, статья 1, параграф 7...» until the two guards doze off (20), the invitation-only «Приключения в Снежной стране» fan meeting where she hides her face behind a book (AV), the night library with snow outside, a stack of the series and her phone in a cat-ear case (no OSD). Every 4★ that Signal Search can give has a channel too. Painters get `X.lookOf(id)` for other characters. Loads after `hsr-signal` |
| `hsr-convene` | Convene scene `H.ConveneShow` (same interface as `H.SignalShow`: `tick`, `draw`, `render`, `enter`, `skip`, `done`): shots `tide` (a night sea with a 3D whirlpool, low hills against a misty horizon, the Radiant or Forging Tide falls in and leaves a flat ripple), `dive` (splash blades and shards, white flash), `comet` (a white comet streaks to the vanishing point and turns blue, purple or gold at `SHIFT` by the best rarity), `vortex` (a dark cloud bloom, then cloud rings in that colour fly at the camera, the comet enters, a ring burst and a four-point star) and `flash`. Per item a ripple hop, then Light Cones pop out of a water bubble and float tilted over a nebula with light ribbons; characters get a waveform with one star per rarity (5★ adds a sonar ring with a rainbow halo), then a dark shore with the figure, its reflection and a light ribbon, or their own cut (`CUTS`: Phrolova 7 shots after the reference video, Scar 6 invented shots); the results are a row of tall cards. 3D parts use `H.R3`, 2D layers use `H.Cine.Kit` on the same 320×240 buffer and `H.Cine.dither` runs last. Exports `CONVENE_SHOTS`, `CONVENE_CINE`, `CONVENE_CUTS`, `ConveneKit` |
| `hsr-explosion` | Guild scene `H.ExplosionShow` (same interface as `H.ConveneShow`): shots `hill` (an evening hill near Axel in `H.R3`: heightfield terrain, the town on the horizon, the abandoned castle on the far hill; Megumin as a backlit silhouette with her staff, Kazuma on a rock; the manatite flies into the staff head), `chant` (three crimson magic circles turn in the sky over the castle, her eye glows), `beam` (one pillar of light in the colour of the best rarity), `boom` (fireball layers, a mushroom cloud, a ground shockwave that bends the grass, a second wave and a rainbow ring at 5★) and `score` (Kazuma holds up his score card: 40 to 69 at 3★, 70 to 94 at 4★, 100 at 5★, Megumin lies face down at his feet). Per item a crimson ring hop, then Light Cones are pulled out of the smoke by Kazuma's Steal, other characters get the Adventurer's Card on the guild counter and a pose on the hill, and the four KonoSuba heroes play their own cuts (`CUTS`). Each own cut is a small story in the shape of the banner rule, a boast, the flaw, the cover by the party, then the pose: Kazuma's Adventurer's Card with a Luck bar that runs off the card, a giant toad with Aqua's legs kicking out of its mouth, «Кража!», Megumin's panties in his hand while the scared toad spits Aqua out; Aqua rising on the Axis fountain, purifying the lake from a cage, alligators chewing the bars (hit-stop, tears), Kazuma dragging the cage ashore while she refuses to leave it; Megumin's name with the crimson rune, the chant with the camera tilting up the stack of circles, the Explosion on the horizon, the fall, Kazuma carrying her piggyback with the score card 90 that turns into 100; Darkness charging the flying cabbages, three missed swings, cabbages pelting her (hit-stop, shake, hearts), the golden shield over Kazuma, Aqua and Megumin. Poses push in slowly over the hero's own place (guild board with falling coins, lake with a water rainbow, hill with the smoke column, Axel gate with the Dustiness banner). Every beat has a subtitle line (`LINES`, speaker and text, no dashes) and its own sounds (`BEAT_SFX`, times inside the beat). A duplicate (Eidolon or credits) plays the short cut that starts at `BRIEF` (flaw, cover, pose); The eight standard 4★ of the pool have their own guild cuts too (4 beats: Adventurer's Card from Luna via the shared `advCard` with a class, an overflowing bar and an ink stamp, the flaw, the cover by Kazuma's party, the pose on their own HSR place with a new quote): Asta (Credits, treats the hall, Aqua's bar tab, Kazuma steals and tears it; control room with a constellation), Arlan (Wounds, toad tongue and the low-HP lightning slash, Darkness jealous while Aqua heals, «Само заживёт»; platform with Peppy), Herta (a puppet signs up, Intelligence, «И это считается сильным?» after Megumin's Explosion, two puppets kuru-kuru freeze a toad over the fallen Megumin; hall with puppets), Serval (bard, Rock and roll, a concert on a guild table, Megumin's Explosion finale blows the roof; Neverwinter with a «ЗАКРЫТО» sign), Natasha (priestess?, Medicine, Aqua's prayer heals +1, Natasha's shot gets Kazuma up; Underworld with a stitched teddy), Sampo (merchant, Connections, sells Aqua her own Axis holy water, Kazuma's Steal gets an empty wallet while Sampo swings Kazuma's purse; Belobog with a wanted poster), Hook (class «Великий Мрак», Pathos, split-screen title duel with Megumin, mole hats for the party; mines with the drill robot), Pela (scout, Analysis, a report on the four flaws of Kazuma's party, her scan freezes a toad so Darkness finally hits; Silvermane fort with «Приключения в Снежной стране»). The seven standard 5★ that the Guild can give have 5-beat cuts in the same shape: Himeko (Caffeine, the suitcase cannon roasts toads, her black coffee knocks Kazuma out, Aqua purifies it to water; parlor car), Welt (Experience, a black hole pulls the toads in, his back cracks next to the fallen Megumin, Aqua heals «дедушка»; space with the Express), Bronya (Duty, a formal speech to sleeping adventurers, «леди Дастинесс Форд Лалатина», her order makes the party combo work; snow with Silvermane banners), Gepard (Defense, his ice shield against cabbages while Darkness is jealous, his flower wilts, Aqua's «Красота природы» makes it bloom; the front), Clara (Family, Svarog's fist flattens a toad, Kazuma's Steal on Svarog raises the threat index, Clara calls Kazuma family and it drops; Rivet Town with Svarog), Yanqing (Swords, flying ice swords slice toads, Megumin names swords «Чунчунмару», a spar with Darkness who never falls; Luofu pagodas), Bailu (Age, her water dragon heals the party, Aqua offers the «little girl» candy, Darkness lifts her to the quest board; a jade-green Luofu). The Trailblazer has two different guild cuts, picked by `key(r)` from `r.gender` or the save gender (`lookFor(r)` draws the matching look, the card shows the save name only for the save gender): Stelle (`stelle`, chaos: Chaos bar, head first in an Axel trash can and out with Kazuma's wallet, a bat home run sends a toad into orbit, Kazuma pulls her and Aqua out of two trash cans; quote «Урны проверены, можно в путь!») and Caelus (`caelus`, deadpan: Stellaron bar, one bored bat swing, Aqua's «Очищение!» only soaks him, Kazuma is glad to meet a normal man until «…Я, кстати, тоже проверяю урны.»; quote «Нормальный отряд? Звучит скучно.», the counter to Kazuma's lie for the future story). F8 «Набор в отряд: появление» has a «Первопроходец» row (Стелла / Келус) for `trail`; the shots tool takes `ksv_stelle_*` and `ksv_caelus_*`. Other banners are unchanged; All times in the module are design times played slower so the lines can be read: the Explosion film and Light Cone reveals 1.5× (`SLOW`), character scenes 1.75× (`CHAR_SLOW`, `slowOf(r)`); shot and beat functions get design time, `length`/`revealCues`/`EXPLOSION_CINE`/`cutLen` are real time; the results are notes pinned on the guild quest board. Exports `EXPLOSION_SHOTS`, `EXPLOSION_CINE`, `EXPLOSION_CUTS`, `ExplosionKit` (`QUOTES`, `LINES`, `BRIEF`, `score`, `height`, `cutLen`) |
| `hsr-midnight` | `H.MidnightShow`, the Midnight Channel pull scene (same interface as the other shows: `tick`, `draw`, `render`, `begin`, `enter`, `skip`, phases cine/reveal/rack). Film (`SHOTS`, `MIDNIGHT_CINE` 7.4 s): `clock` (Express car at night, rain, the LED clock turns 23:59 → 00:00, the Velvet Key glows), `tv` (3D room, the broadcast CRT lights up with yellow static and the silhouette of the best result, gold eyes on a 5★, the static takes the rarity colour), `touch` (a hand presses the screen, ripples, push-in), `fall` (3D fall through yellow fog past spinning tarot cards onto the red and black target floor), `velvet` (3D velvet car with fog windows, Igor's silhouette behind the round table, one face-down card per pull, the best card rises). Reveal: the card flips (arcana numeral, bust or cone art, arcana name), a character card bursts into blue flame and the hero stands in a spotlight with falling cards (a gold ring on 5★); a cone stays on its card. Spread «РАСКЛАД» with tarot cards. Music: `SONGS.aria` (`content/music/AriaOfTheSoul.mp3`, the file holds the piece twice, the repeat lag is 169.102 s, loop 12 → 181.102). Loads after `hsr-explosion` |
| `hsr-personas` | Persona art drawn as vectors in a 100-unit box (feet at 0, head at −100): `H.Personas.draw(g, id, x, y, h, { a, flip, tint, swing, eye, t })`. `ART` holds the six base Personas (Изанаги, Джирайя, Томоэ, Конохана Сакуя, Такэ-Микадзути, Химико); `AWAKE` holds the six awakened ones (Изанаги-но-Оками, Сусаноо, Судзука Гонгэн, Аматэрасу, Рокутэн Мао, Кандзэон), each drawn as its base Persona recoloured through a 240×240 layer (`paint` colour and strength, `source-atop`) over its own back symbol (halo, ring blade, fan blades, sun, flame crown, lotus). `awakeOf(owner)`, `baseOf(owner)`. Shot `personas` shows all twelve. Loads after `hsr-explosion`, before `hsr-midnight` |
| `hsr-fusions` | Fusion cutscenes: `H.Fusions.SHOWS` (one entry per Fusion id: two `lines` as `[member index, text]`, `swap` time of the second line, `act` draw function and its duration `d`, sound cues, `team` effect when the Fusion helps the team) and `H.Fusions.Show`, a subclass of `H.Ults.Show` with its own beats (split cut-in, action, hit in both elements, team effect). Reuses `H.Ults.Stage`, `BURST`, `TEAM_FX`, `TEAM_TINT` and `Art`. Loads after `hsr-ults` |
| `hsr-ults` | Ultimate cutscenes: `H.Ults.SHOWS` (one entry per character: `line`, bust `side` and look variant `v`, `act` draw function and its duration `d`, sound cues, `team` effect for support Ultimates, `impact: 'pull'` for Welt), `H.Ults.Show` (the player), shared beats `cutIn`/`hitBeat`/`teamBeat`, element bursts `BURST` and team effects `TEAM_FX`, `MODES` for Settings. Uses the cinematic kit of `hsr-cine` (320×240 buffer, dither, letterbox) |
| `hsr-outfit-cuts` | Cutscenes of 5★ outfits (`H.OUTFIT_CUTS[outfitId] = { ult, skill, fusions, warp }`, same entry shape as `H.Ults.SHOWS`, `H.Fusions.SHOWS`, `H.WarpIntro.SHOWS`) and their art (`H.OutfitCutArt`: greatsword `blade`, `eclipse`, red `sky`, `rails`, `slash`, `flameWave`). `UltShow` takes the worn outfit's `ult` (`H.wornOutfit`, `H.outfitCutOf` in hsr-shop) or `o.outfit` from F8 → «Сцены» (row «Облик»). So far: «Алый урок» Ultimate (eclipse over the rails, the greatsword opens like a wing, a fire cross, the downward cut and a flame wave; line «Наш путь здесь не закончится.»). Skill cutscenes: `H.SkillShow` (one beat of `skill.d` + 0.2 s fade, top-left «НАВЫК» and the Skill name, Esc skips; the usual hit animation follows), started in `hsr-ui-battle` for `act` events of kind `skill` when the worn outfit has a `skill` cut (`skinCineOn`, `bt.skinSeen`), option «Сцены навыков облика» (`options.skinCine`: on / first / off, default first). «Алый урок» Skill: dash with a fire trail, a small cross cut on the middle target, fire to the neighbours, the sword planted in the ground (1.2 s). F8 → «Сцены» → «Навык облика». Fusions: `FusionShow` takes `fusions[id]` of the first member whose worn outfit has one (`o.outfit` forces it for the member it belongs to, row «Облик» in F8 «Слияние»). «Алый урок» in «Сингулярность Экспресса»: Welt's singularity covers the sun and makes the eclipse, Himeko leaps into its edge and draws a fire corona round it, the corona falls as a fire and imaginary pillar; lines «Этот доспех… Я однажды уже видел его.» / «Тогда повторим урок. Теперь вместе.» (act 1.9 s). Frames: `OUTFIT=himeko_vermilion node tools/hsr98-shots.cjs <out> fus_f_singularity_1_1.2`. Warps: `H.WarpIntro.showOf(r, game)` picks the worn outfit's `warp` (or `r.outfit`, F8 «Прыжок: появление» row «Облик»); `length(id, game, outfit)`, `cues(r, game)`, `overlay(ui, c, r, t, game)` and `line(id, game, outfit)` follow it, so other banners that quote `WarpIntro.line` get the outfit line too. On the crossover banners the outfit's `warp.finale(g, t, T, look, game)` replaces the last beat of the hero's own scenario: the guild pose (`ExplosionShow.outfitFinale`) and the last Con-Ton/Alley shot (also the single shot of a repeat); Signal Search keeps its close-up with the worn bust and the outfit line. «Алый урок» Warp (4.2 s): the Helheim hall with rows of empty grey suits, the red suit in the middle, the pass flies into the gem and lights it; close-up with coffee and the pass; the eclipse finale with the sword opening like a wing; line «Алый урок начинается. Конспект писать не нужно.». Frames: `OUTFIT=himeko_vermilion node tools/hsr98-shots.cjs <out> warpv_himeko_5_4.1` (act starts at 2.9 s). Frames: `OUTFIT=himeko_vermilion node tools/hsr98-shots.cjs <out> skin_himeko_0.56`. Loads after `hsr-fives-scenes`. Plan: `HSR-OUTFITS.md` |
| `hsr-traces-data` | `H.TRACES[kitId]` for all 62 kits: material lines `mats` (Path, foe line, Echo of War material), cost class 4 or 5, three bonus abilities (name, text, link), ten stat nodes (stat, value, level gate, link), six Eidolons (name, text), the ability levels Eidolons III and V add (`up`), `shadow` for the Trailblazers, `own: true` for kits not imported from the original, `eidFx: true` once a kit uses its real Eidolons. Written by `tools/hsr-traces-import.cjs` from StarRailRes, then edited by hand |
| `hsr-traces` | Trace materials in `H.ITEMS` (`kind: 'mat'`), cost tables, gates and caps, `H.traceNodes/traceCost/traceAbilityCap/traceScaleAb`, Game methods (state, upgrade, `autoTraces`, migration and back pay, drops, calyx and Echo rewards, Echo limit, synthesis and exchange, Trailblazer Shadows), letter `v16_traces`, Battle methods (`abilityFor` scaling, `traceFoe`, `hasBonus`, `eidOn`). Loads right after `hsr-game` |
| `hsr-echoes` | Echo of War foes and mechanics: Phantylia the Undying with the Abundance and Destruction Lotuses, Skaracabaz (Synthetic) in two phases with Multiply; the two Echoes in `H.CALYX` |
| `hsr-ui-traces` | UI methods: the Traces section, Shadow activation, the one-time back pay notice |
| `hsr-synth` | Synthesis materials, recipes and recipe items, `cook`, Relic Remains, salvage, `craftRelic`, resin for Remains, synthesis drops; wraps `give`, `buy`, `shopList`, `sellRelic`, `traceBattleLoot`, `traceCalyx`. Loads after `hsr-traces` |
| `hsr-ui-synth` | The Synthesis screen (four tabs), the exchange picker, the relic crafting and result windows, mass salvage. Loads after `hsr-ui-traces` |
| `hsr-guide-data` | Recommended builds copied from the «Ковчег» site (`GUIDE_BUILD_OF`, `guideBuild`) |
| `hsr-guide` | Interastral Guide data and Game methods: sections, acts, Survival Index rows and routes (`guideSite`, `guideHop`), development target, Daily Training (hourly) with its counters, alerts. Loads after `hsr-conductor` |
| `hsr-ui-guide` | The Interastral Guide screen `openIpg` and its sections. Loads after `hsr-ui-synth` |
| `hsr-ui-menu` | Pause "phone" hub with its side panel (close, Mail, Settings, Save, Load, Controls, «В титры») and its screens: characters (details, Traces, relics, abilities, Eidolons, information, outfits; the Light Cone is changed from the details), team setup, inventory with the materials tab, Warp with the event and standard banners (starts `WarpShow`), warp history per banner, shops, rumors, Simulator, Archive, controls |
| `hsr-ui-velvet` | The Midnight Channel banner screen: `tarotCard`, `midnightBanner` (rainy Inaba night, fog that rises with the pity, the Inaba forecast calendar «дней до тумана», Teddie's glasses for the guarantee, the hero or cone on a big tarot card, the Канал/Компендиум switch, three tarot cards as the picker), `velvetContract` and `signVelvet`. Loads after `hsr-ui-menu` |
| `hsr-ui-inaba` | Battle UI of the Investigation Team: `inabaEvent` (events `oneMore`, `aoa`, `aoaEnd`, `persona`, `stitch`, `intent`, `warn`, `scan`), `inabaUpdate`, the «ЕЩЁ РАЗ!» cut-in (`drawOneMore`: diagonal yellow band with the bust), the All-Out Attack prompt (`askAllOut`, sub `aoa`, `aoaOptions`) and dust cloud (`drawAoa`: puffs, stars and the team's portraits over the foes, «ФИНИШ!» when it wins), the stitch thread between two foes (`drawStitch`), enemy intent tags from Rise's analysis (`intentTag`), Yu's Persona list (`drawPersonaModes`, ↑/↓ at any pick, locked after he acts), card tags (`inabaTags`: Persona, «Ещё раз!», «Подхват», «Стежок N», «Навык 0»). Loads after `hsr-ui-battle` |
| `hsr-inaba-scenes` | Ultimate cutscenes (`ULTS`: Мириады истин with Izanagi-no-Okami's light fan, Гарудайн tornado, Галактический пинок that sends a foe to the stars, Пламя Коноханы fire wave (team `heal`), Первобытная сила ground slam, Третий глаз scan rings and reticles (team `buff`)), Fusion cutscenes (`FUS`: Напарники lightning and wind into a dust cloud, Подруги детства ice spikes and flames (team `heal`), Император на связи scan, thread and bolt, Я есть ты two Fool cards and Izanagi for both Trailblazer Paths) and the six Eidolon pictures (`EID`: Туман над Инабой, Джунс на закате, Говядина в дождь, Гостиница «Амаги», Ткани Тацуми, Рисетт на паузе; the base Persona stands left of the hero, at E6 the awakened Persona replaces it and animates). | `hsr-fours-scenes` | Their Ultimate cutscenes, Fusion cutscenes and Eidolon pictures plus shared props in `H.FoursScenes` (snow night, tent, gauntlet, ropes, phoenix, chain, fan, jade tile, kite, fire hoop, firework, brush, stamp, coffin, counter, red lantern) used by the Warp intros and TV channels. | `hsr-eidolons` | Eidolon pictures: the jigsaw grid (10×8 pieces of 32×30 picture pixels; six of them are the Eidolon pieces under the stars of the constellation), the stage view (`view`: black and white, colour, decor, life), the assembly of new pieces (`Assembly`, `syncAsm`, `tickAsm`, `skipAsm`), the breathing and blinking figure and the auras at Eidolon VI (`figure`, `aura`) and the full-screen picture (`UI.openEidolonView`) |
| `hsr-eidolon-scenes` | The 20 Eidolon pictures in `H.Eidolons.SCENES`: title, aura, six fragments (name and line), `paint` (the 320×240 picture; with `live` set it leaves out the actors that `life` animates), `decor` (from Eidolon III) and `life` (Eidolon VI) |
| `hsr-info` | Character files for the «Информация» section (`H.INFO[rosterKey]`): `full`, `aka`, `faction`, `home`, `about`, `intro` (questions with a crew member's note: `q`, `a`, `by`, `note`), `story` (up to six parts), `extra` (more dossier blocks `{ h, text, need }`, used for WuWa archive records and valuables), `voice` and `battle` lines (`t`, `text`; an item with only `h` is a subheading, used for the Trailblazer's Paths). In story text a paragraph starting with `#` is a cyan question, `«` a quote, `~` a verse line. Any entry can carry `need` (`lvl`, `eid`, `char` in the roster, story `flag`); `H.infoLock(game, key, need)` returns the lock text or null. Texts go through `game.fill`, so `{name}` and `{он|она}` work. Loads after `hsr-section6` |
| `hsr-express` | Game side of the Astral Express: investigation scripts of the three cars (`exp_*`), the IPC broadcast, the phonograph with `RECORDS` (a record changes the music of all three cars through `Game.mapMusic`), `GUESTS` and `PHOTOS` for the guest book and Март's photo wall (`Game.bookPages`), and the Party car visitors (`Game.visitors`: two roster characters outside the crew and the active team, a new pair every day at 04:00, each says one of their own `H.INFO` voice lines). Loads after `hsr-info` |
| `hsr-herta` | The prologue on the Herta station in the order of the original: «Хаос глубин» played by Kafka and Silver Wolf in the Base Zone (`kafka_*`, terminals, the Blind Spot curio, the vault), the awakening in the Gallery of Shadow, the broken Atrium lift and Arlan in the control center, the lift to the Master Control Zone, Asta, Himeko as a guest, Hinkel and Abraham, the Supply Zone (power, repulsion bridge), the Doomsday Beast on the platform, Herta in her office and boarding the Express from the platform. Also the Genius Society portraits with Herta's recorded voice and the office script of Herta. Loads after `hsr-express` |

Loads after `hsr-express` |
