| `hsr-axel` | Belzerg art (`H.Axel`): the grounds `meadow`, `axtown`, `guild`, `wizshop`, `stable`, `estate`, `mansion`, `forest`, `keele` as a kit for `H.Jarilo.buildLayer` (grass, paths, water, cliffs, Axel's stone wall, half-timbered fronts, roofs by building, interiors), decor and layer props, enemy art (toads, alligators, goblins, Vanir dolls, turret, spider, Destroyer Prototype) |
| `hsr-axel-story` | The event story «Обмен отрядами»: speakers, Contact family, enemies and encounters `ks_*`, quests `ks_q1`–`ks_q7` and the interludes, all scripts from the signal to the farewell, chat threads (Pom-Pom, Herta, Kazuma after the story), event milestones, the job board, the Adventurer's Card skills, and the `Game` mixins: level floor 40 in Belzerg, no Teleport out of Belzerg during the swap, report statistics from real battles, carrying Megumin |
| `hsr-ui-axel` | Event screens: the «KonoSuba x HSR 98» Events tab (milestones, early access, «Смотреть титры» after the story), the new event notice, the job board (`openUi_ksboard`), the Explosion rating (`ksrate`), the Adventurer's Card (`kscard`, `kslearn`), Herta's reports (`ksreport`), battle overlays for the cage, the Prototype phases and the carried Megumin, the cine backdrop `meadow` and the cines `c_ks_land`, `c_ks_blast` |
| `hsr-credits` | Clip credits (`openClipCredits`, `H.CREDIT_CLIPS`): the story step `{ credits: 'konosuba' }` plays the YouVid clip `k0n0SuBa3Ed` (P3D, 640×360 between black bands) to the song `konosubaEd`, timed from the song position, with one caption card per cut; without WebGL the meadow backdrop plays under the same captions; two presses skip |
| `hsr-conton` | Кон-Тон (Cosplay Convention Crisis, version 1.4): kits of Макото Майн, Аманда Миллер and Таллея из Авии with their alternate looks, four Fusions, six cones, the effect list `FX` and the battle mixin (turn steal, portal, transformation, Heart Cocoon, Shackles, gynoids, capsule, applause). `register()` runs at load. Loads after `hsr-fours` |
| `hsr-conton-info` | Their «Информация» pages, merged into `H.INFO`. Loads after `hsr-fours-info` |
| `hsr-badge` | Banners `con` («Главная сцена») and `alley` («Аллея художников»), the badge item, the letter `v14_conton`, registration (`conRegister`), the event «Четыре дня Кон-Тона» (`conDays`, `claimConDay`) and the guest book lines with the `%has_<id>` condition |
| `hsr-spotlight` | `H.SpotlightShow`, the Кон-Тон pull scene: film on four bars of the minus track, polaroid reveals with the jury card, the heroines' own cuts and Kazuma's cut (`CUTS`, `CUT_LINES`, `BRIEF`), the photo wall with the gorilla. The heroine theme plays over the muted minus (`H.Audio.theme`) |
| `hsr-conton-scenes` | Their Ultimate and Fusion cutscenes, Eidolon pictures and cone art, registered into `H.Ults.SHOWS`, `H.Fusions.SHOWS`, the Eidolon scenes and `CONE_SCENES`. Loads after `hsr-fours-scenes` |
| `hsr-ui-badge` | The Кон-Тон banner screens (stage and alley), the registration desk with Pom-Pom and the Events tab. Loads after `hsr-ui-axel` |
| `hsr-ui-conton` | Battle UI for the collab: events, portraits, foe tags, the gynoid squad (`G.drawEnemy({ id: 'gynoid' })`), the crowd and the capsule |
| `hsr-hares` | «Хитрые зайцы» (Signal Search A agents, version 1.4): kits of Билли Кид, Николь Демара and Энби Демара, three Fusions, three 4★ cones, the letter `v14_hares` and the battle mixin `hares*` (С колена stance, Star Knight charges, Energy Field with DEF down and «every foe is a neighbour», case shots, Series and Гром, the Chain Attack phase, squad link). `register()` runs at load. Loads after `hsr-conton` |
| `hsr-hares-info` | Their «Информация» pages, merged into `H.INFO`. Loads after `hsr-conton-info` |
| `hsr-hares-scenes` | Their Ultimate and Fusion cutscenes, Eidolon pictures, cone art and the close-up lines in `H.SignalKit.QUOTES`. Loads after `hsr-conton-scenes` |
| `hsr-ui-hares` | Battle UI for the hares: the Chain Attack panel (`chainOpen`, `chainKey`, `chainPick`), the Energy Field dome and DEF tag, card pips (stance, charges, case shots, Series) and the squad link mark. Loads after `hsr-ui-conton` |

The desktop wrapper is `js/apps/hsr98-app.js`, the styles are in `css/apps/hsr98.css`. Saves go to `localStorage` (`hsr98.save.1..3`, `hsr98.save.auto`; options in `hsr98.options`).

## Interface and art

**Direction.** The screens follow the structure of the modern game (pause "phone", character details on a platform, team line-up, action order, boss bar), but they are drawn like a 1998–2000 JRPG in the spirit of *Persona 2*: dark navy windows with a brass double frame and corner diamonds, gold serif title plates, a 2×2 dither on window fills, pixel-art characters and a large character bust above the dialogue box. The Star Rail itself is the motif: EXP and settings bars are rails with sleepers and a diamond marker, and the action order is a vertical rail of portraits.

**Screens.**
- *Title:* a real-time 3D scene from `H.TitleScene` (the Express on the Star Rail over Jarilo-VI); commands on the right, the chosen command and its context (disc, save summary) at the bottom.
- *Field HUD:* location and objective top left, party column top right (number keys 1–4), minimap bottom left (M opens the full map), Technique points bottom right. The HUD hides while a dialogue box is open. Each party card shows HP and the stored Ultimate energy as a bar with marks every 25% and a `current/max` number; energy carries over between battles, a full bar shifts colour like the battle ring, and energy gained from an item fills in with a pale flash after the menu closes.
- *Energy items:* Термос Среброгривых (+25% energy to one character, Магазин в Административном районе, a chest on the Snowplains), Искра геоядра (+60% to one character, Магазин Подземья, chests in the Great Mine and Everwinter Hill) and Сбитень на всю смену (+15% to every living member of the active four, Магазин Подземья, a chest in Rivet Town). They are used from the inventory outside battle, are refused for a fallen ally or a full bar, and the gain is stored in the roster, so it goes into the next fight. Their icons share a yellow bolt badge.
- *Minimap:* the area chart at 4 px per tile around the player, with anchors, exits, other markers and the objective. When the objective is outside the frame or in another area, a gold arrow on the edge points to it or to the exit that leads there. When the player comes within 40 px of the minimap in the bottom left corner, the minimap fades over to the right edge above the Technique panel (`updateMinimap`, docks in `minimapDocks()`); it returns once the player is more than 88 px away from the left dock, so it does not flicker at the edge. Each new map picks the dock at once, without a fade.
- *Phone hub (Esc):* leader figure on the left, device panel with UID, Equilibrium Level (bosses beaten), Trailblaze Level rail and 12 tiles, and a narrow side panel on the right: a close button, the Mail envelope, the Settings gear, Save, Load, Controls and the power button «В титры» (it asks before leaving to the title), with a strip of rail sleepers below them. Tiles 7 and 8 are «Путеводитель» and «Синтез». The last tile is «Сообщения». Warp and Events open after the Express introduction; the Navigation map opens anywhere, but Teleport and Save work only near a Space Anchor or in a safe map. A red «!» badge marks the Events tile, the Messages tile, the envelope and the «Esc меню» hint in the field HUD while there is something to claim, an unread letter or an unfinished chat.
- *Events notice:* when the Herta Space Station is done and the team is on the Express (flag `express_intro`), the field shows a one-time window «Открыты события» (flag `events_note`) with both events and the buttons «Открыть события» and «Позже». It waits for dialogues, scripts and battles to end, so old saves past that point see it once on load.
- *Events:* a list of events on the left (a ticket per event with its status and a badge) and the event page on the right. **Comet Gift** («Подарок кометы») is an hourly gift in rounds: the round starts when the events open, and every hour of real time opens the next card (1, 1, 2, 1, 1, 1 and 3 Special Passes, 10 in total over seven hours). Open cards do not burn. Five hours after the last card is claimed the round starts over with all seven cards closed again (`cometTick`, `cometOpen`, `cometOpensAt`, `cometRenewAt`; state `comet: { start, done, got }`, old day-based saves restart the round). Claimed cards get a red rubber stamp. The header shows the round rule (7 hours of gifts, 5 hours of rest), each closed card counts down to its hour, and the footer shows the open cards and the time to the next card or to the new round. **Trailblazer's Will** («Воля Первопроходца», a permanent newcomer event) puts four stations on a rail: Trailblaze Level 5 and 15 give 10 Star Rail Passes each, 25 and 35 give 10 passes and 800 Stellar Jade each. A second page (patch 1.6, `◄ ►` buttons, PgUp/PgDn, or ←→ past the edge) continues the rail to the level cap: 45 and 55 give 10 passes and 800 jade, 65 gives 10 passes and 1000 jade, 70 gives 10 passes and the Trailblazer outfit «Властелин Мусорного Царства». A player who already bought that outfit gets its price (840 jade) instead (`willGive`). The screen opens on the page with the first unclaimed station. The current level is a marker on the rail.
- *Mail (the envelope on the phone):* letters on the left (unread ones have a gold envelope and a badge, unclaimed gifts a jade icon), the open letter on the right with sender, date, a postmark, the text and the attachments. Version 1.1 sends two standard letters, compensation for the update maintenance and for the bug fixes, with 300 Stellar Jade each. «Получить все» claims every gift; «Удалить прочитанные» removes read letters without unclaimed gifts after a confirmation.
- *Messages (the last phone tile):* the contact list on the left (round portrait, name, signature, a badge while a thread is unfinished; the newest thread is on top) and the chat on the right. A chat is one column of bubbles: the contact on the left with a small portrait and the name over the first bubble of a run, the Trailblazer on the right in pale gold. Threads are separated by a date pill. Before each message of the contact a bubble with three jumping dots shows that they are typing; the pause grows with the length of the message and follows the text speed option, and Enter skips it. When the contact waits for an answer, the footer shows one to three answer buttons. Stickers are 68 px squares with the speaker's bust, a small effect (star, heart, question mark) and a caption; photos are Polaroid frames with a dialogue backdrop and the people in it. A quest from a message and the daily reward are pills in the middle of the chat. A new thread in the field slides in a notice at the top centre (portrait, «Новое сообщение» or the count, the first line); a click opens the chat.
- *Navigation map (M, the phone tile, Teleport at an anchor):* a pale navigator's chart in a brass frame, the one light screen in the game, so the map reads as paper and the rest of the interface stays navy. The chart is drawn from the map rows: floors are dark, walls next to them become a light rim, buildings are grey blocks with windows, chasms are hatched, rails keep their sleepers, consoles have a cyan line, closed barriers are red. Colour is kept for markers: blue anchors (grey until touched), gold landing points, brass exits (a red lock while closed), shops, the warp terminal, the Simulator, the navigation console, rumor sellers, the pulsing gold objective and the cyan player arrow with a view cone. The right panel lists the areas of the world (the Express first, then area groups such as Надмирье and Подземье), marks the current area with a cyan chevron and the objective area with a gold diamond; areas not opened by the story show as «???» with a padlock. Enter on a marker opens its card: the area description, anchor state, why a teleport is not possible, lock text, shop goods or bought rumors, and one action (Teleport, Show area, Star Rail). Counters at the top show opened chests and active anchors of the area. The zoom rail has six steps; when the chart is larger than the frame it pans to the chosen marker.
- *Star Rail map (Tab on the map, the navigation console on the Express):* deep space with orbit rings; the Herta Space Station, Jarilo-VI and a locked Xianzhou Luofu in brass rings with compass ticks. Travelled routes are rails with sleepers, the Express is parked at the world it serves, the current world has the cyan chevron and the objective world a gold diamond. The info box shows opened areas, active anchors and chests of the chosen world; Enter opens its area map.
- *Characters screen:* the roster strip at the top shows nine portraits at most. With a bigger roster it scrolls: Q/E (or a click on the Q/E keycaps) move the selection and the strip follows with a short eased slide, keeping one neighbour visible on the side of travel; the mouse wheel over the strip scrolls it without changing the character; the brass arrows beside it page by eight and show how many characters are hidden on that side. A roster of nine or fewer sits centred without arrows. Shots: `charstrip`, `charstripend`, `charstripfew`.
- *Close buttons:* full-screen menus put the cross in the top right corner of the screen; the windows Archive, Save, Load, Controls and Settings put it in the top right corner of their frame. It does the same as Esc.
- *Settings:* sound and music volume, battle and text speed on rails, and «Разрешение», a strip of render presets: 640 (the original 640×480 buffer), 800, 1024, 1280 and «Окно». The layout always stays 640×480 logical units; a preset only changes the canvas buffer, and `render()` scales the context to it, so text and vector shapes get sharper while pixel art keeps nearest-neighbour pixels. «Окно» sizes the buffer to the displayed canvas times `devicePixelRatio` (0.5× to 4×) and follows window resizes every frame. The CSS keeps the canvas at 4:3 with letterbox bars (`container-type: size` on `.hsr98`, width `min(100cqw, 133.333cqh)`); before, a narrow window stretched it.
- *Scene skip (Tab in a dialogue):* a window «Пропуск сцены» with the scene title and its recap, the note that answers stay with the player, and the buttons «Пропустить» (Enter or Tab) and «Читать дальше» (Esc). A skip runs the script without its lines and cinematics, but stops at every choice, menu, tutorial and `ui` step and still applies flags, joins, gifts and objectives; a battle, the next `{recap}` or the end of the script ends it. While it runs, a «Сцена пропускается» plate shows top right. Lines before the first recap of a script cannot be skipped, so one-line talks show no skip plate.
- *Archive, «Сюжет» tab:* every recap the player has reached, in order, with its full text. Saves made before the recaps existed rebuild the list from story flags.
- *Mission:* the objective card has an «На карте» button (M) that opens the map on the objective. The right column «Поручения» lists the side quests (name, giver, current step); Q or the quest's «На карте» opens the map with its target selected.
- *Side quests:* a step with a target (`at: 'map:mark'` in `H.QUESTS`) puts a violet quest diamond on the map chart, on the list row of its area and on the exit that leads there; the HUD shows the current step under the objective, and every step change shows a toast.
- *Simulated Universe terminal (the machine in Herta's Office after the test):* three steps on the simulation backdrop. «Мир»: the four worlds on the left (number ring, title, level, first-clear bonus or lock), the chosen world on the right (text, boss, rewards with the ×3 stamp on a first clear, set bonus) and the waiting rewards strip (C claims one for 40 power). «Путь»: the seven Paths on a ring; an open Path shows its Aeon, buff and Resonance, a closed one tells where its Aeon appears. «Команда»: the four party cards, «Изменить состав» opens the Team screen, «Начать погружение» starts the run.
- *Run field:* the HUD shows the world, the zone number, the domain type, a hint for the domain and a strip with fragments, blessings, curios and the Resonance count; the party cards show the run's HP. Cleared domains show portals labelled with the next domain type. Event domains have a gold frame with the event picture; while an event or an Aeon meeting runs, its picture or the Aeon's Path sign stands above the dialogue.
- *Run screens:* the blessing choice (three tall cards: Path, rarity, name, base or enhanced text, the note for the run's own Path; with the dimension dice two cards can be marked), the curio choice (three pedestals and a description), Herta's shop (her bust, the services with fragment prices, the team's HP strip), enhancement (owned blessings and the text before and after), the run status (M: the domain chain, blessings and curios tabs, team, fragments, «Завершить погружение») and the summary (the domains passed, counts, the rewards or the reason there are none).
- *Run battles:* the Resonance orb sits next to the Skill Points with F, the Path sign and the charge; firing it shows «РЕЗОНАНС ПУТИ». A won battle shows the fragments and the team's HP; a lost free run offers only «Завершить погружение», a lost tutorial battle offers only a retry.
- *Characters:* roster strip (Q/E), sections on the left, the figure on a lit platform, info panel on the right. Relic slots sit around the figure; the Traces section draws the Traces tree over the dimmed figure (see «Traces and Eidolons»); the Abilities section shows the level of every ability. The Eidolons section puts the character into their Eidolon picture (see below) with the six stars of the constellation on its pieces. A click on a star, or Enter and the arrows, selects an Eidolon; the panel shows the official Eidolon name and effect (with the flat +4% note while the kit is not reworked), the name of the picture fragment, the picture stage rail (Ч/Б, Цвет, Декор, Жизнь), what the selected Eidolon adds to the picture and the duplicates it still needs.
- *Team:* four figures on a snow-white floor with name plates; Enter swaps a slot from the roster, L makes a leader, Del removes.
- *Inventory:* five sections, icon grid on rarity cells, detail panel with actions.
- *Battle:* see the Modules table; Contact shows the acting character's bust next to the choice window.
- *Damage preview:* while the player picks an action, `Battle.preview(kind, target)` runs the same damage formula as `hit` (`dmgCalc`, no random rolls, no state change) for every enemy the action reaches. Each enemy shows the HP the hit will take as a pulsing white part of its HP bar (a paler part up to the crit ceiling), the toughness it will take as a blue part of the toughness bar (gold when the hit breaks it), and a label `normal~crit ◆` above the bar: red numbers mean the normal hit kills, orange crit numbers mean only a crit kills, a gold ◆ means the hit breaks the enemy (the break damage is included), a blue ◆ means toughness damage without a break. The boss shows the same on its top bar. Heals and shields show on the party cards (`+N` or «Щит N» and a pulsing part of the bar). The estimate leaves out random effects (DoT chances, follow-ups, random bounce hits; the cast plate says «+N отск.» for bounce skills) and the 10% bonus later hits get after the break.
- *Damage board:* the «Урон» panel at the top right lists the party by damage dealt to enemies in this battle (every ally hit, break and DoT as it plays), with the total and a bar for each ally.

**Banners.** The Warp terminal has three banners, switched with the tabs at the top (Q goes left, E goes right): the hero event banner, the Light Cone event banner and the standard banner. The event banner spends Special Star Rail Passes (or 160 jade) and lets the player choose its 5★ hero (`BANNERS.ev.stars`, state key `evStar`, default Ruan Mei): «Трилогия сливового цвета» with Ruan Mei (4★ March 7th, Herta, Asta) or «Бабочка на кончике меча» with Seele (4★ Hook, Natasha, Pela). Every 5★ from the event banner is the chosen hero (there is no 50/50), every 4★ is one of that hero's three. The choice is a column of two portrait cards at the right edge of the banner (click, or ↑/↓); it can change at any time, and the event pity (`pityEv`) is shared by both heroes. `Game.bannerOf('ev')` merges the chosen hero into the banner, event log entries carry `ban: 'ev'` and `star: <id>`. The standard banner «Путь Первопроходцев» spends Star Rail Passes; its 5★ characters are Himeko, Welt, Bronya, Gepard, Clara, Yanqing and Bailu (Seele moved to the event banner in 1.2; the new three are tagged «новинка» in the list). Each banner has its own pity counters (`pity`, `pityEv`), and the Warp opens on the banner whose passes the player holds. Ruan Mei's banner art is a round moon window with a lattice, a plum branch with falling petals and Ruan Mei at 3× scale; Seele's is a violet Underworld night with a scythe arc of light, house silhouettes with lit windows, pipes and fluttering butterflies; the standard banner shows its seven heroes in two staggered rows. The cinematic ticket is red and gold for the event banner and blue and silver for the standard one.

**Warp.** The Warp follows the structure of the modern game's summon and is drawn as a real-time low-poly scene, the way a 1999 PC or PlayStation game would show it (software rendering at 320×240, affine textures, vertex wobble, 15-bit dither). The cinematic has six shots and runs 12.7 s:

1. *Pass:* the Star Rail Pass hovers over the gold ticket console and slides into the glowing slot; cyan flash.
2. *Hyperspace:* speed streaks and a chromatic halo; the pass tumbles toward a bright star, the Astral Express comes out of it and grows until its headlamp fills the screen.
3. *Fly-by:* the Express rushes past the camera with sparks under the cars.
4. *Arrival:* a low shot on the platform as the car brakes to a stop, speed lines and sparks.
5. *Pom-Pom:* a knee-height tracking shot of Pom-Pom walking along the car, then turning to the camera.
6. *Door:* the rarity tell. The door frame and the light behind Pom-Pom take the colour of the best item: blue (3★), purple (4★), gold (5★). On 3★ and 4★ Pom-Pom throws the ears and arms wide in a burst of steam, the camera pushes into the hat lamp and pans to the door, which slides open with rays of light. On 5★ Pom-Pom crouches and leaps toward the camera, gold steam stays behind, and the empty door opens in gold.

Then every item is revealed in pull order. A Light Cone drops into a glass case with a dashed ring and rays of its rarity colour. A character gets a Warp intro: the sign of the character's Path, then a short scene of that character (see «Warp intros» below), then the card. A 5★ item first gets a prelude: light shafts, the dim Path emblem and five gold stars. A name plate (Path glyph, name, stars, Path and element) and a result tag (new character, Eidolon +1, +3 000 credits or a Light Cone added) appear with each reveal; a character's plate also shows the line the character says on joining. A ten-warp ends on a slanted results rack of ten plates in three rows (3, 4, 3) with names, New tags and a glow on 4★ and 5★ plates.

Controls: Enter (or numpad Enter) moves the Warp on: it skips the cinematic to the reveals, completes the current reveal (for a character it jumps to the card), moves to the next one and closes the results rack. Esc or the «К итогам» / «Пропустить» / «Закрыть» button in the top right corner skips to the results (unseen 5★ items are still revealed first) and closes the rack; a single warp closes after its reveal. Clicks elsewhere on the screen, Space and other keys do nothing while the Warp plays, so a stray press cannot skip a pull.

**Warp history.** H on the Warp banner, the History button next to it, or Enter on the Archive's Warps tab opens the log. Every pull stores its number, the item, its rarity, the result (new character, Eidolon +1, +3 000 credits, a new Light Cone or its superimposition), the pity count for 4★ and 5★ items and the date and time. Items from one ten-warp share a batch and a line separates batches. The header shows the total number of pulls, how many 5★ and 4★ items dropped, the last 5★ and two rails with the pulls left to each guarantee. Q/E filter by rarity (all, 4★ and above, only 5★), ←/→ turn pages of ten rows. The save keeps the newest 1000 pulls (`warpLog`, `warpN`, `warpBatch` in the state); text logs from older saves are converted when the save loads.

**Warp intros.** A pulled character is the payoff for the jade the player saved, and the same characters already appear in two other places: the Ultimate cutscene shows what a character can do in battle, the Eidolon picture shows their world piece by piece with every duplicate. The Warp intro is the first meeting: one gesture that says who this person is, in three or four seconds. Each intro has three parts:

1. *Path sign* (1.3 s, `WarpIntro.SIGN`). The screen goes dark, letterbox bars slide in and the emblem of the character's Path draws itself like a constellation: gold lines for 5★, violet for 4★, a star at every corner and a bright head on each stroke. Then the emblem fills with light and the Path name appears under it («ПУТЬ / РАЗРУШЕНИЕ»). The background and the way the emblem leaves belong to the Path: Destruction cracks the dark and bursts into shards, Preservation builds an amber wall that parts like a gate, the Hunt fires an arrow across the screen, Erudition turns its rings and opens like an iris, Harmony rings out in waves, Nihility pulls everything into a point and goes black, Abundance grows branches from the corners and blooms. The sign comes before anything about the character, so in a ten-warp the player can guess who is coming from the colour and the Path.
2. *Scene* (4.2 s for 5★, 3.2 s for 4★). Three beats: where the character is and what they are doing; the Star Rail Pass arriving (red and gold from the event banner, blue and silver from the standard one, the same pass as in the cinematic) and the character's answer to it; then a push into the final pose. The pass is the invitation the player paid for, and the way each character takes it is the point of the scene.
3. *Card*. The scene holds on the full figure (breathing, blinking, the scene keeps moving), the bars slide away, the name plate slides in with the character's line under it and the result tag appears. The figure always stands on the same spot (`WarpIntro.CARD`), so the plate and the tag never cover it.

| Character | Path | Scene | Line on the card |
|---|---|---|---|
| Himeko | Erudition | Night in the parlor car: coffee pours into a cup and the pass lands on the saucer; she looks up from the cup; by the window with two cups and her red case | «Кофе на двоих готов. Куда едем дальше?» |
| Welt | Nihility | The pass streaks through space and stops in a gravity well; his cane keeps it in orbit and his glasses glint; a black hole and floating rocks behind him | «Вельт Ян. Как самочувствие? Тогда в путь.» |
| Bronya | Harmony | She walks along the ranks of the Silvermane Guards in snowy Belobog; the wind brings the pass and she catches it; under the Belobog flag | «Броня Рэнд, Среброгривые стражи. Я с тобой.» |
| Gepard | Preservation | A silhouette on guard at the gate in a snowstorm; the pass hits his ice shield and freezes to it; ice spikes and the Silvermane banner | «Гепард Ландау, капитан стражи. Держись за моим щитом.» |
| Clara | Destruction | Little Clara at Svarog's feet in the robot settlement; the pass is out of her reach, Svarog's hand takes it and brings it down to her; his red eye behind her | «Господин Сварог разрешил. Я пойду с тобой!» |
| Yanqing | Hunt | A dash and three cuts through a training dummy on a Luofu terrace; the pass lands on the tip of his sword; a fan of flying swords | «Лейтенант Яньцин! А сильные противники в пути будут?» |
| Bailu | Abundance | She hops past the herb cabinet under red lanterns; the pass lands on her horn, she laughs and her tail knocks it away; the elixir gourd and a rain of light | «Доктор Байлу на месте! Поранишься, сразу ко мне.» |
| Ruan Mei | Harmony | Embroidery under the moon window, the pass drifts in with plum petals; she puts it into a glass dish as a sample and a plum blossom grows from it; rings of blossoms | «Жуань Мэй. Мне любопытно, куда ведёт твой путь.» |
| Seele | Hunt | The pass flutters among violet butterflies over the Underworld; a quantum glitch, a slash, the pass on the tip of her scythe | «Зеле. Не отставай, и поладим.» |
| March 7th | Preservation | Her camera viewfinder tracks the pass, the shutter clicks; the photo develops while she laughs; snapshots fall around her | «Я Март 7! Давай сфоткаемся на память?» |
| Herta | Erudition | Three puppets in glass cases, the middle one wakes up; the pass freezes into an ice cube; she spins and stops | «Ладно, малявка. Побуду с тобой, пока не надоест.» |
| Asta | Harmony | She draws constellations at the station console; the pass becomes the headlamp of a constellation shaped like the Express | «Я Аста! Покажу тебе созвездия, только попроси.» |
| Arlan | Destruction | An alarm in the storage zone, his silhouette with the polearm; lightning catches the pass and the red light turns calm blue | «Если что, встану впереди. Это моя работа.» |
| Serval | Erudition | A spotlight on the workshop stage; the pass becomes her guitar pick, one strum and the amplifiers fire | «Медиатор что надо. Ну что, зажжём?» |
| Natasha | Abundance | Late light in the clinic: the pass lands on her clipboard and she writes a note; green crosses and the Wildfire banner | «Раны оставь мне. Только не геройствуй зря.» |
| Sampo | Nihility | The pass flies to his stall and vanishes in a puff of smoke; it turns up by his ear and a gift box bursts into confetti | «Сампо Коски, к вашим услугам! Слухи, карты, консервы.» |
| Hook | Destruction | The pass lands on a barrel that starts to shake; Hook bursts out of it holding the pass up; the Moles flag and sparks | «Хук нашла сокровище! Теперь ты тоже из «Кротов»!» |
| Pela | Nihility | The tactical screen locks onto the pass; it slides into a folder and gets an approval stamp; her glasses glint | «Досье на тебя уже готово. Пела, разведка Среброгривых.» |

The lines are short (58 characters at most, no dashes) and keep the voices of the story script: Herta's «малявка», Hook speaking of herself by name, Clara's «господин Сварог», Welt's «Как самочувствие?». What the scenes show comes from the story and the Eidolon fragments (Himeko treats everyone to coffee, Serval's pick, Sampo sells maps and tins, Hook's hide-and-seek in barrels, Ruan Mei always takes a sample), not from new lore.

The scenes paint into the cinematic kit's 320×240 buffer (`H.Cine.stage`, dithered) and use the bust for close-ups (closed eyes, open mouth or blush), the field sprite for wide shots, the 48×96 figure for silhouettes and actions and the 96×192 figure for the card pose; the props of the Ultimate cutscenes (`H.Ults.Art`: sword, butterfly, flower, crystal, bolt, gift box, Svarog) are shared. `render(game, r, t, std)` returns the buffer for the intro time `t` (0 at the start of the sign), so `WarpShow` only adds the 5★ prelude before it, the plate and the tag after it, and plays `cues(r)` (the sign's `warp`, `glint`, `star4`/`star5` and the Path's own sound, then the scene's cues). Frames take 1.2–3.4 ms on average in Edge; the first frame after a cut can take 10–60 ms while a bust of a new size is built.

To add a character to the Warp, add an entry to `H.WarpIntro.SHOWS` (the test fails for any character from `WARP_POOL` or the event banners without one), end the last beat on `pose(S, P)`, keep a 5★ scene at 3.8–4.6 s and a 4★ scene at 2.8–3.6 s, and use only sound ids that exist in `hsr-audio`.

Shots: `warpv_<id>_<rar>_<sec>` renders any moment of a reveal. For a 4★ character the sign runs 0–1.3 s, the scene 1.3–4.5 s and the card follows; a 5★ character adds the 1.6 s prelude (sign 1.6–2.9 s, scene 2.9–7.1 s).

**Ruan Mei.** A 5★ Harmony character with Ice, only from the event banner. Skill «Повторяющаяся мелодия пения струн» gives the team Overtone for 3 turns (+32% DMG and 50% faster toughness damage, `breakEff`). Ultimate «Лепестки на воде, отдых во сне» raises the team RES PEN by 25% for 2 turns and marks every enemy with Rebloom: when a marked enemy would recover from a break, it stays broken, takes Ice break damage and acts a little later. Talent «Соматотипическая спираль»: the other allies get +10% SPD, and every break by an ally adds Ruan Mei's Ice break damage. Technique «Шёлковая серенада» starts the next battle with Overtone. Her look follows the official art: very dark hair with a side bun and a gold pin, teal eyes, a teal qipao with gold trim and a dark front panel, long teal side panels with bare legs between them, cream puff sleeves, long dark teal gloves, a dark mandarin collar with a red stone, pearls and white plum blossoms at the waist (look keys `bun`, `mandarin`, `pearls`, `puff`, `longgloves`, `blossom`, `low: 'slit'` with `apron`).

**Light Cones overhaul (22.10.2026, `HSR-CONES.md`).** Every cone follows one data contract: `ref` (the original id) or `own: true` (cones invented for guests), level-1 `stats`, passive name `skill`, template `tpl` with `#n`/`#n%`, the S1–S5 table `p`, `bonus` mapping stats to `p` columns, `fx` and the story `desc`. `H.coneInit` builds `text`, battle code reads numbers through `H.coneP(u, fx, n)` (module helpers `cp`, `cf`, `coneK` wrap it). The 40 original cones carry the official names, stats and passives; the 23 guest cones (Section 6, Cunning Hares, Solaris-3, Axel, Inaba, Con-Ton) were re-imagined as scenes from their heroes' lives with new names where needed. The art is the author's pixel images in `hsr-cone-pix.js` (`tools/hsr-cone-pix.py`), the screen is `ui.openConeView` (V from the inventory, cone picker, shop and cone banners; arrows preview S1–S5, Tab shows the full art). Numbers quoted in the older sections below predate the overhaul; the tables in `HSR-CONES.md` and the data files are the source of truth.

**Light Cone event banner.** The second event tab sells the signature 5★ Light Cones of the event heroes, the same way the original game pairs a cone banner with each character banner (`BANNERS.cone.stars`, state `coneStar`, chosen separately from the hero with ↑/↓ or the cone cards at the right edge): «Отражение прошлой меня» (`mirror`, Harmony, for Ruan Mei; 4★ Воспоминания о прошлом, Тренд галактического рынка, Беседа после операции) and «В ночи» (`night`, Hunt, for Seele; 4★ Фехтование, Отдых гениев, Спокойной ночи и мирного сна). It spends Special Passes or jade and keeps its own counters (`pityCone` with `guar5`/`guar4`, `warpNCone`). Rules follow the original cone banner, scaled to this game's pity: 5★ rate 1.6% with hard pity at 50 pulls (the hero banners use 1.2% and 60), a 5★ is the chosen signature cone with 75% chance (`TUNING.coneFeatured`), and a miss (one of the standard 5★ cones) makes the next 5★ the signature cone for sure; 4★ work the same way with the three featured cones. Log entries carry `ban: 'cone'`, `star` and `off` for a miss; the history shows «вне события · дальше гарант» on those rows. The bottom panel shows whether the next 5★ is guaranteed.

Signature cones carry `sig` (the hero) and `fx` (a battle effect). `Game.coneEffect(key)` passes `{ id, fx, sup }` into the battle spec only when the cone's Path matches the wearer, like the stat bonus; Superimposition reads the cone's `p` table (S1–S5), see the Light Cones overhaul below. «Отражение прошлой меня»: Break Effect +60%; the team gets 10 energy at the start of the battle; after the wearer's Ultimate the whole team gets +24% DMG for 3 turns. «В ночи»: CRIT Rate +18%; for every 10 SPD above 100 the wearer's basic attack and Skill deal +6% DMG and the Ultimate gets +12% CRIT DMG, up to 6 stacks, which rewards Seele's own Skill speed buff. Art: Ruan Mei and her reflection in a gold mirror with plum blossoms and pearls; Seele with her scythe under the moon over a field of blue flowers with glowing butterflies.

**Light Cones by the original (HSR-CONES.md).** Cones move to the original one at a time. A converted cone carries `ref` (the original cone number), `stats` at level 1 of the original (fractions kept, `charStats` rounds), `skill` (the passive name), `tpl` (the passive text, `#n` is parameter n, `#n%` the same in percent), `p` (five rows, one per Superimposition), `bonus` that points at parameters (`{ critR: 1 }`) and `desc` (the official story). `H.coneInit(map)` builds `text` (S1) from the template, so shops and sites keep reading `text`; `H.coneText(id, sup)` and `H.coneSegs(id, sup)` give the text on any S, `H.coneParam(id, n, sup)` one number, `H.coneStats(id, lvl)` the stats at a hero level. In battle `coneP(u, fx, n)` (also `H.coneP`) returns parameter n at the wearer's S, or 0. Cones without `p` keep the old rule (`coneFx`, `H.coneLegacyMul`: +25% per step). The cone view `ui.openConeView(id, uid)` shows the art at 160 × 224 without stretching, the Path, the S row (← → preview another S, the owned one is underlined), stats at the holder level, the passive with S-dependent numbers in gold (`ui.richWrap`/`ui.richText`), the holder and the story with a scroll bar; Tab shows the art at 320 × 448. It opens with V or a click from the inventory, the cone picker, the credit shop and the cone event banner. `drawConeCard` no longer takes `scale`. Cone art goes through the character pixel pipeline: `coneArt(id, w, h)` picks a pixel size `round(w / 160)` (`GRID_W`), paints the scene on two 4× canvases (background, and after `b.front()` the subject), reduces each with `G.downsample` (most common colour per cell; the subject gets the dark outline) and scales the result up by whole pixels, so the cone view (160 × 224) shows the 160 × 224 picture at 1× and the Tab view (320 × 448) the same picture at 2×. `b.stars` draws whole pixels; `b.shape` fills a polygon with a stroked edge. Shots: `coneview_<key>[_<S>]`, `coneviewart_<key>`, `conecard_<key>` (every card size), `conebig_<a>-<b>` (four large cards), `coneart` (all cones); references: `node tools/hsr-icon-refs.cjs <out> --group=cone` downloads the Full Artwork (`image/light_cone_portrait`) and the cone tables of StarRailRes and draws `ref-cone.png`.

**Version 1.2 heroes (standard banner).** Three 5★ characters joined «Путь Первопроходцев». Each one adds a new verb to the battle rather than a bigger number:

- *Bailu* (Abundance, Lightning) keeps the team standing. Skill «Песнь среди облаков» heals the target, then bounces twice to the most wounded ally (−15% per bounce). Ultimate «Благодатный громовой прыжок» heals everyone and gives Invigoration for 2 turns (an ally that already has it gets +1 turn). Talent: an Invigorated ally heals once per enemy action when hit; once per battle (`flags.elixir`) Bailu stops a killing blow on any ally, herself included, and heals them instead («Тыква эликсира!»). Technique «Прогулка под дождём» starts the battle with Invigoration. Look (redrawn 2026-10-09 from the in-game model): periwinkle hair in two long tails with gold rings, cyan eyes, pointed ears (`elfears`), pink-lilac dragon horns that curve outward with a small tine (`horns`); a black turtleneck under a blue sleeveless tabard with a light cyan cloud hem and a white cloud mark (`coat: 'tabard'`, bare shoulders), gold cloud clasps with yellow tassels (`clasps`), wide white sleeves with blue and gold cuffs (`bellsleeves`), a white fur tuft on the left shoulder (`furtuft`), the medicine gourd with a red cord at the hip (`gourd`), long blue tails behind (`tails`), a short black skirt, fluffy blue leg warmers over black shoes (`warmers`) and the blue dragon tail with a white fin (`dragontail`).
- *Yanqing* (Hunt, Ice) rewards protecting him. Skill «Стремительный железный шип» gives Soulsteel Sync (`flags.sync`): +20% CRIT Rate, +30% CRIT DMG, enemies pick him 60% less often (`aggro` buff), and after each of his attacks a 50% chance of the follow-up «Ледяной клинок» that can freeze. Any hit that takes HP removes Sync; a shield that absorbs the whole hit keeps it, so he pairs well with March 7th and Gepard. Ultimate «Среди блаженного дождя» gives +60% CRIT Rate (and +50% CRIT DMG under Sync) before a 350% strike. Technique «Истинный меч»: +30% DMG against enemies at 50% HP or more for 2 turns (`vsHigh`). Look (redrawn 2026-10-09 from the in-game model): a slim teen (`body: 'f'` with `sex: 'm'`) with `youth: true`, which keeps the male body but draws the softer face (large eyes, thin brows, no red lips); light ash-blond hair with a centre part (`bangs: 'center'`), long front locks and an ahoge, amber eyes; a white shirt with a mandarin collar, silver frogs and a blue and red stripe under a dark teal robe to below the knee (`coat: 'robe'`: black lapels, black sash with a light blue belt and a silver swallow, cloud band with a pink line at the hem), wide elbow sleeves over black forearm sleeves (`halfsleeves`), a teal cape over the right shoulder with a red cord (`sidecape`), a silver cloud brooch (`brooch`), a jade flute with a pink tassel (`flute`), white trousers tucked into pale boots (`tuck: true` draws `boots` over the trousers). The sword was dropped from the look because the model does not carry one.
- *Clara* (Destruction, Physical) fights through Svarog. She takes 10% less damage. When an enemy hits Clara, Svarog strikes back (160%) and puts a Mark on it (`svMark`). Skill «Осторожно, Сварог» hits all enemies and hits every marked enemy a second time, spending the marks. Ultimate «Обещание, а не приказ»: +25% DMG reduction for 2 turns, enemies aim at her much more often, and the next two Svarog counters are strong (260% plus 130% to the neighbours) and answer a hit on any ally (`flags.promise`). Technique «Небольшая цена победы» attacks all enemies and draws their attacks for 2 turns. Clara is the same character as the Clara of the story; the story speaker now takes her look from the kit.

The `aggro` buff stat multiplies the Path weight in `pickTarget` (`1 + aggro`, at least 0.1). New Fusions: «Гости с Лофу» (Yanqing + Bailu: an Ice strike, a team heal and Invigoration) and «Дети Подземья» (Clara + Hook: Svarog hits and marks all enemies, Hook sets them on fire). Mail letter `v12_heroes` (300 jade) announces the update; the title reads «CD-ROM v1.2».

Balance check (60 seeded auto battles per cell, team [X, March 7th, Natasha, Asta], party levels 18 / 16 / 21 against the Gepard, Svarog and first Cocolia fights): Yanqing and Clara land beside Himeko (Yanqing is best against Svarog, slower against Cocolia; Clara is fastest against Gepard), Seele stays the glass cannon. Bailu in Natasha's slot lifts the Svarog fight from 77% to 100%, the same step Gepard gives over March 7th, as expected from a 5★ over a 4★ of the same role.

**Ultimate cutscenes.** An Ultimate is the payoff for the energy a character saved, so it gets its own short scene before the hits land. Each scene has three beats. The cut-in (0.75 s) shows the bust on a band in the element colour with the Ultimate's name and the rarity diamonds. The action (1.25–1.55 s) is unique to the character and uses what the character carries or is known for: the Trailblazer hits a star like a baseball, Preservation Trailblazer throws a burning spear in front of an amber wall, March 7th takes a photo before the ice rain, Dan Heng's spear thrust follows a wind dragon, Himeko fires a beam from orbit, Welt opens a black hole with his cane, Natasha heals under a green cross, Serval plays a chord between amplifiers, Asta draws a constellation, Arlan slams a charged polearm, Herta raises a giant puppet of herself, Gepard raises an ice wall, Bronya leads a Silvermane salute under a waving flag, Seele cuts through a swarm of butterflies, Sampo throws a gift box that explodes, Hook points at a falling fireball, Pela marks targets on a tactical grid, Ruan Mei spreads a flower circle, Bailu jumps into a healing thunder rain, Yanqing sends a fan of flying swords, and Svarog rises behind Clara. The last beat (0.85–0.9 s) shows the outcome in the real battle location: the camera zooms on the enemies the Ultimate hits, with an element burst on each one, or, for support Ultimates, the team gets the healing, shield, buff, speed, bloom or guard effect. The battle UI holds the events of the Ultimate until the scene ends, so damage numbers and kills appear right after it. The scene runs on the battle clock: Settings → «Скорость боя» and Shift speed it up the same way as the fight (×3 plays a scene in about a second). Settings → «Сцены абсолютных техник» has three modes: «Всегда», «Первая в бою» (each character plays the scene once per battle) and «Выкл.» (the old banner in the battle view). Option key `ultCine`: `on` / `first` / `off`.

**Fusion cutscenes.** A Fusion costs two full Ultimates and only exists for a pair of characters who know each other, so its scene is about the pair. The cut-in (0.9 s) splits the screen along a slanted seam: each partner's bust slides in from their own side on a band of their element colour, the halves meet with a white flash, and the plate reads «СЛИЯНИЕ» with the Fusion name over both names. The action (1.6–1.8 s) is unique to each pair and has two lines, one per partner, so the pair talks to each other: March 7th lays an ice rail and Dan Heng runs along it as the Express passes behind («Поезд отправляется!»); the Trailblazer swings the bat while March 7th takes the photo, which then frosts over («Фото на память»); Welt opens the black hole and Himeko's beam sets it on fire («Сингулярность Экспресса»); Serval's guitar sends lightning through Gepard's ice wall («Семья Ландау»); Bronya's wind round opens the way for Seele's butterflies and cuts («Сломанные крылья»); Hook throws a fireball while Natasha's green cross heals («Подземное братство»); Asta draws a constellation and Herta's crystals spin around the station («Станция «Герта»»); Asta's gold ring and lightning charge Arlan's polearm («Смена караула»); Pela marks the enemies and Sampo throws newspapers into a whirlwind («Горячие новости»); Bronya's flag stands in front of the amber wall while the Preservation Trailblazer throws the burning spear («Клятва Клипота»); Bailu rides her water swirl while Yanqing's swords fly («Гости с Лофу»); Svarog rises behind Clara and Hook and slams the ground («Дети Подземья»). The hit beat (0.85 s) zooms on the enemies the Fusion hits and bursts both of its damage elements on each of them, the second one 0.17 s later. Fusions that heal, shield, buff or speed up the team add a team beat (0.75 s) with that effect on the whole party; the partners glow in their element colours. A scene lasts 2.8–4.6 s at ×1. The battle UI takes the `fusion` event and the Fusion's `act` event out of the queue and holds the damage until the scene ends; the scene runs on the battle clock like the Ultimate scenes (×3 or Shift speed it up). Settings → «Сцены слияний» has the same three modes as the Ultimate scenes: «Всегда», «Первая в бою» (each Fusion plays its scene once per battle) and «Выкл.» (the old «СЛИЯНИЕ» banner in the battle view). Option key `fusionCine`: `on` / `first` / `off`.

**Eidolon pictures.** Behind the Eidolons section every character has their own picture, a 320×240 painted scene shown at 2× and cut into a 10×8 jigsaw. Six pieces are Eidolon pieces: they sit under the six stars of the constellation (an arch around the figure: I and II on the left, III and IV above the head, V and VI on the right) and hold the key objects of the scene. A missing piece is a hole that shows the starfield with its star. Each Eidolon places its piece, and three of them also change the whole picture:

| Eidolons | Picture |
|---|---|
| 0 | black and white (luma with a slight contrast boost, 15-bit dither), six holes |
| I | colour returns to the whole picture |
| II | one more piece |
| III | decor comes alive: lamps flicker, stars twinkle, snow, steam, sparks, petals, screens |
| IV, V | more pieces |
| VI | the seams disappear, the whole scene moves (the Express in motion, crowds, Svarog's search light, fireworks, orbiting swords, spinning puppets) and the character comes alive: breathing (the upper body rises one pixel), blinking (the `closed` look) and an aura of their element or motif |

The idea behind it: every duplicate is another meeting with the same person, and the more often you meet them, the more of their world you see. The six fragments of a picture run from everyday things (I and II) through the place (III and IV) to the people and the moment that define the character (V and VI). The fragment lines come from the story of this game and the character kits; they do not add lore. The stat effect of an Eidolon did not change (base HP, ATK and DEF +4% each).

A duplicate from the Warp (`addChar`) or the F8 grant (`debugChar`) remembers the old count in `eidSeen`. `Game.eidNew(k)` is then above zero: the phone marks the «Персонажи» tile (`alerts().chars`), the roster strip marks the portrait and the section list marks «Эйдолоны». Opening the section plays the assembly: each new piece flies in from below the platform and snaps into its hole with a flash (the panel follows the piece), Eidolon I spreads the colour from its piece as a wave, Eidolon III fades the decor in, Eidolon VI fades the seams out with a gold sweep and wakes the character. Any key or click finishes it at once; `seeEid` clears `eidSeen`. Saves without `eidSeen` count every Eidolon as seen, so old saves do not replay anything.

**V** or «Смотреть картину» opens the picture without the interface: the figure stands on a shadow instead of the platform, a plate at the bottom names the character, the picture and its stage, and Q/E go through the pictures of the roster. Pictures are painted once per character (5 to 40 ms in Edge); a frame of a living picture (decor or life) repaints the overlays into the 320×240 buffer and dithers it, about 1.5 to 4 ms.

| March 7th | Вагон-салон | Глыба льда, Фотоаппарат, Гирлянда снимков, Снимок Ярило-VI, Лук, Фото на память |

| Ruan Mei | Лунное окно | Пирожные, Пяльцы, Ветка сливы, Луна, Пробирки, Лепестки на воде |
| Bailu | Аптечная терраса на Лофу | Тыква эликсира, Шкаф с травами, Облака Лофу, Фонари, Зонтик, Громовой прыжок |
| Yanqing | Площадка Облачных Рыцарей | Чучело, Стойка с мечами, Знамя Облачных Рыцарей, Облака Лофу, Истинный меч, Единение души и стали |

To add a character, add a scene to `hsr-eidolon-scenes.js` with the key objects in the six Eidolon cells (in picture pixels: I 96–128 × 150–180, II 96–128 × 90–120, III 128–160 × 30–60, IV 160–192 × 30–60, V 192–224 × 90–120, VI 192–224 × 150–180). The figure covers about 128–178 × 86–222, the section list darkens the left third and the panel hides x ≥ 220, which the full-screen view still shows. The test fails for a character without a picture.

**Characters.** Every character has one `look` object in `hsr-data.js` (skin, hair, streak or tips, eyes, bangs/sides/back/crown, body type, coat and collar, colours, accessories such as `beret`, `ushanka`, `molehat`, `gauntlet`, `boots`). The looks were checked against the official character art: Arlan has brown skin, silver-white hair with a dark strand, magenta eyes and a light jacket with a blue lining; Herta has ash-brown hair and a black beret with a violet flower; Hook wears the white mole hat; Sampo wears a crimson coat. The female Trailblazer gets long hair, an open coat to mid-thigh with a gold lining, a skirt, a thigh strap and ankle boots from `trailLook('f')`. Men and women use different body types: `body: 'm'` and `'tall'` (or `sex: 'm'`) select the male type with a squarer jaw, narrower eyes without lashes, thick brows drawn over the bangs, a plain mouth instead of red lips, a thicker neck and square shoulders in the bust; in the figure broader shoulders with deltoids, a V-shaped torso, a smaller head (`hu` 0.44–0.45), longer arms, bigger hands and feet; in the field sprite a torso two pixels wider, lower-hanging arms and 1-pixel eyes under brows. `'f'`, `'tallf'` and `'small'` keep the female or child type. **Legs and skirts** (reworked 2026-10-09 from the in-game model renders on the fandom wiki, because skirted figures read as bare below the waist and Clara looked undressed): legs are one tapered shape per side (thigh, knee, calf) with a shade stripe on the right leg. The left leg ends at x 24 and the right one starts at x 25, so a one-pixel gap separates them right from the hem: a gap that is not aligned to the pixel grid gets filled by the 4×4 reduction, the thighs merge and the gap only opens lower as a dark wedge. Nothing dark may sit between the legs under a hem (no skirt lining below the hem, no inner thigh shading); bare legs get only a light shade band under the hem. Skirts (`low: 'skirt'`, hem at `hip + skirtLen`, default 10) flare wider, with pleats with light and dark folds, a waistband and an optional hem stripe (`hem`) and petticoat lace (`petti`); Coats `mid` (open to mid-thigh, `lining` colour on the front edges and hem, `coatLen`) and `parka` (Clara: closed red coat that opens below the belt, sweater V at the neck, belt with a loose strap, fur at the hem corners and cuffs) draw their back behind the legs. Legwear and shoes are accessories: `stockings` (thigh-high), `socks` (to the knee), `ankle` (ankle boots), `boots` (to the knee), `thighboots` (with `kneeplate`), `garter` (strap on the left thigh), `barefoot`; `legs` still colours full tights. `tails` hangs long panels behind the legs (to the knee, or to the ankle with `tailsTo: 'ankle'`). Current canon: March 7th (redesigned 2026-10-09, see «Character redesign») has a blue pleated skirt with a white hem stripe and petticoat, a white garter with a blue bow and black ankle boots with blue cuffs; Natasha a dark teal front panel between long white panels, red tails, black tights and knee boots; Asta a white open coat with a violet lining over a dark skirt; Herta a lace petticoat, white knee socks and violet coat tails; Bronya black tights, black thigh boots with gold knee plates and blue tails; Seele navy shorts, violet tails and knee boots; Hook a dark skirt with a yellow hem and knee boots; Pela white ankle boots over tights; Clara a red parka over a beige sweater and a white dress, a black thigh strap, barefoot. **Model pass** (2026-10-09, every playable character except Bailu, Yanqing and Clara, from the in-game model renders `Character <Name> Game.png` on the fandom wiki): small details are look keys shared by the bust, the figure and the field sprite. `rolled` (sleeves rolled to the elbow with a grey cuff, both Trailblazers), `waistbelt` (belt with a gold buckle, only between the panels of an open coat), `crossbelt` (Sampo), `claw` (Sampo's silver claw on the left arm), `straps` and `buttons` (Welt), `coatLow` (lower colour of a long coat: Dan Heng's teal), `L.sleeve` (sleeve colour that differs from the coat: March 7th's white blouse sleeves under the lilac jacket), `waistbow` (March 7th's tied jacket), `hairflower` (Himeko's gold rose), `sashtail` with `sashSide` (Asta's pink ribbon, Seele's red one), `leotard` and `onesleeve` (Seele's white centre and single long sleeve), `kneepad` / `kneepadr` (Arlan, Hook), `furcollar`, `chestplate` and `pauldron` (Gepard: fur on the shoulders, silver armour with ice crystals), `drills` (Bronya's curls), `furcuffs` (Hook), `L.cuff` (cuff colour of `bellsleeves`: Herta's white cuffs on black sleeves). Colours were corrected too: Caelus wears the long coat, Welt has no white streak and wears brown trousers, Natasha has slate hair and brown gloves, Serval a blue skirt over dark tights, Arlan a cream jacket and blue cargo trousers, Gepard white trousers in black boots with blue tails, Sampo a grey vest and navy trousers, Hook a brown coat over a yellow shirt, Pela a black beret with gold ornaments instead of an ushanka. Pom-Pom wears the red conductor's cap with a gold headlamp and black visor, a red tailcoat with gold cuffs and black lapels, a cream beard with a gold clasp, and has long dark ears that hang like hair. The bust and the figure are pre-rendered from the 3D model of the Warp (`hsr-pompom`); the 18×26 field sprite is drawn by hand in the same chunky style as the other sprites, with the same colours and features (lamp on the cap, ears, beard, belly, gold cuffs) in all four directions.

**Character redesign** (2026-10-09, user request, one character per step with the user's approval in between; Bailu, Yanqing, Clara and Svarog are excluded). Each character is compared with the fandom in-game render and reference sheet and gets its own silhouette details as look keys shared by the bust, the figure and all four sprite directions. Done: March 7th — pale pink-white hair with a pink gradient (`tipsFade`: a half-tone band, then `tips` from bust y 44), layered bangs `wisp` with alternating shaded strands, jagged shoulder-length `sides`/`back: 'flick'`, pink-over-blue eyes (`eyeFade` also splits the sprite eyes), coat `shrug` (white blouse, the blue jacket worn off the shoulders with a lilac `shoulder` gradient and tied in a knot under the bust, lavender hip `belt`), collar `sailor`, `choker` with `halter` straps, blue `tassel` earring, `clover` pin, `glove1` (one black glove), `cufftails`, `bowcase` (black bow at the right hip under the `camera`), short `sashtail` (`sashTo: 'knee'`), `garterbow`, `anklecuff`, `backbow` (field sprite seen from behind). Dan Heng: new slim male body `slim` (narrower shoulders, waist and hips than `m`, also in the Eidolon and Warp intro `WAIST`/`HANDS` tables), V-shaped jaw `jaw: 'v'`, shaggy hair (`crown: 'shag'` side tufts, `sides: 'shag'` jagged locks, small `ahoge: 'curl'`, dark `hairHi`), `wisp` bangs (on a male face the brows are drawn under the bangs), red `eyeliner` (bust sizes only), silver `dangle` earring on the right ear and teal `earcuff` on the left, coat `split` (white top, teal lower half from the waist, teal `lining` along the opening, split hem below the knee, dark back panel between the legs), black high collar with a `zip` and gold `stars` (constellation), teal `dragonpad` with a white tassel and black `armbandr` on his left arm, black `bracer` with a red cuff on his right forearm, teal `flap` under the belt, brown `hipbelt`, `thighstrap` with a jade bead, calf boots (`bootsTo: 'calf'`) with a gold `bootstrap`. Stelle (`TRAIL_F` in hsr-art, Caelus keeps the base look): silver-grey hair with `crown: 'wild'` (two loose strands on each side and one on top, no tufts on the crown so no cat ears), `wisp` bangs, long jagged `sides`/`back: 'layer'` (to the waist in the figure, shaded strands in the bust and the field sprite), coat `mid` that hangs straighter (`coatFlare: 2.4`, `coatLen: 15`) with gold `piping` along the lapels and a gold `shbuckle` on the right shoulder, the gold feather pin, black gloves with a `goldcuff`, a short black skirt (`skirtLen: 8`) with two gold `skirtbuckles`, a grey tie and a light-blue glimpse of shorts at the slit, a diagonal `knifebelt` with the red-handled knife and a silver ring, a yellow `sashtail` on her left side to the knee, ankle boots with a gold `anklerim`. The test checks `back` is `layer` for Stelle and `wolf` for Caelus. Pitfall: `hug` clamps every bang point to the head outline, so a bang band whose corners sit at the temples drops its whole top edge to y 20 and leaves skin gaps between the strands; band shapes need extra top points in the middle (fixed for `wisp` and `sweep`). Caelus (base `CHARS.trail.look`, also `trail_p`): `slim` body and `jaw: 'v'`, the same silver-grey hair as Stelle cut short (smooth domed crown with `gloss` highlights, `bangs: 'sweep'` = one mass with a jagged lower edge of clumps swept to his right and a thin strand to the nose, shaded along the clump partings, `sides: 'feather'` with outward flicks at ear level and thin locks to the jaw, `back: 'wolf'` with wisps down the neck), coat `split` in black on both halves (`coatLow` = `top`) to the knee (`coatLen: 23`, the split hem is now measured from the hip, Dan Heng keeps 31), gold `lining` on the opening and on the inside of the back panel (`backLining`), white cowl T-shirt (`bustSplit` draws a draped `hood` collar with the gold hood lining at the neck), gold `piping`, `shbuckle`, feather pin, rolled sleeves, fingerless black gloves with `goldcuff`, a yellow `armribbon` tied on his right upper arm with a loose end, the `knifebelt`, a gold pocket `watch` on a chain at his left hip, a yellow `sashtail` to the knee, black trousers with red `pantstripe` lines on the shins, black sneakers with white `soles`. Stelle overrides every key that matters, so her look did not change. Himeko: crimson hair with `gloss`, `sweep` bangs (on a female face the clump tips over the eyes sit 2.4 units higher, so the eyes stay open), long wavy `sides`/`back: 'wave'` with curled ends, the gold rose `hairflower`, amber eyes with a yellow lower half (`eye2`); coat `cape` is now a black coat with a patterned tan `lining` thrown over the shoulders (behind the body down to the shin, shoulder caps and her black stand collar drawn over the arms, gold studs at the hem, a gold cord with a teal `capetassel`), bare arms (`cape` counts as bare-armed), a white `ruffle` and black coat sleeve above the elbow on her right arm with a black `glove1`, a gold `bangle` with a black scrunchie on her left wrist; white halter dress (`collar: 'halter'`: high neck band under the black `choker`, crossed straps, gold ring, gold `rose` on the collar; `bustCape`), gold `leafband` embroidery under the bust and at the waist, a ruffled `peplum`, black `lace` flower panel on the thigh, and `low: 'gown'`: a floor-length skirt with a high slit over her right leg, red lining that widens into the train, the outer left panel behind it. Welt: body `lean` (between `slim` and `tall`, added to the Eidolon/Warp intro tables), `jaw: 'v'`, brown hair with `crown: 'swept'` (raised top and flyaway locks at the upper right), `bangs: 'sidepart'` (part on his right, a lock to the temple on that side, the rest swept over the brow to a long lock beside his left eye; brows drawn under), pointed `sides: 'temple'`, a faint ash `streak` (`STREAKS.thin`), lower rectangular `glasses:slim`; coat `split` in light grey to the knee (`coatLen: 20`) with brown `lining`/`backLining`; black high collar (`neck` colour for `collar: 'high'`, also in the figure) with a silver `neckpiece` and two rivets, a dark `scarf` (all Welt-only keys were reused: the scarf now wraps the neck and falls over his left chest with a fringe), a silver diamond `brooch2`, white vest with a black `vestv` and round `buttons`, brown `straps` on both sleeves, `cuffbtn` and a black `glove1` on his right hand, a brown `harness` with silver plates over the trousers, the cane. Natasha: slate hair with `gloss`, `sweep` bangs, shoulder-length `flick` sides, the low tail with a white `hairwrap` at the nape; pink eyes; coat `bolero` (white bolero with puffed short sleeves and grey trim over a black corset `inner` with gold `corset` studs, `bustBolero`), black high `neck` collar with a gold rim and the green `vial` pendant, a red `shoulderbow` on her right shoulder, a long dark `longglove1` on her right arm, a gold `armbandr` and dark wristband on the bare left arm holding the gold `hourglass` syringe; `low: 'slit'` with `slitTo: 'knee'` (navy front panels to the knee, narrower, uneven hem, the centre seam replaced by silver `sigil` marks), white `tails` to the calf (`tailsTo: 'calf'`) with red `tailsLining`, brown `hipbelt`, the brown teddy `bear` at her right hip, dark tights, knee boots with a blue `bootgem`. The red kerchief and the white hair ribbon are gone. Serval: ash-blonde wavy hair (`sides`/`back: 'wave'`, `sweep` bangs, `gloss`) that turns light blue only near the ends (`tipsAt: 58` moves the `tipsFade` band down in the bust; in the figure the back hair turns at the chest), a thin blue lock (`streakShape: 'lock'`, one pixel wide on the sprite), a silver `hoop` earring, the `choker` with a silver ring; coat `half` (asymmetric: bare shoulders, white jacket front on her right with a loose white sleeve with blue and pink cuff lining and a black wrist wrap, black ruffled bandeau on her left with a long `blacksleeve` with silver buckles and a `pinkglove`, bare midriff with a pink `tattoo`, white `xstraps`, blue `feathers` with a gold cross on her right shoulder; `bustHalf`), blue pleated skirt with black studded `shortsr` on the left half, a black `beltstrap` hanging to the knee, gold `pegs`, one white tail on her right to the ankle (`tailsOne: 'l'`), sheer brown tights with a black `stocking1` and red `gband` with gold chevrons on the right leg, ankle boots with blue `anklecuff`. Asta: dusty-rose hair with `sweep` bangs, long sides, a small `sidetail` on her left (drawn behind the head in `backAcc`, at the back of the head on profile sprites), a `sidebraid` on that side, the gold ring hairpin `clip` moved to her right fringe; blue eyes; coat `offcoat` (a white coat slipped off the shoulders: bare shoulders and upper arms, the coat hangs from the elbows with wide sleeves to below the wrists and magenta `lining` that turns pink lower down, front panels to `coatLen: 17`, black clips; `bustOffcoat` shows the bunched coat at the arms), sleeveless frilled blouse (`ruffles`) with a white stand collar, purple `neckband`, the big black `bow` with purple and gold tails, the lanyard; blue `waistband`, gold and pink `pinkbelt` straps; skirt half purple (`panell`) half black with gold arcs (`goldarc`), white `thighbow`, black `garter`, a pink `sashtail` to the ankle, purple `bracelets` on her right wrist, heels with an `anklestrap`. Arlan: `slim` body, `jaw: 'v'`, off-white hair with a smooth crown and `gloss`, `bangs: 'hide'` (a heavy fringe that hides his right eye down to the cheek; the sprite covers that eye too), `feather` sides, the short `nape` tail, a dark `templel` streak at that temple; coat `cut` (asymmetric cream jacket: long on his right with black `chevrons`, cropped at the chest on his left over the black suit; his left arm has a black short sleeve, bare forearm, white `gloveR` with a blue cuff, the blue `armband` with the gold crest and a silver `pauldronr`; his right arm keeps the cream sleeve with a blue band and the mechanical `gauntlet` hand; `bustCut`), black high `neck` with a white tab, blue `hoodline`, the round `gear` device and the ID `badge`, dark-blue `harnessb` belt, black `pouch` on the left thigh, a blue `cord`, the `kneepad`, calf boots with white `soles`. `gauntlet` and `armband` were Arlan-only keys and moved to the arms they sit on in the original. Herta: new body `petite` (a slight girl with long legs instead of the chibi `small`; added to the Eidolon/Warp intro tables), ash-taupe hair with `gloss`, blunt bangs and a few `strays` flicking out low at the sides (placed below the cheek; higher they read as elf ears), the black `beret` with a gold `beretBand` and the purple lily; coat `doll` (bare shoulders with a white `shoulderfrill`, black lapels down the sides of the white dress, black `bellsleeves` with white cuffs, black `tails` to the calf with a wide purple `tailsLining`; `bustDoll`), purple `keychoker` with a silver key (drawn below the chin line), gold `padlock` brooch with silver `chains`, purple `tie`, lavender `diamonds` on the dress, the tiered skirt with petticoat lace, white `stockings` with doll knee joints (`dolljoint`), ankle boots with white `anklecuff` flaps and purple `heelbow`. Gepard: `lean` body (was `tall`), `jaw: 'v'`, ash-sandy hair (redone once on request): `crown: 'wind'` (rounded top with wind-swept locks flaring out to the upper right and left, a small lock on top; on the sprite only side flicks, no top tufts), `bangs: 'swoop'` (strands swept from the upper right down to the left, one long lock over the nose, shaded partings, a light highlight lock), `sides: 'jaw'` (thin locks to the jaw); blue eyes; closed white coat with a white high collar and blue stripe, white `furcollar` with grey shading, the V-shaped silver `chestplate` with ice crystals rising over the fur, gold `aiguillette` and `goldclasp` rings, black belt and blue `hemband`, a layered white-and-blue `pauldron` with ice spikes on his right shoulder, an `icegauntlet` with ice shards and a white fur `pelt` hanging from that hand, a black `bracerR` with a blue cuff on his left forearm, one blue `tails` panel on his left (`tailsOne: 'r'`, now supported next to `'l'`) to the ankle with a cyan `tailsLining`, white trousers with gold `goldstripes`, blue `kneeband` and silver `bootplate` on black riding boots. All his armour keys were Gepard-only and were redrawn in place. Bronya: body `tallf`, silver-lavender hair with `sweep` bangs and `gloss`, long sides and back, bigger `drills` (five curls per side from the chest to the hip in the figure), grey-brown eyes, big purple `dropgem` earrings; coat `wrap` (sleeveless white wrap dress with bare shoulders, black high `neck`, detached white puff sleeves at the elbows with gold straps on her right and a blue cuff on her left, long brown gloves; `bustWrap`), the blue `icebow` with a gold knot and ice crystals on her chest, the `medal` redrawn as the red-and-white ribbon on a blue band on her left upper arm, gold `goldclasp` rings and `goldtassel`, a short white front skirt (`skirtLen: 8`) with long white `tails` to the ankle and blue `tailsLining`, dark tights, black `thighboots` with gold `kneeplate`, a blue gem and gold `shinleaf` ornaments. `halfsleeves` and the old blue `bow` are gone. Seele: navy hair with `gloss`, `sweep` bangs, the big ahoge, long jagged `sides`/`back: 'layer'` that turns violet from the chest down (`tips` + `tipsFade`, `tipsAt: 54`); violet eyes; coat `leo` (bare shoulders, black bustier with a bow, white `leotard` centre with bare sides at the waist; `bustLeo`), a blue `neckscarf` with a red stripe and a knot, black shorts; her right arm in a black gauntlet sleeve with white `furcuff` rings at the shoulder and wrist; her left arm bare with a black armlet, the red `armribbon2` bow with a long tail and the flared blue `onesleeve` from the elbow; violet `petals` panels over the hips fading from white through cyan, one grey `tails` panel on her right to the ankle, the red `sashtail`, cyan `legstraps` and a black garter on the right thigh, black `shinx` cross straps on the left shin, blue ankle boots with a black `anklerim`. The red neck bow is gone. Sampo: `lean` body, `jaw: 'v'`, dark indigo hair with `crown: 'swept'`, `gloss`, Gepard's `swoop` bangs (a lock between the eyes), thin `jaw` locks with grey-white `tips` near the nape; green eyes, silver earring; the cropped red `jacket` with purple lapels, white `rolled` sleeves, black gloves with a red `glovecuff`, a black `turtle` neck, the grey vest with a pointed `vesthem`, the black `crossbelt` redrawn as a harness strap with silver spikes plus a hip belt, the silver `claw` moved to his right shoulder and upper arm with a purple blade (on the bust's left edge, on the sprite's shoulder), a silver `chainband` on his left forearm, a black `holster` with a red strap and a silver blade on his left thigh, dark purple trousers tucked into calf boots with a `kneeband`. Hook: keeps the chibi `small` body; lime-blonde hair with `gloss`, amber eyes; the `molehat` redrawn (rounded white mole hat with dark octagonal eye holes, brown ear flaps with white fur pompoms hanging past the cheeks, the gold `molebadge` with orange and yellow ribbons on the right side; the sprite gets the flaps too); coat `mid` (dark brown, to `coatLen: 9`, dark `lining`) with a yellow `yoke` on the shoulders, the white fur collar, white `furcuffs`, black gloves, yellow pleated dress with the big yellow `bow` and a gold badge, a yellow `tiestripe` with a white stripe, yellow-buckled `pouches` at both hips, a short dark skirt with a yellow hem, a grey `kneepadr` on her left knee, black calf boots with yellow `socktop`, an orange `bootband` and orange soles. Pela: indigo hair with `gloss`, blunt bangs with silver `hairpins`, straight hime `sides: 'straight'` to the chest, teal eyes, round glasses; the black `beret` now carries gold medallions with blue gems and gold tassels on both sides (`beretgold`, replaces the flower; the sprite shows two gold dots); closed white jacket with a black `blackyoke` over the shoulders and silver clasps, a white high collar, the blue `cravat` with a gold leaf clip, a white `whitebow` below it, blue `sidepanel` stripes with gold edges, white `puff` short sleeves over black `longgloves`, a black pleated skirt (`skirtLen: 7`), white `tails` with blue `tailsLining`, brown tights, white ankle boots with a blue `heelbow`. Pitfall found here: body-level bust shapes must live in `bustBodyAcc`, not in `paintHeadAcc`; the head painter also runs for the figure's scaled head, so Hook's `yoke`/`tiestripe` and Pela's `blackyoke`/`cravat` were drawn as huge shapes around the figure's head until they were moved. Ruan Mei: dark hair with `gloss`, `sweep` bangs, `flick` sides and shoulder `back`, the gold-pinned `bun`, a white `hairblossom` with a gold pin at her right temple, a dark teal `streak` low under the hair on that side (`streakShape: 'under'`); the teal `dress` with a sheer centre (`sheer`, gold clasp and gold scallops on the bust), teal `shorts` hem, the navy `frontpanel` with gold rungs and a gold curve hanging between the legs, the cream `shawl` (draped over the shoulders in the bust with teal and pink tassels; in the figure it hangs behind the arms to the knee from `figBackAcc`, with tan tassels), cream `puff` caps, dark teal `longgloves`, a silver `bangle`, light-blue `tails` with teal `tailsLining`, a teal `garter`, navy heels. With her every playable character except Bailu, Yanqing, Clara and Svarog has been redesigned. Second pass (2026-10-10, user request): the future playable characters in `SPEAKERS` get the same treatment, one at a time. Kafka: wine-purple hair with `gloss`, `sweep` bangs, `flick` locks and the low tail, round black `shades` pushed up on the head (on the sprite too); coat `cape` with a `sleeve` colour means a long-sleeved white shirt with the black coat worn over the shoulders (`capeLen: 18` to mid-thigh, dark plum lining only along the opening, gold studs; `bustCape` draws the shirt, the pointed `collar: 'shirt'` and the coat edges), silver-ring `choker` (moved below the chin), black `chestharness`, high black `corsetHi` with gold spikes and silver buttons, magenta `jabot`, white `ruffcuff` cuffs, magenta gloves, black shorts, magenta `thighcross` straps, plum tights, one black thigh `boot1` on her right leg with a silver zip and gold tip plus a `thighstrap`, an ankle boot on the left. Silver Wolf: ash-grey short hair with `gloss`, `wisp` bangs, `jaw` sides and the `nape` back, a big dark `tailbow` with blue dots on the ponytail (drawn behind the head in `backAcc`), a purple `visor` (game goggles) pushed up on the head; coat `crop` (cropped black jacket with puffed sleeves, blue `lining` on the opening, white fur `collar`, white belt strips at the hem, a white crop top with a black strap and a logo, bare midriff; `bustCrop` with a pin badge), dark elbow bands, fingerless gloves, a white `whitesleeve` on her left forearm, the `choker`, black shorts with a `whitebelt` and a controller buckle, a purple `sash2` with checker dots on her left hip to the knee, a `gunholster` with a hanging blade at her right hip, a `thighgadget` with a blue strap on the right thigh, a light-blue `kneebandage` on the left thigh, a `fishnet1` patch below the left knee, calf boots with blue `bootflap` cuffs and white bands. The old face bandage and side ponytail are gone. Cocolia (also the figure of the boss `cocolia1`; the Mother of Deception form keeps `COCOLIA_MOD` in hsr-foes): honey-blonde hair with `parted` bangs, `jaw` sides and the low tail, the `braid` now hangs on her left (`braidSide: 'r'`), a black `hairbowback` behind the head, purple `dropgem` earrings, violet eyes; white-silver dress with a black high `neck` and a white `xcollar`, the blue `iceemblem` on the bodice, a gold `icebrooch` with ice crystals and a blue ribbon on her left chest, the green `mantle` with `medals` on her right shoulder (on the sprite too), dark brown `sleeve`s and bodysuit (`low: 'shorts'`), a white `glove1`, a white `apronw` front panel fading to ice blue, long white `tails` to the calf, brown tights, black `thighboots` with gold `kneeplate` and `shinleaf`. Miyabi (refs from the Zenless Zone Zero fandom wiki, `Agent_Hoshimi_Miyabi_In-Game.png` and `Hoshimi_Miyabi_Character_Sheet.png`): fox ears are all black now (lighter grey inside instead of white), straight hime `sides`, the teal haori `split` coat gets a gold `lining` at the collar and opening, white `cuff`s with white `haoritri` triangles on the sleeves (bust corners too), a black `obi` underbust corset with a silver plate and straps, a gold `goldtassel` charm, a red `redcuff` on her left wrist, a `skirtslit` that shows the tights on the right leg, darker skirt. Aqua (`Aqua-anime.png` and the season 3 visual `Aqua_3rd.png`): body `tallf`, pale sky-blue hair with `gloss` that deepens to a stronger blue toward the ends (`tips` with `tipsFade`), `wisp` bangs, long `sides`, the new `back: 'flow'` (long hair that tapers into points beside the knees in the figure, like `long` in the bust, two tip pixels on the sprite), the `hairloop` (a ring of hair standing up on top of the head with the blue orb and two gold beads at its base; three pixels and the orb on the sprite), blue eyes; coat `sleeveless` (navy vest with cut-in armholes over bare shoulders, white V edges from the shoulder points to the bow over a lavender yoke (`inner`), a white front stripe left of centre and a white hem band over the skirt; `bustSleeveless`), the dark maroon high collar (`collar: 'high'` with `neck`), the green `gembow` with gold tails and the blue gem, white `detsleeves` from the middle of the upper arm with a gold band and navy cuffs (the bust is cropped above them), a navy pleated skirt with a gold `hem` stripe and a lavender petticoat, navy `thighboots` that start lower (`thighTop: 6`, default 10) so the bare thigh shows, with a pale blue-white band, a navy line above it and gold V flaps on the boot tops (`bootvflap`). The Guild scene follows the art: the hill and chant shots take the orb and the open eye from `staffOrb` and `figEye`, the eye cut from `bustEye`, and she now falls to the left so her whole body stays in the frame.
