Darkness (`Darkness-anime.png`, `Darkness_3rd.png`): body `tallf`, golden hair with `gloss`, `wisp` bangs, long `sides`, the new `back: 'hightail'` (a high ponytail: a knot behind the crown with a braid band over the top of the head, the tail sweeping down her right side behind the shoulder to the hip; on the sprite a top knot, a column beside her and a tail down the back seen from behind) with orange ends (`tips`, `tipsFade`, `tipsAt: 58`), red crossed `xclips` at both temples, blue eyes; coat `crusader` (a black bodysuit (`inner`, `sleeve`, `legs`, black high `neck`), the white-lilac breastplate (`plate`) with orange trim on the V neckline and the lower edge, a short orange band with white stripes above the belt, below it a black skirt to mid-calf, the orange front panel with white edges that ends in a point, long white side panels (an orange stripe on the left one), white `tassets` at the hips; `bustCrusader`), the red `ribbonbow` with a gold gem, white feathers on her right shoulder (`wingpad`) and a layered pauldron on her left (`platepad`), the `studbelt`, brown-orange `gauntlets` with white bands on both forearms, black gloves and white `greaves` with knee cops and white sabatons. Her sword stays out of the figure because the Guild knight cut and her Ultimate plant their own sword next to her. Pom-Pom (2026-10-17, user request: the bust and figure baked from the 3D model stood out from everyone else): redrawn as 2D art from the in-game NPC model render (biligame wiki `建模-NPC-帕姆.png`, because fandom's image CDN now answers curl and headless Edge with a Cloudflare challenge). One drawing `pomArt(p, L, q)` in its own 100×100 space (feet at y 100) serves both: the bust frames art y 4..72 at 0.94 of the bust unit, the figure scales it by 0.58 so Pom-Pom stands 58 units tall on `FOOT`; `q` (art units per output pixel) turns on fine details below 1.3 (lens mesh, stitch dashes, plaque glyphs, armband lines, watch hands) and picks the mouth: a 7-pixel `ω` at bust size, a 3-pixel `v` at figure size, a smooth line above 2× size. Look: cream head with a dark widow's peak under the visor, big blue eyes under a thick black slanted lid line, pink cheeks, long ears hanging to the floor and flaring out (cream front at the face, dark outer side lower down with a cream inner edge), a cream ruff with the gold trefoil brooch and a white jabot, the open red coat over a cream belly with dark edges and hem, black cuffs and white paws, the oversized red armband with a gold border and emblem on the left arm, a gold pocket watch on a chain, dark legs with cream feet; the tall red shako widening upward with a black top rim, gold side edges, the plaque with trefoils, a small clock over the headlamp in a gold gear ring and toothed gold brackets, and a black glossy visor with a gold rim. The field sprite `spritePom` follows the same colours (cream ear fronts, belly, armband, watch); its mouth pixel is pale pink because a dark one read as an open mouth.

**Default facial expressions** (2026-10-18, user request). Every HSR character has a resting expression taken from the fandom in-game model render (`Character <Name> Game.png`, `Character Trailblazer Game (M|F).png`, Cocolia from `NPC Cocolia.png`). The look key `mood` picks a preset from `MOODS` in `hsr-art.js`; a preset sets eyes (`open`, `soft` = upper lid one pixel lower, `narrow` = lowered lid slanting down toward the nose, `half`), mouth (`line`, `smile` and `frown` = two lip pixels with softer corner pixels one row up or down, `tiny`) and brows (`level`, `soft`, `low`, `high`, `stern`, `arch`). Presets: `smile` March 7th, Serval, Asta, Hook, Pela, Bailu, Yanqing, Sushang, Guinaifen, Topaz; `gentle` Himeko, Natasha, Kafka, Tingyun, Yukong, Cocolia; `sly` Sampo; `lazy` Qingque; `flat` Silver Wolf, Hanya (and Phrolova); `cool` Dan Heng, Bronya, Ruan Mei; `serious` Welt, Gepard, Luka; `stern` Arlan, Blade; `pout` Seele; `blank` Stelle (`TRAIL_F`), Herta, Clara, Lynx, Xueyi. Caelus keeps the old neutral face (no `mood`). Lids, brows and mouth shapes only apply at a head scale of 0.75 or more (busts, figures at 2× and up); smaller figure heads keep the plain face, except `half` lids. Cinematic variants still override: `closed` replaces the eyes and `mouth: 'open'` the mouth. Outfits inherit `mood` from the character look.

**Enemies.** Each enemy is drawn once in a 100×100 unit space by a function in `hsr-foes.js` and rasterised through the same pipeline as the characters: 52 px for normal enemies, 70 for elites and 100 for bosses (all shown at 2×), 22–32 px for field symbols. The designs follow the official models on HoYoWiki: Voidrangers in dark armour (gold blades for the Reaver, a blue blade and a tabard for the Eliminator, a white-plated centaur for the Trampler), crystal Spawns by element, the Baryon ball, the traffic-light Beetle, the bike-like Hound, the tube-headed Spider, the tall Direwolf, Silvermane Guards with white masks and blue plaid (tall white helmets for the Cannoneer and the Lieutenant), the Vagrant miner, Shadewalker knights, the orb-bodied Frigid Prowler and the Doomsday Beast. Gepard and Cocolia bosses reuse their character figures with a shield or a spear; Cocolia, Mother of Deception has her own art `motherArt` (2026-10-10: masked face with a spiked ice crown, blonde fringe, a purple starry bodysuit under ice armour, black clawed arms, armoured legs fused into an ice point, white hair and blue and violet ribbons streaming to both sides, six floating cross glaives that bob between the two frames); the old `COCOLIA_MOD` figure and its prop layers were removed. `META` in the module holds the idle animation flag, the aura colour and the crop of the portrait icon used in the action order, the boss bar and the Archive.

**Maps.** Every map is prerendered once into a 16 px layer and drawn at 2×, so tiles, characters and enemies share one pixel size. Walls are autotiled: a top and a front face where the floor is below, rims on open sides, contact shadows on the floor next to walls. Snow maps separate cliffs (snow with rock outcrops and a rim) from boulders (grey rocks with snow caps); ice maps use faceted blue ice. Chests, Space Anchors (the crystal floats and a ring turns once activated), Fragmentum crates, levers, red barriers and exit arrows (pointing off the map, red with a lock while closed) are small pixel objects.

**Pixel pipeline.** Busts and figures are drawn as vector shapes on a 4× canvas and reduced by taking the most common colour of each 4×4 block, then a selective outline is added. Thin details (eyes, glasses, pins) use pixel-snapped rects so they survive the reduction. Bangs and side locks are clamped to the head outline (`hug`), otherwise their corners stick out like ears. Light Cone art is painted at the card size and gets a PS1-style 15-bit ordered dither.

## Music

Seven tracks are MIDI arrangements of the OST by HOYO-MiX. The files are in `content/music/` next to the recordings they were made from, and `hsr-audio` loads them with `H.Midi.load` the first time they are needed.

| Track id | File | Plays | Tempo | Loop |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `menu` | `Main Theme.mid` (login theme) | title screen, also after "exit to title" | 66 BPM | the whole piece, 18 bars (the last bar is 3/4), 64.5 s |
| `battle`, `boss` | `Battle Theme.mid` (*Crises*) | regular fights on the Herta Space Station and in the Simulator, the Grizzly in the Great Mine, the cinematic `c_drones` | 134 BPM | 5 intro bars once, then 64 bars (114.6 s) |
| `order` | `Order.mid` (*Order*) | every regular fight on Jarilo-VI (field enemies, `bp_bronya`, `seele`) and the cinematics `c_escape`, `c_ambush`, `c_smoke`, `c_seele` | 109 BPM | bars 0–3 once, then bars 4–51 (105.7 s) |
| `svarog` | `Godfather.mid` (*Godfather*) | the Svarog fight `svarog` | 130 BPM | 8 beats once, then 186 beats (85.8 s) |
| `doomsday` | `Dawn of Disaster.mid` (*Dawn of Disaster*) | the Doomsday Beast fight `doomsday`, its cinematic `c_doom` and the Echo of War `cx_echo_doom` | 146 BPM | bar 0 once, then bars 1–61 (100.3 s) |
| `gepard` | `Braving the Cold.mid` (*Braving the Cold*) | both Gepard fights `gepard` and `gepard2` and the cinematic `c_gepard` | 145 BPM | bars 0–8 once, then bars 9–70 (102.6 s) |
| `cocolia` | `Cocolia Boss Theme.mid` (*Wildfire*) | the encounters `cocolia1` and `cocolia2` and the Echo of War `cx_echo_coco` (`music` field in `CALYX`) | 144 BPM | bars 0–66 once, then bars 67–110 (73.3 s) |

Where a battle theme plays:

- A world can name its regular battle theme (`battle` in `H.WORLDS`, set to `order` for Jarilo-VI). `Game.battleTheme()` reads it for the map you stand on, and both field encounters and story battles without their own `music` use it, so everything on the Herta station and in the Express Simulator keeps *Crises*.
- Every story boss with a theme names it in `ENCOUNTERS`; the Grizzly has none and keeps `boss`.
- A cinematic that leads straight into a fight sets `keepMusic` (`c_doom`, `c_gepard`, `c_ambush`, `c_seele`): its theme is not swapped back to the field music when it ends, so the battle continues the same track instead of restarting it. Svarog and Gepard at the front line have no cinematic before the fight, so their scripts start the theme with a `{music}` step on the line where the fight becomes certain (Svarog launches the destruction protocol, Gepard orders the arrest). The test walks every script and checks that such a theme reaches its battle without another music change.

How the tracks were made:

- The main theme was arranged by hand from the stems and spectrograms: piano pulse and melody, strings, violins, horns, basses, a cymbal swell and a bass-drum roll into bar 15. The battle themes were transcribed automatically from source-separated stems (drums, bass, strings, guitars, voice, piano), quantized to a 16th grid (triplet beats use a triplet grid) and cleaned: quiet stem bleed is gated, clashing seconds inside one part are removed, the lead voice is monophonic.
- The four Jarilo-VI themes use a grid of beats rather than bars: each beat has its own division, because *Godfather* switches between triplet shuffle sections and straight sixteenths across two breaks that are not a whole number of bars. Sustained parts get legato (a gap of up to two slots is closed, a re-attack is kept), and every part carries a CC 11 curve taken from the loudness of its stem in sixteenths, so swells, accents and the pumping chords of the originals survive the note transcription. Low impacts in the drum stem that the drum transcriber does not classify (orchestral booms, the four-on-the-floor of *Braving the Cold* bars 10–13) become bass drum booms (key 35). The bass of *Godfather* sits around 33–45 Hz, below what the pitch model tracks, so it was taken from onsets and harmonic sums of the bass stem directly.
- Part levels were set by rendering each channel alone through this synth and matching its level to the level of its stem in the recording; the four new files render at about −25 dB RMS, like *Crises* and *Wildfire*.
- The loop points come from the recordings: *Crises* repeats sample-exact after 64 bars, and *Wildfire* repeats bars 67–78 at bars 111–122. *Order* has no repeat, so it loops from the end back to the bar where the drums enter, after its opening ramp. *Godfather* repeats its first section 154 beats later with a two-beat offset and then ends in a coda and a fade; the loop jumps from the repeat back into the first section (lag 186 beats) and leaves out the coda. *Dawn of Disaster* ends on a final hit, which the loop cuts. *Braving the Cold* closes with the same build that opens it, 62 bars later, so the loop runs from the first full section to the end of the second build.
- The synth follows General MIDI program families, so the files also play in any GM player. CC 74 (brightness) is used by the battle files to open the string and synth filters. Programs 56–59 and 61–63 play the brass section, 39 the sub bass and 118 the falling synth drum; the older files do not use them.
- The old chiptune tracks stay: `title` and `final` for the cinematics and Everwinter Hill, and the fallback when a MIDI file cannot be fetched (for example when the page is opened from `file://`). A fallback of a fight track keeps the noise percussion.
- The credits name HOYO-MiX for the main theme and the battle themes.

## Game design

**Pillars.** Break the enemy, not only its HP bar. Every turn is a Skill Point decision. The world responds to what you say.

**Core verb.** Choose an action on the timeline. Basic ATK gives SP and Skills spend it. Ultimates cut into the timeline at any time. Every hit with a weak element takes Toughness away. A break delays the enemy by 25%, deals break damage and applies the element effect: Bleed, Burn, Freeze, Shock, Wind Shear, Entanglement or Imprisonment. A break also cancels a boss's charge or lock-on.

**Loops.**
- Moment to moment: read the weaknesses and the turn order, spend or save SP, time the Ultimates, break the enemy before it charges.
- Session: explore a map, strike enemies first with F and Techniques, open chests, break crates for Technique Points, rest at a Space Anchor, then beat the story boss.
- Meta: character levels, Traces (ability levels, bonus abilities, stat nodes) paid with Trace materials, Light Cones (Path bonus, superimposition S1–S5), four relic slots with 2-piece and 4-piece set bonuses, Eidolons from duplicates, Warp, Simulator domains and Echoes of War.

**Messages.** The verb is reading and answering. The aesthetic is fellowship: the crew and the people of Jarilo-VI keep talking to you between missions, and the answers let you play the Trailblazer your way (polite, teasing or silent) without changing the plot. How it works:
- *Story threads* arrive when their flag is set (`need`, optionally `lvl` for the Trailblaze Level): March and Herta after the Express introduction, Asta on Jarilo-VI, Himeko and Welt in Belobog, Sampo after he turns up in the city, Pom-Pom at Trailblaze Level 12, Dan Heng in the Underworld, Natasha and Hook after the Moles, Seele after the night at the Grand Hotel, Clara after Rivet Town, and March, Bronya, Gepard, Serval and Pela after the ending. A contact gets the next thread only after the earlier one is read, so a chat never jumps. Contacts from Belobog only write once they have met you, and the threads after the ending follow what the ending tells (the Underworld opens, Bronya's oath, March's photo at the monument).
- *Daily threads* (`daily: true`) arrive one per game day (the day starts at 04:00, as in Events) from the first contact in data order who has no open thread. They are finite, and each pays 5 Stellar Jade (`DAILY_GIVE`) when it ends.
- *Answers.* A reply menu has one to three answers. The answer label is posted as the Trailblazer's message unless the branch starts with its own `@|` lines; the branch can hold more lines, stickers and nested menus, then the thread goes on. Only the picked indexes are saved (`state.chats.th[id] = {at, k, n, picks, done}`), and `chatView` rebuilds the transcript from the script, so text fixes reach old saves.
- *DSL.* A thread is `{ id, who, need, lvl, daily, mail, steps }`. Steps are `'who|text'` (`@` is the Trailblazer, `{он|она}` and `{name}` work as in the story), `{ pick: [[label, ...steps]] }`, `{ sticker, who }`, `{ photo, who }` and `{ quest }`. A test walks every answer path of every thread and checks the text (no dashes, no unfilled forms, at most 170 characters), the speakers, stickers, photos and quests.
- *Herta's invitation* moved from the mail to Messages. `mail: 'herta_test'` on the thread converts old saves: a letter that was already read gives the thread as read, an unread letter arrives as a new message.

**Persona 2 systems.**
- *Contact.* Every non-boss enemy family (Antimatter Legion, Fragmentum, Automatons, Silvermane, Shades, Trotters) reacts to one of eight tags: joke, cute, threat, science, kind, sing, bribe or silent. Each character has two Contact lines with different tags. When a mood reaches 3 points, the result depends on the mood: Eager (the enemy leaves a gift), Happy (it leaves credits and Jade), Scared (it flees) or Angry (it gets +30% ATK and acts sooner). The Archive records the outcomes and hints.
- *Fusion.* Twelve pairs, for example March 7th + Dan Heng, Himeko + Welt, Gepard + Serval, Bronya + Seele, Preservation Trailblazer + Bronya, Yanqing + Bailu and Clara + Hook. When both members have full energy, R spends both Ultimates on one two-element attack. The Archive lists a Fusion after you use it for the first time.
- *Rumors.* Sampo sells rumors in Belobog and in Boulder Town. A rumor you buy comes true: a new item in the shop, a Warp Trotter on the plains, a hidden chest in the Great Mine, kinder Shades, a discount on the Express, the Doomsday Echo or a 5★ Light Cone in the flea market.

**Economy.** Credits come from battles, chests, Contact and the credits Calyx. Shops, rumors and fuel spend them. Stellar Jade comes from chests, the first clear of each boss, Contact, the ending, Trailblazer's Will and the version letters, and Warp spends it (160 each, 4★ pity at 10 and 5★ pity at 60 on each banner). Star Rail Passes come from Pom-Pom (10), the ending and Trailblazer's Will (40); Special Star Rail Passes come from the Comet Gift (10) and only work on the event banner. Trailblaze Power (600, +1 every 6 real seconds, reserve 2400; see «Trailblaze Power») pays for the Simulator. Traces are the main credit sink (see «Traces and Eidolons»). Technique Points (max 5) come from crates, coffee and resting.

**Difficulty curve.** The levels go up in a sawtooth, and each boss is a peak with an Anchor before it:

| Area | Enemy levels | Boss |
|---|---|---|
| Herta Station | 1–6 | Doomsday Beast 8 (with Himeko as a trial character, two waves) |
| Outlying Snow Plains | 8–12 | Gepard 15 at Belobog's gate |
| Backwater Pass | 13–14 | Bronya's squad 14, then Bronya 15 (ends at 1% health) |
| Boulder Town | — | Vagrants 18 (with Seele as a trial character) |
| Great Mine | 15–18 | Automaton Grizzly 19 |
| Rivet Town | 19–23 | Ice Out of Space, the orphanage fight |
| Robot Settlement | 22–24 | Svarog 25 twice (the second fight with the Wildfire buff) |
| Silvermane Guard Restricted Zone | 25–27 | Dunn 26, the Grizzly 27, Gepard and three Fragmentum waves |
| Corridor of Fading Echoes | 28–30 | Guardian Shadow 30, Cocolia's echo 30 |
| Everwinter Hill | 30–32 | three waves with the Engine of Creation, Cocolia 29 (ends at half health), Cocolia, Mother of Deception 31 |
| Future Market (1.4) | 40–44 | IPC team leader 42, the crew against Topaz's IPC squad in three waves 42–44 |

After the first Cocolia fight, the Trailblazer receives the power of Preservation (Fire) from Qlipoth. Bronya joins the final fight as a trial character. The headless playthrough reaches about level 30 before Cocolia.

**Jarilo-VI maps.** Overworld: `j_plains`, `j_belobog` with `j_florist`, `j_workshop`, `j_hotel`, `j_fort`, `j_museum`, `j_cable`, then `j_backwater`, `j_restricted`, `j_fissures`, `j_everwinter`, `j_pillars`; `j_pathspace` is the Path Space of «Возвращение». Underworld: `u_boulder` with `u_clinic`, `u_club`, `u_grandhotel`, `u_mine` and `u_camp`, `u_rivet` and `u_rivet2`, `u_settle` and `u_settle1`; `u_rivet3` is the stealth excursion of «Рынок будущего II». Every map has an official name, its anchors and its area text in `H.WORLDS`. Old saves are moved by `JARILO_STEPS` (`state.jv`): the player is placed on a free tile, done steps of rebuilt missions are marked, opened chests are counted again on the new maps. A map without a `v` spawn mark places a loaded player at its first anchor, then at its first exit.

**Story.** The script follows the order of the original missions and keeps the maps and fights of this game. Prologue: Kafka and Silver Wolf plant the Stellaron (the vessel choice picks Caelus or Stelle), the Trailblazer wakes with March 7th and Dan Heng, Asta sends the team after Arlan, Arlan joins the team for good (a normal roster character you can level and upgrade; while he is in the team his post in the storage zone stays empty), Himeko's drones clear the storage zone, the Doomsday Beast attacks, Nanook's gaze and Welt, Herta's puppet and the invitation (staying on the station shows the joke ending and returns to the choice), the Express with Pom-Pom, Welt and March at the window, the jump to Jarilo-VI. Jarilo-VI: Sampo in the snowdrift, Gepard on the plains, the history of Belobog, the audience with Cocolia, Serval's broken heater, Pela's tour, the Goethe Hotel, the teen Cocolia dream, Bronya's arrest, the escape into the Fragmentum alley and Sampo's smoke, Natasha's clinic, hide-and-seek with the Moles and Hook, the Fight Club and Sampo's confession, Seele saving Bronya from vagrants, Svarog and Clara at the Geomarrow vein, the Grizzly and Oleg, the night at the Goethe Grand Hotel with Cocolia's grief and Bronya's doubts, the orphanage in Rivet Town, Clara and the fight with Svarog, the memory archive, Natasha as the real head of Wildfire, Bronya's letter, Serval hiding the team from Gepard, Gepard at the front line, Cocolia and Bronya's choice, the Engine of Creation, Himeko's orbital cannon, the ice spear, Path Space and Qlipoth's lance, Cocolia's end, Bronya's cover story, the news brought to the Underworld (miners, the Moles or Gertie), Perkins leading to Clara and Svarog, the cable car reopening, Bronya's oath at the monument, the photos with Pela, Hook, Serval and Clara, Sampo on the TV and the Express leaving while Bronya and Seele argue on the fort balcony. After the credits Bronya invites the crew to the Sun Festival: Topaz and the IPC debt, the raid on the Great Mine, the opinions of the people, the stealth bombs in Rivet Town and the Pillars of Creation. Old saves keep their progress: the flags `bronya_met` and `jailed` are renamed to `plains_met` and `fled` on load, and a save that already has `arlan_met` gets Arlan in the roster (`fixStoryJoins`).

**Simulated Universe.** A run is a chain of domains on one Path, and the Path choice decides which blessings come up most.
- *Entry.* Herta's message (thread `herta_test` in Messages, after the Express introduction) starts the quest «Виртуальная вселенная: Первичный тест»: talk to Herta in her office, stand in front of the machine, clear World 1 (the tutorial on Preservation, Qlipoth after the first win, Fuli after the boss), report to Herta. The report follows the original: Herta's puppet joins, the Light Cone «База данных», 50 Jade and 3000 credits.
- *Worlds.* World 1 «Станция «Герта»» (level 8, a random ornament set), World 2 «Снежные поля» (17, «Запечатывающая космос станция», boss Gepard), World 3 «Подземье» (25, «Живой Вонвак», Svarog), World 4 «Холм вечнозимья» (33, «Замершее Салсотто», Cocolia in two waves). A world opens when the previous one is cleared and its boss is beaten in the story.
- *Domains.* Combat (a guard opens the portals, an optional second group pays more), elite (3★ blessing and a curio), event, trade and encounter (14 events), respite (two healing capsules, a Technique orb, Herta's shop; the last respite before the boss also enhances any blessing), boss. HP and energy carry over from fight to fight. Worlds 2–4 have 11 domains, World 1 has 7. Every domain autosaves.
- *Blessings and Paths.* A blessing choice offers three; 45% of the picks come from the run's Path, sealing waxes raise their Path. Three blessings of the run's Path open the Path Resonance. Aeon meetings open new Paths for later runs: Aha in World 1, Lan and Yaoshi in World 2, Nanook in World 3, IX in World 4.
- *Fragments.* Cosmic Fragments are the run's currency: 50 at the start, 30 per battle, 60 per elite, 15 per data crystal (F). They buy blessings (80), curios (120), two enhancements (180), revivals (80) and enhancements by rarity (100/130/160). Nothing carries over after the run.
- *Rewards.* A first clear pays 3 × 60 Jade, 3 × 2000 credits and two ornament sets (a sphere and a rope each). A repeat pays 60 Jade, 2000 credits and one set for 40 Trailblaze Power; with less power the reward waits at the machine (up to three). A lost battle ends a free run without a reward.
- *Balance.* The bot test picks blessings at random and has no gear. Enemy ATK carries the danger (`atk`, `bossAtk` per world, `eliteAtk` in `TUN`); HP multipliers only make fights longer. At the level where a world opens the bot wins World 1 always, World 2 about 3 runs of 4 at level 13, Worlds 3 and 4 about 9 of 10; four levels higher it wins every run.

**Planar Ornaments.** Two more relic slots, «Планарная сфера» and «Соединительная вязь», with their own main stats (elemental DMG on the sphere, Break Effect and Energy Regeneration on the rope). A set needs both pieces: «Запечатывающая космос станция» (ATK +12%, another +12% at 120 SPD), «Живой Вонвак» (Energy Regeneration +5%, at 120 SPD the character acts 40% sooner at the start of battle) and «Замершее Салсотто» (CRIT Rate +8%, at 50% CRIT Rate the Ultimate and follow-ups deal +15%). Only the Simulated Universe gives them; the cavern drops never roll them.

**Section 6 (version 1.3).** Two rank S agents from Zenless Zone Zero, sold only on the Signal Search.
- *Хосими Мияби* (Hunt, Ice). Fallen Frost («Изморозь», 0 to 6): Basic «Кадзахана» +1, Skill «Хисэцу» +2, Ultimate «Последний снег» +3 and Ice DMG +30%. While the Basic is picked, ↑/↓ sets the Shimotsuki charge level (each level spends 2): «Симоцуки» 1 to 3 is an enhanced Basic (single 2.4, blast 3.2/1.2, blast 4.4/1.8, more Toughness) and still gives SP. Her ice hits stack Icefire («Ледяное пламя») on a foe; three stacks give Frostbite («Обморожение»): a burst («Прорыв ледяного ожога»), Frostburn («Ледяной ожог», Toughness damage taken +30% for 2 turns, Shimotsuki +25% against it) and 1 Fallen Frost once per action. «Мороз и вьюга»: Shimotsuki +30% with Yanagi or a Harmony/Nihility ally.
- *Цукисиро Янаги* (Nihility, Lightning). Stances Jougen (Lightning DMG +15%) and Kagen (RES PEN +12%) switch on her Skill and Ultimate; the old stance lingers until her next turn. Skill «Гекка рутэн»: blast, Polarity Disorder («Сбитая полярность», 30% of the pending anomaly damage without removing it), Shock, Shinrabanshou (the next two Basics hit as a blast) and Lunar Eclipse (team Disorder damage +40% for 2 turns). Ultimate «Райэй тэнгэ»: all enemies, Polarity ×1.6. While she is alive in the team, Disorder («Хаос») works: a new anomaly kind on a foe that already has another one bursts the old ones for 1.2× their pending damage and gives Miyabi 2 Fallen Frost. Freeze is never consumed. The Fusion «Секция 6» bursts every anomaly and fills Fallen Frost.
- *Anomalies* are the existing DoTs, Freeze and Frostbite (`Battle.anomalies`); without Yanagi nothing changes for other characters. Knobs: `frostMax`, `icefireMax`, `frostbiteDmg`, `frostburnBurst`, `frostburnTough`, `shimoVsBurn`, `chaosMult`, `polarityMult`. Bot check (level 30, March and Natasha): Miyabi with Pela is close to Yanqing, the pair is about 10% faster than Miyabi with Pela, and Yanagi shines against bosses weak to Lightning.
- *Warp tabs* are icons now (event hero portrait, tape, Star Rail Pass; Q/E keycaps at both ends, the plate names the active tab): «Прыжок события» holds two channels, hero and Light Cone (`ev`/`cone`, toggle under the banner title or Tab), like Signal Search. History merges the channels of a tab.
- *Signal Search.* A Warp tab with its own layout: channels on the left (Tab), a stack of CRTs, rules and the agent picker on the right. Channels: «Выделенный канал» (`BANNERS.sig`, agents, 50/50 with a guarantee, `sigFeatured`, rank A Dan Heng, Arlan and Sampo) and «Канал амплификаторов» (`BANNERS.amp`, the signature cones «Сто процентов дыни» and «Луна, что светит звёздам», 75/25 like the cone banner). Ticket «Шифрокопия» (`tape`), else 160 jade. Own pity per channel (`pitySig`, `pityAmp`), log entries `ban: 'sig'|'amp'`, ranks shown as S/A/B. Faucets: the 1.3 letter (10) and the two missions (5 each).
- *F8 «Сцены»* has a fifth mode «Поиск сигнала: появление».
- *Art keys* (gated, other characters unchanged): `foxears`, `tie`, `back: 'hime'` (hair to the knees), `sheath` (katana at the hip) for Miyabi; `shcape` (teal cape under the left pauldron), `naginata` (shaft behind the back, blade down from the right hand), `waistcorset` for Yanagi. The `blunt` bangs got arc points on top, so no strip of forehead shows between the hair cap and the bangs.

**Convene (version 1.3).** Two 5★ guests from Wuthering Waves, sold only on the «Призыв» Warp tab. They were merged into 1.3 and have no story missions.
- *Фролова* (`remembrance`, the new Path «Память»; Quantum). She has no energy: her Ultimate needs 6 notes. Basic «Движение жизни и смерти» writes a String note. Skill «Шёпот мимолётного сна» (blast) writes a Wind note and sets Reincarnate, so the next Basic is the enhanced «Движение судьбы и конца» (1.8×, delay). Any ally Ultimate turns her next note into a Cadenza. The queue keeps 6 notes; an overflow drops the oldest note that is not a Cadenza. Ultimate «Вальс покинутых глубин» (all foes) puts Геката on the action rail as a separate untargetable unit (`Battle.summons`, SPD `hecateSpd`) and gives Phrolova «Маэстро» (ATK +60%). While Hecate is out, the Basic is «Знак Гекате», a cue that makes Hecate act at once. Hecate plays two notes per turn (Strings 1.8× on the foe with the most HP with a delay, Winds 0.9× on all, Cadenza 1.1× on all), then «Финальный поклон» (1.2× on all, +3% per Aftersound stack) and leaves; the next ally on the rail gets «Неоконченная пьеса» (DMG +25% for one turn). Every note adds «Послезвучие» (0 to 24 stacks, crit DMG +2.5% each, 10 at the start). Hecate leaves when Phrolova falls. Technique: two Cadenzas.
- *Скар* (Destruction, Fire, invented kit). Skill «Элизиум» seals the target with 2 seals for 3 rounds. A sealed foe attacks only Scar, Scar takes 20% less damage while his Elysium stands, and his hits add embers (up to 6). A seal breaks when a different ally hits the foe (Scar does not count; Hecate counts as her own side). When all seals are broken, «Разрыв Элизиума» hits for 1.5× plus 0.25× per ember (adjacent foes take half), gives 10 energy and advances Scar by 50%. Ultimate «Аберрантный кошмар» turns him into the goat reaper for 2 turns: Quantum element, Basic «Коса жнеца» (blast), Skill «Элизиум кошмара» (all foes, 1 seal), every burst heals 8% max HP. When the form ends it costs 15% of his current HP and never kills. Technique: the first foe starts sealed with 2 embers.
- *Fusion «Диссонанс»* (Phrolova + Scar): Quantum and Fire 1.6×, Hecate plays every written note at once, and every foe gets a full Elysium that bursts right away.
- *Light Cones*: signature «Место у окна» (Память; Hecate's notes give DMG +32% and DEF ignore 8% for 2 turns) and «Сказка о чёрном ягнёнке» (Destruction; bursts +30% DMG, ATK +24% after a burst), 4★ «Больше пота, меньше слёз» (Память).
- *Banner* «Призыв» (the tab after Signal Search): channels «Призыв резонатора» (`BANNERS.conv`; the 5★ rate and pity of the other banners, 1.2% and 60; the chosen guest 50% with a guarantee shared by both guests, `convFeatured`; featured 4★ 50%: Serval, Natasha and Herta with Phrolova, Sampo, Hook and Arlan with Scar) and «Призыв оружия» (`BANNERS.forge`; pity 50, the 5★ is always the chosen signature cone, `forgeFeatured`, featured 4★ 50%). Tickets «Сияющий прилив» (`rtide`) and «Кующий прилив» (`ftide`), else 160 jade. Each channel has its own pity (`pityConv`, `pityForge`); the per-banner state keys live in `BANNER_STATE` in `hsr-game`. Faucet: the 1.3 letter (10 Radiant and 5 Forging Tides).
- *Screen*: a tide gauge shows the pity, a moon phase shows the guarantee, the stage follows the guest (opera hall with lycoris for Phrolova, an Elysium hex dome over a night village for Scar, a forge for the weapon channel); Tab switches the channels.
- *Scene*: `hsr-convene` (see Modules). Every reveal and cut takes the look through `G.lookOf`.
- *F8 «Сцены»* has a sixth mode «Призыв: появление».
- *Art*: the looks of Phrolova and Scar in `hsr-data` are placeholders and wait for the character redesign (one character per step with the user's approval). Hecate and the Aberrant Nightmare are foe art in `hsr-foes` (`hecate`, `scar_nightmare`).

**Stellaron Hunters (event banner).** Kafka, Silver Wolf and Blade join the event banner next to Ruan Mei and Seele (`BANNERS.ev.stars` and `BANNERS.cone.stars`, faction `hunters`). They drop only there. Each adds one verb that the other characters do not have.
- *Кафка* (Nihility, Lightning). Verb: turn the allies' Basic ATKs into damage. Talent «Жестокая нежность»: after an ally's Basic ATK or enhanced Basic ATK, Kafka follows up on that foe (1.4×) and applies Shock, once until her next turn (`flags.trill`). Skill «Ласковый лунный свет» (blast) makes every DoT on the main target deal 75% of its tick at once (detonation, the DoT keeps its turns). Ultimate «Сумеречная трель» hits all foes, applies Shock and detonates every DoT at 100%. Technique: all foes take damage and Shock. Teams with Sampo, Hook, Serval or Yanagi feed the detonations; her own Basic ATK never triggers the talent.
- *Серебряный Волк* (Nihility, Quantum). Verb: rewrite a weakness. Skill «Разрешить изменения?» implants the element of a living ally into the target for 3 enemy turns (`implant`, the weakness list is rebuilt from `ENEMIES` data, so the data is never changed) and lowers all RES by 13% (`resDown`). While the Skill is picked, ↑/↓ chooses the element; the default is the strongest ally's element the foe is not weak to. A natural weakness is not implanted twice. Only one implant per foe. Talent: every Basic ATK, Skill or Ultimate has a 65% base chance to plant a Bug on the main target (ATK −10%, DEF −8% or SPD −6%, 3 turns; slowed foes also feed Welt's talent). Ultimate «Пользователь забанен»: 3.8× and DEF −45%. Technique: all foes take Quantum damage and Toughness damage regardless of weakness.
- *Блэйд* (Destruction, Wind). Verb: spend HP. Skill «Адский пейзаж» costs 30% max HP and does not end the turn (`keepTurn`): for 3 turns DMG +40%, the Basic ATK becomes «Частокол мечей» (blast, 0.4× ATK + 1.0× max HP, costs 10% max HP) and the Skill is locked. Taking a hit or spending HP gives a charge of «Дар Шуху» (one per enemy action); at 5 charges he hits all foes (0.44× ATK + 1.1× max HP) and heals 25% max HP. The HP tally counts every HP he lost in battle (cap 90% max HP). Ultimate «Смертный приговор» sets HP to 50% (spending the excess adds to the tally), hits target and neighbours for ATK, max HP and the tally, then resets the tally. Spending HP never drops him below 1 HP.
- *Fusions*: «По сценарию Элио» (Kafka + Silver Wolf: Lightning weakness on every foe, Shock, detonation) and «Шёпот против мары» (Kafka + Blade: Death Sentence on a blast, detonation, full Shuhu charges, so the follow-up and its heal fire at once).
- *Light Cones* (cone channel): «Просто надо подождать» (Kafka; DMG +24%, SPD +4.8% per attack up to 3, hits plant Erode «Паутинка», a Lightning DoT of 60% ATK), «Непрекращающийся дождь» (Silver Wolf; EHR +24%, CRIT +12% against foes with 3 debuffs, a 65% chance to plant «Эфирный код», damage taken +12%), «Недоступная сторона» (Blade; CRIT +18%, max HP +18%, DMG +24% after a hit or HP spending until the next attack).
- *Banners*: «Пускай никто не спит» (4★ Serval, Sampo, Hook), «Контракт Зеро» (4★ Pela, Asta, March 7th), «Неприкаянная душа» (4★ Natasha, Arlan, Herta). The hero picker turns compact for more than three heroes. Backdrops: an opera hall with puppet threads, a cyberspace grid with hack windows, a sword graveyard under a red moon.
- *Knobs* in `TUNING`: `kafkaFollow`, `kafkaShock`, `kafkaDetSkill`, `kafkaDetUlt`, `implantTurns`, `bugChance`, `bugTurns`, `bugAtk`, `bugDef`, `bugSpd`, `shuhuMax`, `hellCost`, `hellDmg`, `hellTurns`, `forestCost`, `tallyCap`, `shuhuAtk`, `shuhuHp`, `shuhuHeal`. Bot check at level 30 (with March 7th and Natasha): Kafka sits between Himeko and Seele and is fastest with Sampo; Silver Wolf makes Seele about 15% faster than Pela does; Blade is close to Clara and Seele and needs a healer against Cocolia.
- *UI*: Blade's charges are pips on his card, «Ад N» shows the Hellscape turns, the Skill button is disabled inside Hellscape; the implant picker sits above the cast plate; an implanted weakness icon gets a pulsing frame. Ability texts carry `text` where the effect is not a plain damage line.
- *Story looks*: `SPEAKERS.kafka`, `SPEAKERS.silverwolf` and `SPEAKERS.blade` point to the roster looks, so the prologue scenes use the same art.
- *Art keys* for Blade (gated): `dangleR` (earring on the right), `frogknot` (Chinese knot with two red buttons on the vest, bust and figure), `armwrap` (bandage on the upper arm, viewer's left), `handwrap` (bandaged hand over the glove, viewer's left). He also uses `mandarin`, `goldcuff`, `kneebandage`, `greaves`, coat `split` without `backLining`, `crown: 'messy'`, `bangs: 'hide'`.
- *Information pages* come from the RU wiki «/Лор» pages. Blade's story parts that reveal Dan Heng's past are left out, and so are lines about characters and places that are not in the game.

**Version 1.4: nine 4★ from the original.** Рысь (Abundance, Quantum: Survival Response raises max HP and pulls enemy fire onto a Destruction or Preservation ally, HoT), Лука (Nihility, Physical: Fighting Will, Sky-Shatter Fist with 3 to 6 jabs and an uppercut that detonates Bleed), Сушан (Hunt, Physical: Sword Stance on broken foes, Dawn Herald acts again at once), Сюэи (Destruction, Quantum: Karma from Toughness damage, 3-hit follow-up, Divine Castigation hits any Toughness; +40% Break Effect turned into DMG), Тинъюнь (Harmony, Lightning: Benediction, 50 Energy ult), Ханья (Harmony, Physical: Burden returns a Skill Point every 2 hits, twice), Цинцюэ (Erudition, Quantum: jade tiles of three suits, the Skill keeps the turn, four of a suit opens the Hidden Hand), Юйкун (Harmony, Imaginary: Roaring Bowstrings, 2 stacks that fall off with ally turns; Diving Kestrel CRIT while strung), Гуйнайфэнь (Nihility, Fire: any Burn tick plants stacking Firekiss, the ult bursts every Burn). They are in the standard 4★ pool, in Signal Search and in the event trios (Ruan Mei: Xueyi, Sushang, Asta; Seele: Hanya, Yukong, Pela; Kafka: Luka, Guinaifen, Sampo; Silver Wolf: Qingque, Tingyun, March 7th; Blade: Lynx, Arlan, Herta). Fusions: «Ландау в сборе» (Lynx, Gepard, Serval), «Урок бокса» (Luka, Hook), «Под аплодисменты» (Sushang, Guinaifen), «Сёстры-судьи» (Hanya, Xueyi), «Небесный флот» (Yukong, Tingyun), «Ставка на представление» (Qingque, Guinaifen). Letter `v14_fours`, site news `v14` (it also announces the Herta Station rework), title «CD-ROM v1.4». Every one has a Warp intro, a Signal Search TV channel (broadcast, tape, private moment), an Ultimate, an Eidolon picture and an information page. A side fix in the engine: an advance earned by an Ultimate used in the hero's own turn now waits for the end of that turn, and advances add up. Art: Lynx uses the new keys `cathood` (white hood with cat ears, bust, figure, sprite) and `capelet` (short cape with fur hem, `capeletFur`); Luka uses the coat `openvest` (open sleeveless vest over the `inner` tank top, standing collar, bare arms) and the keys `mecharm` (mechanical arm on the viewer's left with a gold pad `padCol` and `lava` cracks), `lavatee` (dark lava print on the tank top), `shinL` and `legR` (asymmetric trouser legs); Сюэи adds `charmpanel` (long navy talisman panel with crimson and gold diamonds, figure and sprite) and `onimask` (red demon mask on the belt); Гуйнайфэнь uses back `side` with the flag `sideR`, which mirrors the side ponytail and `hairflower` to the right (bust, figure, front sprite), plus her own keys `lotus` (red flower with gold centre, white feather and tassel cords), `cloudbust` (halter straps, teal edging, gold cloud ornament), `petalcuff` (red shoulder caps with white petals), `ropecoil`, `hipbow` (bow with a gold disc), `cloudhem` (teal strip, gold frogs, cut-outs and cloud hem), `thighswirl`, `anklegold`, the `tailsGold` field (gold line and white edge on `tails`) and `neck: 'none'` (no collar under `bodice` in the bust); Ханья got her own keys too: `sweephorns` (black crescent horns with red inner faces), `jadecollar` (gold collar, jade pendant, sheer upper chest), `knotbar` (strap with gold knots under the bust), `armorpad` (black and gold pauldrons with a jade stone over navy drapes), `lotussash` (blue sash bow with a gold lotus yin-yang medallion), `sealpanel` (navy front panel with gold diamonds and a red seal), `hipswirl`, `thightattoo`, `brushpen` (gold brush at the hip) and `tasselanklet`; the others are built from existing keys.

**Investigation Team (Persona 4 collab).** Stages 1 and 2 of the collab plan: the kits, battle verbs, Fusions and signature Light Cones exist in `hsr-inaba` and the characters are in `CHARS`; no banner sells them yet, and the banner, art and scenes follow in later stages. Ultimate and Fusion cutscenes and Eidolon pictures exist (`hsr-inaba-scenes`); until their information pages exist, the test skips the six ids in `P4_LATER` only in the information-page check. The game already falls back when this content is missing (no cutscene, no picture, no page). Names use the fan Russian spelling (Ю Наруками, Ёсуке Ханамура, Чие Сатонака, Юкико Амаги, Канджи Тацуми, Рисе Куджикава). Faction `inaba`; every kit has an `arcana`.
- *«Ещё раз!»*. When a faction member breaks a foe's Toughness with its own Basic ATK, enhanced Basic or Skill, it acts again at once (event `oneMore`, then `turn` with `keep` and `oneMore`). The action still gives SP and energy. The extra action cannot give another «Ещё раз!»; Ultimates, follow-ups and DoTs never give it. While Yu is alive, one other ally may also get it, once until Yu's next turn (`flags.bond`).
- *All-Out Attack* («Атака всем отрядом»). During «Ещё раз!», when every living foe is broken (`aoaReady`), `allOut()` spends the extra action: every living ally hits every foe for `aoaAtk` ATK in its own element, +`aoaYu` with Yu in the team, no Toughness. Events `aoa` and `aoaEnd`; the turn ends with Basic ATK energy and no SP. Auto battle always takes it.
- *Ю* (Hunt, Lightning, Шут). Three Personas on the Skill mode list (↑/↓ uses `setSkill`): Изанаги «Зиодайн» (2.2 + DEF −15%), Джек Фрост «Буфула» (blast 1.3/0.6, Freeze 35%), Ёсицунэ «Хассо Тоби» (6 bounces of 0.45). The Persona sets Yu's element for the Basic ATK too and is locked once he acts in a turn (`flags.personaLock`). The first break with each Persona in a battle gives `wildSp` SP. Ultimate «Мириады истин»: 1.6 on all, Toughness without a weakness, the next Skill is free (`freeSkill`). Technique: first Skill free and ATK +15%.
- *Канджи* (Destruction, Physical, Император). Skill «Стежок» sews the target to its neighbour with the most HP for `stitchTurns` of Kanji's turns (one pair at a time, `flags.stitch`); every ally hit on one of them carries `stitchDmg` of the damage and `stitchTough` of the Toughness (regardless of weakness) to the other, Kanji's Ultimate «Первобытная сила» carries all of it. Talent: +`kanjiVsStitch` DMG on stitched foes, `kanjiStitchEnergy` energy when a stitched foe breaks. A lone foe cannot be stitched. Technique: a shield on the most wounded ally.
- *Рисе* (Harmony, Imaginary, Влюблённые). Skill «Полный анализ» (no damage): «Проанализирован» for 3 turns: Toughness damage taken +`analyzeTough`, CRIT Rate against it +`analyzeCrit`, damage taken +`analyzeVuln`, and its next action is rolled in advance (`planNext` → `u.planned`, event `intent`; the enemy then uses exactly that action). Talent: before an analysed foe hits everyone, fires a charged attack or a lock-on, Rise shields the targets for `riseWarn` of their max HP (event `warn`). Ultimate «Третий глаз»: analyses every foe for 2 turns, team DMG +24%, SPD +10%, healing 6% per turn. Technique: +10 energy and a scan of what the foe families like in Contact (`b.scan`, event `scan`).
- *Ёсуке* (Nihility, Wind, Маг). Skill «Гарула» (blast, SPD −12%, Wind Shear 60%), Ultimate «Гарудайн» (all, Wind Shear, delay 15%). Talent «Атака Ёсуке»: after an ally break, a follow-up of `yosukeStrike` with a sure crit on another unbroken foe, once until his next turn.
- *Чие* (Destruction, Ice, Колесница). CRIT Rate +`chieCrit`; her crits hit Toughness without a weakness for `chieCritTough` of the usual amount. Ultimate «Галактический пинок» (3.2) knocks out a common foe left under `chieExecute` HP.
- *Юкико* (Abundance, Fire, Жрица). Skill «Диарама» (heal + cleanse), Ultimate «Пламя Коноханы» (team heal, Fire on all, Burn). Talent: every Burn tick on a foe, from anyone, heals the most wounded ally for `yukikoBurnHeal` of her max HP.
- *Fusions*: «Напарники» (Ю + Ёсуке: Lightning and Wind on any Toughness, then the All-Out Attack if every foe is broken, fx `allOut` → `pendingAoa`), «Подруги детства» (Чие + Юкико: Chie's hit always crits, so it hits Toughness without a weakness, Yukiko heals the team), «Император на связи» (Канджи + Рисе: analyses every foe, stitches the target, 3.0 Physical), «Я есть ты» (Trailblazer + Ю, two ids `f_fools`/`f_fools_p` for both Trailblazer Paths: two hits, Yu's on any Toughness, +10 team energy).
- *Signature Light Cones*: «Самый вкусный омлет» (Yu, Hunt; CRIT DMG +32%, DMG +24% during «Ещё раз!», his All-Out Attack share +30%), «Стежок за стежком» (Kanji, Destruction; ATK +24%, DMG +20% against stitched foes, a stitched foe's break heals 8% HP), «Просто Рисе» (Rise, Harmony; Energy Regeneration +10%, every analysis gives the team DMG +16% for 2 turns). Effects live in `inabaDmg`, `inabaBreak` and `analyze`; art scenes `truth`, `thread`, `affection` in `hsr-cones`.
- *4★ Light Cones* (`hsr-inaba` CONES, `sig` = the 4★ hero, so the Midnight card shows that hero's arcana): «Каждый день в «Джунс»» `junesday` (Yosuke, Nihility; Effect Hit Rate +16%, follow-up DMG +24%), «Испытание дракона» `dragontrial` (Chie, Destruction; ATK +16%, DMG vs broken foes +24%), «Гостиница «Амаги»» `amagiinn` (Yukiko, Abundance; Outgoing Healing +12%, DoT DMG +24%). Effects live in `inabaDmg` and scale with Superimposition through `cf`. They are the featured 4★ of every Compendium banner (`comp` four, 75% like other cone banners, miss sets the 4★ guarantee) and stay out of the standard pool. Scenes in `hsr-cones`; shot `conebig_<id>-<id>` draws chosen cones large.
- *Izanagi* was redrawn from the P4 model render: long black coat with red lining and grey chest stripes, a row of white buckles down the right front, silver crotch plate, silver helmet with two horns, a dark visor and yellow eyes, a long white ribbon arcing from the head, thin legs with silver blade feet, the long serrated sword held low across the body (`swing` turns it from the hip).
- *Jiraiya* was redrawn from the P4 model render: slim white jumpsuit with ribbing, bell-bottom legs with camo cuffs and black-tipped shoes, the big gold chevron collar with white grin teeth over a red scarf, long arms with camo gloves and spinning gold shuriken, a black round head with two ball antennae (red slits) and gold shuriken eyes.
- *Tomoe* was redrawn from the P4 model render: tall white cone helmet with a dark visor, blue eyes and a black emblem, long dark hair flowing back, slim yellow bodysuit with a black centre line, segmented silver hip armour over black flaps, white lace-up boots, the gold naginata with glowing orange blades at both ends.
- *Konohana Sakuya* was redrawn from the P4 model render: red bird mask with a beak, a dark visor and a flower, red bodysuit with a pale neckline, white pleated skirt, dark tights and long red pointed boots, two huge wings of red-pink feathers with pale tips and gold veins and a red flower at each wing root.
- *Take-Mikazuchi* was redrawn from the P4 render: a rounded black robot with white bones painted on the chest, arms and legs, round shoulder pods, red and blue wires at the elbow, white mitten fists, a small skull head on a short neck and the golden zigzag bolt.
- *Himiko* was redrawn from the P4 render: long cream satin gown with a train, black body and long black arms, a gold cup in one hand, the gold antenna staff across the body, white ribbons and a gold crown on the dark head, and the tilted white parasol dish with red spokes on gold struts.
- *Information pages* (`hsr-info`) come from the English megamitensei wiki (there is no Russian Persona wiki): profile, design, personality and the P4 story up to the first rescues, without the culprit or the endings; voice lines are the wiki's quotes from P4 The Animation, Persona Q and P4 Arena. All six are done.
- *Looks*: redesigned one by one from the megamitensei wiki renders (scratch `imgs.py` lists a page's files and gets their URLs through `api.php`). Ю: silver bowl cut with a blunt fringe, grey eyes, the open black Yasogami jacket to the hips with dark stitching, white shirt, gold buttons, black trousers, brown loafers. Ёсуке: light brown messy hair, the closed black Yasogami jacket with gold buttons and a red collar at the neck, orange headphones, white sneakers. Чие: brown bob, the zipped green track jacket with lime stripes, grey pleated skirt, white socks, black loafers. Юкико: long black hair with a red headband (new art key `headband`, an arc over the hair cap drawn with the face, so bust and figure both get it), red cardigan over the black sailor collar, yellow scarf, grey pleated skirt, black tights (`legs`), brown loafers. Канджи: platinum slicked-back hair, the black jacket worn open over a black T-shirt with a white skull (new art key `skullprint`, bust and figure), piercings, brown shoes. Рисе: auburn twin tails (new art key `twintails`: two side tails behind the head in bust and figure, two columns in the field sprite), black blazer with the sailor collar and yellow scarf, grey pleated skirt, dark brown thigh-highs, black loafers. All six are redesigned.
- *Banner «Полуночный канал»* (Warp tab before the standard one, ticket «Бархатный ключ» `vkey`, else 160 Jade). Channel `mid`: Yu «Дикая карта» (arcana 0 Шут), Kanji «Мягкое сердце» (IV Император), Rise «Настоящая я» (VI Влюблённые); the 5★ is 50/50 between the chosen hero and the standard 5★ characters with a guarantee after a loss (`midFeatured`, the other two Persona heroes never come as a loss), the 4★ is 50% Yosuke, Chie or Yukiko. Channel `comp` «Компендиум»: the signature cones «Самый вкусный омлет», «Стежок за стежком», «Просто Рисе», 75/25 and the cone pity like the cone banner. Each channel has its own pity (`pityMid`, `pityComp`) and counter (`warpNMid`, `warpNComp`); the history tab shows both. Kazuma's 1% 4★ cameo works here as on every banner except the Guild. Until the gacha scene exists, pulls play the usual Warp show.
- *Screen*: the forecast calendar shows the days left to the 5★ pity («дней до тумана», weather from clear to fog), the fog over the street rises with the pity, Teddie's glasses light up on a guarantee; ↑/↓ or a click on the tarot cards picks the hero, Tab switches to the Compendium. The first pull opens Igor's contract («Я действую по своей воле…»); signing gives `contractKeys` keys.
- *Scene*: `hsr-midnight` (see Modules); the arcana of a card comes from `H.VELVET.arcanaOf(charId, coneId)`: the kit's `arcana`, else `ARCANA_OF` for the HSR roster (March 7th XVII Звезда, Dan Heng IX, Himeko III, Welt V, Natasha XIV, Serval XIX, Asta X, Arlan VIII, Herta I, Gepard XI, Bronya XX, Seele XIII, Sampo XV, Hook XVI, Pela II, Ruan Mei XXI, Kafka XVIII, Silver Wolf XII, the Trailblazer 0 like Yu), a signature cone takes its owner's card, other cones go by Path. F8 «Сцены» has the mode «Полуночный канал: появление».
- *Own cuts* (`CUTS` in `hsr-midnight`, after the card flip; then the plate and the line from `QUOTES`): Ю: fog (Teddie's glasses slide on, the fog thins), shades (masked Shadows close in on the studio target), voice (the Fool card descends, «Я есть ты… Ты есть я…»), crush («Персона!», blue flame, Izanagi rises), zio (lightning from Izanagi bursts the Shadows), pose (Izanagi behind Yu, the line «Правда где-то в этом тумане. Я её найду.»). Ёсуке: bike (he rides in and crashes into a trash can, «Помогите…»), junes (the Junes jingle, his Shadow in the shop window: «Тебе здесь просто скучно.»), shadow («Ты не я!», then «Ладно. Ты это я. Я это ты.», the Shadow fades into green light), jiraiya (Jiraiya rises in a Garu whirlwind that blows the trash can away), pose (the line «Партнёр, прикрою! Только про мусорку никому.»). Persona art: `jiraiya` (white jumpsuit, camo cuffs, gold grin chest plate, red scarf, black frog head with spinning shuriken eyes, shuriken in the palms). Чие: kick (training kick at a post on the riverbank at sunset, «Хья-а-а!»), steak (the Aiya mega beef bowl, star eyes, «Мясо! Вот где сила!»), shadow («Тебе нужна слабая Юкико.», «Неправда!», then «Правда. И это тоже я.», the Shadow melts into ice light), tomoe (Tomoe rises, the kick raises a row of Bufu ice spikes, «Томоэ! Буфу!»), pose (the line «Сначала драка, потом говядина!»). Persona art: `tomoe` (yellow jumpsuit with black side stripes, red sash, black helmet with a yellow band and a red plume, a naginata held across the body). Юкико: inn (the Amagi Inn at night, lanterns, a polite bow, «Добро пожаловать в гостиницу «Амаги».»), laugh (her laughing fit: closed eyes, open mouth, blush, «Пф… Ха-ха!»), cage (her Shadow in a golden birdcage: «Увезите меня отсюда!», «Я не такая!», then «Я хотела уйти. Это тоже я.», the bars fly apart), sakuya (Konohana Sakuya rises from Agi flames among petals), pose (the line «Куда мне идти, я теперь выберу сама.»). Persona art: `sakuya` (petal wings, red layered gown, crimson hair, flower crown). Канджи: gang (night riverbank, folding chair on the shoulder, «Кто тут шумит по ночам?!», the bikers run), shop (Tatsumi Textiles, he sews a knitted bunny, «Ровнее стежок. Вот так.»), shadow (his Shadow holds the bunny: «Ты боишься, что тебя засмеют.», «Заткнись!», then «Боюсь. И всё равно шью.»), mikazuchi (Take-Mikazuchi slams its golden thunderbolt, the ground cracks), stitch (a thread pulls two Shadows together and one bolt hits both, the battle verb), pose (the bunny on his shoulder, the line «Плевать, что скажут. Я такой, какой есть.»). Persona art: `mikazuchi` (black armour with a painted white skeleton, skull face with yellow eyes, horned helmet, bone-white fists, the golden zigzag bolt). Рисе: stage (Risette on stage with spotlights and glowsticks, «Всем привет! Это Рисетт!»), tofu (Marukyu Tofu, glasses as a disguise, «Тофу свежий. Только не узнавайте меня.»), shadow (her Shadow under a gold spotlight: «Смотрите! Какая я настоящая?», «Ты не я!», then «Я и Рисетт, и Рисе. Обе.»), himiko (Himiko rises with scan rings, «Химико! Начинаю анализ.»), scan (the battle verb: the Full Analysis panel with weaknesses and the next enemy action, «ОСТОРОЖНО!», shield rings), pose (hearts and scan rings, the line «Я на связи. Всегда, со всеми вами.»). Persona art: `himiko` (white gown with gold bands, pink visor, satellite dish head with an antenna). All six cuts are done. Look variants for cuts go through `vlook(look, { closed, mouth, blush })` (cached per look).
- *Faucets*: the 1.3 letter (10 keys), the contract (1), the rumor «Полуночный канал» from Sampo in Belobog (1200 credits): the broadcast screen in the Общий вагон shows the Midnight Channel once per game day (04:00 reset) and drops one key, `midnightKeys` (10) in all; Pom-Pom comments the first time.
- *Battle UI*: see `hsr-ui-inaba` in Modules. Quiet analysis debuffs («Анализ: …») stay out of the enemy tag line; ally card tags are cut to fit next to the HP number. Shots: `p4persona`, `p4stitch`, `p4onemore`, `p4aoaask`, `p4aoa` (`hsrShots.p4Battle(mode)`).
- *Engine changes for everyone*: enemy skill choice moved into `pickSkill(u)` (same RNG order), hits accept `raw` damage (no formula, no crit roll), `critTaken` on a foe adds to the attacker's CRIT Rate, `skillCost` honours `freeSkill`, unknown Technique and fx kinds fall through to the Inaba mixin.
- *Official site and Clippy*: the 1.3 news on hsr.hoyoverse.com (`js/apps/hsr-site.js`) has seven paragraphs on the Midnight Channel (heroes, keys and rules, cone channel, scene, «Ещё раз!»/All-Out Attack, Fusions, rumor) and the 10 keys in the letter; `source()` names the banner; `memberName()` shows `trail_p` as «Первопроходец на Пути Сохранения» so the two «Я есть ты» Fusions differ. Clippy (`hsrSiteReaction` CHAR_LINES in `js/apps/clippy.js`) has a line for each of the six; the roster line counts `site.characterIds()` and lists every Abundance healer. `tests/hsr-site.test.js` checks the notes against TUNING, the letter, cones and Fusions.
- *Bot check* (scratch `bal.cjs`, level 30, with Pela, March 7th and Natasha unless named; battle length in unit turns): Yu is close to Himeko (Seele is faster on bosses, Yu is fastest on mobs), Kanji is close to Clara on Svarog and Gepard and much faster on mobs, Chie beats Arlan on Svarog but suffers against Ice-resistant Gepard and Cocolia, Yosuke and Pela trade places by fight, Yukiko is close to Natasha, Rise with Seele is close to Ruan Mei except against Cocolia. «Ещё раз!» comes 1–5 times and the All-Out Attack 0.5–2 times per fight with Investigation Team members.

**Forgotten Hall (Зал забвения).** The Garden of Recollection copies the Trailblazer's memories and pays in items and Light Cones. Only the permanent series «Наследие Возвышающейся цитадели» exists for now; Memory of Chaos and the Luofu series are left for later. Sources: fandom `Forgotten Hall`, `Forgotten Hall/Stages`, `Forgotten Hall/Rewards`, `Messenger` (EN) and `Посланник`, `Мимолётные огни` (RU); the per-stage turbulence comes from a Jarilo-VI Memory guide because fandom does not list it.
- *Access.* The mirror in the Общий вагон, after «Мимолётные огни». The Messenger's menu: «Войти в Зал забвения», «Войти в „Бесценные ценности“», questions (official RU lines), goodbye.
- *Structure (original).* Stages 1–5: one node, one battle in two waves, 20 cycles, stars at 10 and 14 cycles left. Stages 6–10: one node, two one-wave battles in any order, 20 cycles, stars at 8 and 12. Stages 11–15: two nodes with two teams (a character stands in one node only), 30 cycles, stars at 12 and 18. The third star: at most one downed character. Cycles are shared by all battles of a stage. A stage opens after the previous one.
- *Cycles.* The engine's action value is 10000/SPD, so a cycle has the original length: the first 150, then 100. `memoTick` counts them; when a battle needs more cycles than the stage has left, it ends in defeat («Циклы закончились»). Every cycle start pushes a `cycle` event and applies the turbulence.
- *Memory Turbulence.* 1–5: +50% Wind / Ice / Fire / Physical / Lightning DMG; 6: Skill DMG +25% and +2 SP each cycle; 7: Ultimate DMG +25% and +20 energy each cycle; 8: the ally with the highest ATK deals +60% for one turn; 9: Weakness Break Efficiency +50% for the next attack; 10: CRIT Rate +8% each cycle up to 40%; 11: the lowest-HP ally heals 60%; 12: next attack +50% DMG; 13: a shield of 25% max HP; 14: CRIT Rate +20% and CRIT DMG +40% until the next cycle; 15: enemies take +35% DMG until their next action.
- *Battles.* Full HP, 50% energy, every team Technique fires at the start, no Contact, no retreat, no EXP or drops. A lost battle can be replayed (the cycles of earlier battles stay) or the hall can be left.
- *Enemies.* The original line-ups, with Bronya, Svarog, Gepard, Cocolia and the Grizzly as bosses and the new enemies above. Levels map the original 32–65 onto 18–49 (`TUN.lvlFrom`/`lvlTo`); `TUN.hp` sets an HP factor per stage, `atkRamp` adds 2% ATK per stage.
- *Balance* (scratch bot `fhbal.cjs`: two auto teams with four rolled relics each, levels around 22 + 2.2 per stage): stages 1–7 give 3 stars at that level, 8–13 about 2–2.5, 14–15 about 2 (the third star is the hard one against the bosses); no defeat at that level, cycle defeats appear 5–10 levels lower on the late stages.
- *Rewards.* First clear of a stage: 1–5 give 20 Сияющий отсвет, 5 «Журнал приключений» and 2 500 credits; 6–15 give 40 Сияющий отсвет, 2 «Путеводитель путешественника», 3 «Журнал приключений» and 5 000 credits. Every 3 stars (15 tiers, claimed in the hall, C takes all): 200 Jade, 5 000 credits and 80 Нефритовое перо. The original also pays Lost Crystal and Refined Aether, which this game does not have; credits are a quarter of the original.
- *Shop «Бесценные ценности».* «Пассаж Световых конусов» (Сияющий отсвет): Фермата, Равноценный обмен, Мы — Дикий Огонь, Весенние потоки рек, Прошлое и будущее, Гав! Время прогулок!, Важность завтрака, 200 each, no limit; each has its original S1 effect, +25% per superimposition (`memoDmg` and the `memo` hooks). «Шутки сбываются» (Нефритовое перо): Самомоделирующаяся смола 1200 (1 per refresh), «Путеводитель путешественника» 10 (10), credits ×2 500 for 10 (12), then credits ×1 250 for 10 without a limit; the stock refreshes every 42 days. The resin is spent in Synthesis («Реликвии») with 100 Relic Remains and picks the main stat of the crafted relic. Echo of War also pays 10 Сияющий отсвет, up to three times a week, once the hall is open.

**Tuning.** `TUNING` in `hsr-core.js` holds the main knobs: growth, enemy HP and ATK, toughness per action, break delay, energy gains, Warp rates and pity. The boss HP and ATK multipliers are in `ENEMIES`.

## Missions

The phone tile «Миссии» opens the mission screen. There are three kinds, each with its colour on the screen, the HUD and the map markers: Trailblaze missions (gold) follow the main story, Companion missions (violet) are stories of one character, Adventure missions (blue) are side errands. The list shows active missions only, grouped by kind and chapter; the Archive keeps the story that is behind.

**Trailblaze.** The story keeps one objective (`state.obj`), and `TRAIL_MISSIONS` names it with the official 1.0 mission it belongs to. A mission ends when its story flag is set (`club` marks the end of the hide-and-seek), and it pays a reward from the table: 440 Jade over the whole story plus credits and EXP books. Missions that the game tells inside one scene end together. «Виртуальная вселенная: Первичный тест» is a Trailblaze mission of the prologue, as in the original, but it stays optional and runs next to the main one.

**Tracking.** One mission is tracked: the HUD shows its name and step, the minimap arrow and the map marker point at it. By default it is the current Trailblaze mission, or the first other mission when the story is over. Enter on the screen tracks a mission or switches tracking off.

**Prologue.** The four 1.0 missions follow the original order and places. «Хаос глубин» (Base Zone) is played by Kafka and Silver Wolf as guests: the security gate, the corridor of portraits, the Reception Center, the route to the Monitoring Room, two terminals, the Blind Spot curio and the screen that leads to the vault and the vessel choice. «Эпицентр бури» (Storage Zone): waking in the Gallery of Shadow, March gives the bat, the first battle, the broken Atrium lift, Dan Heng from the upper tier, Arlan's encryption key, the stairs, the fight at the lift (`c_drones`) and Himeko in the Master Control Zone. «Момент вселенского спокойствия»: Asta and three Light Cones, Hinkel's satellite test, Abraham's cyclic number, the alarm. «Неисчезающие тени» (Supply Zone): the hatch without power, the Electrical Room lever, the repulsion bridge, the Doomsday Beast on the platform, Herta in her office, the decision with Himeko on the platform and the Express. Flags in order: `chaos`, `intro`, `lift`, `arlan_met`, `storm`, `asta_met`, `moment`, `hs_power`, `hs_bridge`, `doom`, `herta_met`, `express_intro`. Old saves without `state.hsv` that stopped in the middle of the prologue move to the Master Control Zone before Asta.

**Ruan Mei** (Trailblaze Continuance, chapter «Венец смертного и божественного», opens with Herta's message after `ending`, data in `hsr-seclusion.js`): Herta and Asta prepare the meeting of three geniuses; Ruan Mei on the platform feeds you a dessert with the anti-truth serum and asks to catch her runaway creations (Molten Cheese Tart with its fan club in the Storage Zone, Salted Plum at the hologram of the Master Control Zone); her access card opens the Seclusion Zone; Gray Bean Paste cries in the Breeding Ground, Ruan Mei's notes reveal the plan to clone an Emanator, the stranger in the plaster head points at the Incubator, the synthetic Skaracabaz lives 56 seconds (eight of its turns) and falls apart with its swarm unless the team breaks it first; Ruan Mei asks whether you want to forget her. The creations then live in the Seclusion Zone.

**Hook's chain** (Companion, chapter «Обрывки жизней пред горнилом»; it opens after Cocolia, data in `hsr-hook.js`):
1. «Сокровище старого бригадира». Julian's group message. Fersman is attacked by vagrants in the vagrant camp behind the Great Mine, his vein detector is stolen. Swetta names four suspects; exactly one lies (the cook says the helper did it, the tailor and the helper deny it, the merchant blames the cook). Naming the cook goes straight to the chase; a wrong name leads to Fersman's tent, a label and four crates, the right one smells of stone-grilled olm. The cook tries to sell the detector to Sampo; after the fight Sampo gives Findie (Находюша). Balaway cannot repair the probe; Fersman says his real treasure is Hook.
2. «Сокровище малышки Хук». Hook's typo message. Rivet Town: Julian's marbles and rusty key at his parents' shop, the broken toy drill in the mining team's junk, monsters at the orphanage, Findie finds the time capsule and Junjun. Natasha repairs Junjun and sees that his eyes are erebus; Hook will sell him but not his eyes.
3. «Подарок Хук». The «Дикий Огонь» advert: an auction in the vagrant camp with a Geomarrow Probe. Three buyers are needed (the weird man, the gemologist, the silent vagrant); Gertie in Boulder Town, the old lady (after her grandson is saved from two thugs) and the careless vagrant (after the robot with his contract is beaten) raise Junjun's price by 50 shields each. The probe is stolen, Hook beats the thief and refuses to sell it to him. With two or three extra buyers Hook has 750 shields and wins the probe; otherwise Natasha outbids her and sells it to her for the money and three months of cleaning. At the birthday party Fersman gives Junjun's clothes and house, bought with the money for the detector.

Each mission pays 60 Jade, 6000 credits and EXP books. Hook, Natasha and Fersman appear where the step needs them even when Hook or Natasha are in the team (mark field `always`).

**Adventure.** «Мимолётные огни» (chapter «Приключения: Звёздный экспресс», data in `hsr-messenger.js`): at Trailblaze Level 21 an unknown contact («???» until you meet her) writes «Ты здесь?» and says something on the Express is meant only for you. Pom-Pom feels that something is wrong on the train; Himeko and Welt have not noticed anything; the mirror in the Общий вагон is new, and the crew cannot see it. The second look brings the Messenger out of the mirror: she explains the Garden of Recollection and the Forgotten Hall. After any cleared stage she asks to keep her secret, and Welt asks what you found: tell the truth (`fh_truth`) or call her a curiosity spirit (`fh_lie`). The mission pays 2 000 credits and 3 «Журнал приключений» (the original's Condensed Aether does not exist here); her next words at the mirror depend on the choice.

## Level design

Each map has one landmark and a clear main path. Side rooms off the path hold chests, and one secret per region sits behind a breakable crate. Space Anchors mark the breathers before the peaks. Locked exits explain why they are closed.

- **Astral Express.** Three cars after the original layout (the Trailblazer's room upstairs in the Party car is left out). The Общий вагон is the hub: a marble aisle between red carpet lounges, two bay planters in the middle under the shell lantern, Pom-Pom with the buffet trolley in the waist, Himeko at the coffee counter, Welt by his armchair, the Warp terminal on the left and the Simulator in the bottom left corner (as the tutorial says), the navigation console and the IPC screen at the right end, the phonograph, the guest book and the Omni-Synthesizer. The left door goes down the stairs into the Вагон для вечеринок (round bar with two glowing columns, purple booths with visitors, Pom-Pom's coatroom, the arcade machine, the spiral stair to the closed storeroom, the locked door to the next car); the right door leads to the Спальный вагон (a corridor along the windows with wall-mounted paper whales; Dan Heng's archive with the Data Bank and his bedroll on the water floor, March's room with the bed, desk, mirror, bunny chair and photo wall, Himeko's and Welt's locked doors, two lobbies). Dan Heng and March wait in their rooms while they are out of the team. Every car is a teleport destination and an area on the navigation map. The train remembers the journey: new acquaintances sign the guest book, March pins a photo for each chapter (empty frames show what is still ahead), records for the phonograph unlock with the places visited, roster characters visit the Party car, and after the Jarilo-VI ending the IPC screen plays its first broadcast with the hint about the Luofu. From «Мимолётные огни» on, an oval standing mirror in the left lounge of the Общий вагон leads to the Forgotten Hall; only the Trailblazer sees it. After the meeting the Messenger stands to the left of it (npc mark `o`, `near: 3.2`): she fades in as you come within about three tiles and fades out when you walk away, and talking to her opens the same menu as the mirror.
- **Herta Space Station** (plan and decisions in `HSR-HERTA.md`, second pass zone by zone in `HSR-HERTA-2.md`). Five zones of the original plus the office, with 15 Space Anchors under their official names. Every zone has one landmark and windows on space and the Blue:
  - *Master Control Zone* (`hs_control`, 50×50, traced from the original minimap): an arrowhead pointing north. The Watcher Zone deck under the glass tip with the master control crest, screen walls and the golden globe hologram over the raised spine; Asta at the main console where the arms widen, Peppy walking east of her, two round wells over the Blue; the north-west computer lab with the song contest, the east labs, stairs down to the Central Passage (wood floor, Wen Shiqi on his bench, Cooking Genius No. 123, the three trashcans); in the south-west wing Rocky and Bernard at their computers, Sheila and the door to Herta's Office; the Space Elevator at the south tip. Anchors «Зона наблюдения», «Центральный проход». Fifteen readables of the original on note stands and tables, the researchers and ambient pairs with their lines from the game, the Herta puppet, the bookshelf note for Mare, the Man With Luscious Hair (four talks).
  - *Base Zone* (`hs_base`, 38×61, traced from the original minimap): the Space Elevator and security gate at the south end where Kafka arrives, the long corridor with the Genius Society portraits, the Reception Center (the station emblem hologram under the north wall, the front desk with the Introductory Guide and Herta's Manuscripts, two glass benches with plants, the honeycomb decal, side galleries with the Strange Plant), the Medical Cabin in the west (four exam pods around the green cross station, Joanne), two corridors up to the north-west room (Herta puppet) and the Monitoring Room (the Blind Spot curio), Asta's office east of it (her letters, shopping list and the scam email), the sealed hatch with the Triple Authentication panel between the corridors. Anchors «Комната наблюдения», «Приёмный центр». Two Formidable Foes, three of the six little robots, the Strange Plant that needs watering on two days (`{day}` step), Watson's award.
  - *Storage Zone* (`hs_storage` and `hs_storage2`, 72×31 each, traced from the original minimaps): the round Atrium with the white lift column over the shaft, cross paths and Fragmentum corrosion; the west block with the control center (Arlan in the prologue), the stairs up, the hexagonal doorframes, Emily and the guard, and the Disposal Site maze with Eikura Shuu's memorial; the north-west room (the cluttered monitor, the cold coffee cup, a little robot, a Formidable Foe) and the south-west room (Warp Trotter, Herta's Manuscripts). The two curio wings keep the order of the original: north-east, left side House of Cards, No. 458, No. 269, No. 2691, right side Broken Blade, Wind Glider, No. 021, the Shattered Curio in the middle; south-east, left side No. 752 Rating Pistol, No. 269, the empty case, No. 148, right side No. 1381, No. 269, the unknown curio. Every exhibit floats in its own lit glass case (`curio` decor). The south-east wing ends in the Gallery of Shadow and the Stellaron room, where the Trailblazer wakes. Upstairs a ring over the shaft with bridges to the Space Elevator in the middle, the Special Purpose Lab (hanging scanner, robot arms, Capote, Lamina, the Light Cone cabinet, a break room), the transparent chamber behind an Unearthly Marvel bridge (the strange voice, Tottonid reports) and a second Marvel puzzle with a chest, the sealed door to the Seclusion Zone. Anchors «Внешний отсек центра управления», «Атриум», «Галерея теней», «Лаборатория специального назначения».
  - *Supply Zone* (`hs_supply`, 62×30, traced from the original minimap): the west column (Sheila's script in the northmost room, Gunn's confession, the meeting room for «Версия 1», the Electrical Room with the generator, the lever and the Space Elevator, the Locked Device room with the paper cranes and the password 1233211234567, Adler at the south end), the Spare Parts Warehouse (a ring around the hourglass centrepiece and grey tanks, a Formidable Foe, the hatch to the north corridor), the maintenance rooms (the Door to A New World toilet with Curio Management Log III, the Unearthly Marvel with a chest, the Warp Trotter), the Security Department (Arlan after the prologue, the Comet Hunter letters, the attack log, Wen Tianweng's workshop with the Kapo-Kali recipe), the bridge booth and the orange and blue repulsion bridges (one colour at a time, the blue one leads to the precious chest island), and the T-shaped platform with departure boards, the docked Express and the scared researcher's rest corner. Anchors «Склад запчастей», «Перрон», «Электроподстанция».
  - *Seclusion Zone*, three floors of a round tower as in the original. Top, `hs_seclusion3` (entered from the second floor of the Storage Zone): the Distribution Center, an east-west bridge under a glass dome over the Blue, the round platform with the lift down to the Incubator, Ruan Mei's long-range terminal (the call sometimes fails, «Абонент вне зоны обслуживания»), her lab log, the genius's manuscripts, the stranger in the plaster head, Joseph, Wendy, Wenfry and the Sightless Orphan. Middle, `hs_seclusion` (stairs from the top floor): a ring around the pit, the Breeding Ground in the north-east with Ruan Mei's creations and the Herta manuscript ad, the greenhouse fields in the north (weeding on three days), the labs in the north-west (the experimental pod that Herta pulls you out of), the Pharmaceutical Room in the south-west with light-column dispensers and Ruan Mei's desk, the shuttle area in the south-east with the large display that needs a short and a tall team leader, the balcony over the pit. Bottom, `hs_incubator`: the dark arena of the synthetic Skaracabaz and the throne of boxes with the Insighter. Anchors «Распределительный центр», «Питательная среда», «Фармацевтический кабинет», «Инкубатор». Opens with Ruan Mei's mission.
  - *Herta's Office* (`hs_office`): a half-round hall with blue neon arches over the dais of the Simulated Universe, a light wood floor with a grey walkway, the six Genius Society portraits with Herta's comments from the game, Herta's puppet, cases of spare puppets, the TV of Section 6.
  - *Space Elevator*: each zone has one elevator that asks for the zone (Master Control Zone first; the other zones keep their old lift tiles until their pass).
- **Storage Zone in the prologue.** The main path runs from the Gallery of Shadow at the end of the south-east curio wing through the Atrium (broken lift, Dan Heng) to the control center in the west, then up its stairs and across the ring bridges to the Space Elevator on the second floor.
- **Jarilo-VI rework** (plan and decisions in `HSR-JARILO.md`, stage by stage). Place, enemy and shop names follow the official Russian localisation (Надмирье, Пригородные снежные поля, Камнеград, Большая шахта, Заклёпкоград, Коридор исчезающего эха, Холм вечнозимья, Форт Клипота, Монумент вечнозимья, Вечная мерзлота, Свинка-копилка пространства, Верховная хранительница; Рональд keeps the Магазин в Административном районе, Лаудер the Магазин Подземья). Maps with `land` use the `hsr-jarilo` art. Domain entrances on the maps are props with `obj: 'domain'`, `art` (`gold`, `crimson`, `shadow`, `cavern`) and `calyx: id` (or a list): they open the Simulator screen for that domain only.
- **Simulated Universe domains.** Small islands over the starry chasm. The start is opposite the portals and the guard stands between them, so the portals only open on the way through the fight; in the corridor domain the portals are on the left so the party cards never cover them.
- **Storage Zone.** A crate maze. The main path loops through the lower corridor into the cargo bay, where the Doomsday Beast waits.
- **Outlying Snow Plains** (`j_plains`, 62×39, traced from the original minimap): a hooked valley from the Landing Point in the east to Belobog's city gate at the north-west tip, with a dead-end loop round a rock spine in the south. The Landing Point and the Long Slope are the Space Anchors; the golden Calyx (Bud of Memories) near the landing point runs the Calyx tutorial, the crimson one (Bud of the Hunt) sits in the upper valley. The main path passes the snowdrift where Sampo hides, the buried houses (Vache's dugout with his lab records and his letter to Natasha) and ends at the gate, where the Silvermane Guards ambush the team (two waves, then Gepard, who stops at 30% health: encounter `endAt`). A Formidable Foe (Ice Out of Space), a Warp Trotter pack, the rumour's golden Trotter in the southern loop, Lynx with food on the Long Slope after the story. The gate stays locked until Gepard. Old saves are moved by `fixJarilo` (`state.jv`, `JARILO_STEPS` in `hsr-game`): the player lands at the landing point, opened chests carry over by count, a visited map unlocks its anchors.
- **Administrative District** (`j_belobog`, 41×75, traced from the original minimap): Alisa Rand Avenue runs north to south with tram rails in the middle; the Golden Theater plaza (anchor «Золотой театр»), the Central Plaza with the fountain and the Everwinter Monument (anchor «Центральная площадь»), the twin stairs east to Qlipoth Fort, the south end with Ronald's shop and the Neverwinter Workshop, the slanted street to the Goethe Hotel (anchor «Отель «Гёте»»). The Ding-Dong tram (`tram` on the map, `Game.updateTram`, `H.Jarilo.tramSprite`) runs along the rails and pushes the player off them with a line from the original. Sub-maps: `j_cable` (cable car station under the plaza, ticking suitcase, chest, platform to Boulder Town), `j_florist` (Vaska), `j_workshop` (Serval, the first Magnetic Flow Connector), `j_hotel` (lobby, corridor with five doors and the twins, the room), `j_fort` (the Supreme Guardian's office).
