Interiors use `inside` lands in `hsr-jarilo` (parquet, carpets, wallpaper with wainscot, windows with curtains). The chapter «Ночь в городе вечнозимья» lives in `hsr-jarilo-story`; street life (about twenty named residents, 19 bins, wanted posters, mailboxes, theatre programmes, the hotel menu, the gold watch and the Praise reward) lives in `hsr-jarilo-life`. Exits to the cable station, the cordoned alley, the Restricted Zone and the fort open with their story flags. Cond strings accept `$item` (the party holds the item).
- **History and Culture Museum** (`j_museum`, 34×66, traced from the original minimap, `museum` land with teal walls, cream tiles and a teal carpet runner): the entrance hall with two columns, the lobby with Eris's semicircular desk (anchor «Краеведческий музей»), Uldan's storeroom, the General Hall with two pairs of picture partitions and two exhibit islands, Boris's restoration room to the west, the Industrial Hall to the east (car stage, automaton models, terminals, the Gears and Wisdom sculpture, the big radiator) and the History and Culture Hall up the stairs (portraits of the Supreme Guardians, Voidranger ice sculptures, Alisa Rand's lance, the bat, the projector with three topics). All 82 exhibits of the original are props with their official Russian texts; the portraits open their captions as documents. The door is on the west side of the Central Plaza; the booth attendant lets the team in only after the story (`ending`, then `mus_pass`). Visitors speak in short overheard lines: an NPC with `bark` (lines `mark|text`, where the mark is the speaker on the same map, empty for the NPC itself) starts the exchange when the player comes within 2.5 tiles (`Game.updateBarks`, `barkLine`), each line hangs above its speaker for 2.6 seconds, the same group stays quiet for 25 seconds. An NPC without a script but with `name` says its `text` under that name.
- **Backwater Pass** (`j_backwater`, 34×57, traced from the original minimap, `backwater` land: pink-beige townhouses, slate roofs, a violet Fragmentum tint, blue rifts on the cobbles): a winding shopping street from the alley in the south of the district to the sealed Goethe Mansion at the end of the north-east lane. The south tip, the west corridor to the octagonal plaza (anchor «Транспортный узел», the Stagnant Shadow «Форма миража»: the Decaying Shadow `decaying` with two Imaginary Weavers, `adds` on a Calyx entry), the abandoned café in the south-east pocket (barista's handbook, the estate ad, the upside-down chair, the second Formidable Foe with Blaze Out of Space, the cellar hatch down to Boulder Town), the loop round a block with the Leisure Plaza (anchor «Площадь отдыха», the crimson Calyx Bud of Abundance, the phone booth, Wallace's «Ищу друга», a Warp Trotter, a Magflow Link that opens a cache), the north street to the top bar (the golden Calyx Bud of Aether, the board with three old newspapers, the first Formidable Foe at the end of the north alley) and the lane to the mansion (anchor «Особняк Гёте»). The chapter «Можешь бежать, но тебе не спрятаться» is played on this map: the arrest ends with the escape here (`bp_in`), the way back is closed, guards block the west corridor, the Magflow Link in the south lane (`mf_backwater`) opens the first gate, the monsters in front of the switch, the switch (a `lever`) opens the second gate into Bronya's ambush, her squad and then Bronya (`bp_bronya`, ends at 1%), Sampo's smoke and the wake in Boulder Town. Gates accept `open` (a cond that opens them for good), levers accept `if`. The field enemies, domains and the Magflow cache appear after the chase (`fled`). The district alley leads here after the chase, and Boulder Town's way up comes out of the café hatch after `returned`. The phone booth rings only after the district booth (`phone_called`).
- **Boulder Town** (`u_boulder`, 45×44, traced from the original minimap, `cavern` land): the north street to the Rivet Town path with Fidora and her robot, the hotel square (anchor «Гранд-отель «Гёте»», Gertie, the Treasure Chest Magician with two chests), the central plaza with the green-domed clock tower, Lauder's shop, Antonia, Tanya, Balaway's closed appraisal shop and the cable car platform (opens with `cable_open`), the west street with the Fight Club (anchor «Бойцовский клуб», Scott, the Mysterious Kid) and Hook's corner, the Gathering Place of Wildfire (Oleg, Joshua, the message board, the flier, a letter), the clinic door (anchor «Клиника Наташи»), the south avenue with Turner's stall, Julian and Alina and the south branch to the Great Mine; the Furnace Core hangs over the north. Interiors: `u_clinic` (beds, Natasha, Fersman, the clinic readables and «Белобожские музыканты»), `u_club` (the octagonal ring, Dr. Dig, punching bags with proverbs, Scott's box with the code 0-2-1-3), `u_grandhotel` (Gertie's kitchen and dining room, the Cheap Room with beds, the rating card and the review book). Missions on these maps follow the original: «Игра в прятки» (waking in the clinic, `c_lostworld`, the practice round and three hidden Moles: props without sprites and Julian in disguise), «Восьмое и последнее правило» (the ring fight `club_ring`, Sampo caught outside), «Долгое ожидание лезвия клинка» (three marked spots with Sampo, Seele cutting the bullet, the vagrants), «Свидание на закате» (Gertie, the dream in the Cheap Room, the night walk with Bronya, Natasha's request), «Лежащие среди ржавчин» (supplies to Natasha, the plan with Oleg; Svarog's trigger in Rivet Town waits for `rust_done`), «Мы не умеем прощаться» (the talk on the way to the hotel, Seele and Bronya at the orphanage, the meeting at the Gathering Place and Bronya's letter). Old saves get the new step flags from their milestones (`JARILO_STEPS`, step 3). The Hook chain points at `u_clinic:n` and `u_clinic:g` for Natasha and Fersman.
- **Great Mine** (`u_mine`, 43×44, traced from the original minimap, `cavern` land): tunnels in bands round the pit with the glowing Geomarrow vein and its rail ring (`vein`), the entrance pocket (anchor «Вход», the crimson Calyx Bud of Nihility, the crowd of miners and vagrants with overheard lines), the west ramp, the upper gallery with the dormitory readables and a Warp Trotter, the north-east cave to the Vagrant Shelter, the middle band with the bridge and the lift, the central hall (the Stagnant Shadow «Форма шипа»: an Automaton Direwolf with two Hounds, the Magflow Link to a cache, a Formidable Foe, anchor «Смотровая площадка»), the south loop (the golden Calyx Bud of Treasures, anchor «Главный туннель шахты») and the south-east chambers (the shift board, the Stagnant Shadow «Форма ожога» after the story). «Уж сколько их упало в эту бездну» follows the original: Seele and Natasha at the entrance, Peak asleep on the bridge, Elaine, the vagrants at the lift (`mine_lift`) who drop Findie, the standoff with Antonia (`mine_stand`), the broken cart and the two parts found with Findie, the Direwolf (`mine_direwolf`), the ledge, Clara and Svarog, the Grizzly and Oleg. Findie: a prop with `findie: true` is a search target; while one is visible on the map an arrow over the player points to the nearest and a top banner says how hot the trail is (`Game.findieTarget`). The field enemies, domains and the cache appear after the story of the mine (`mine_done`). **Vagrant Shelter** (`u_camp`, the old camp map kept for the Hook chain): anchor «Убежище бродяг», Seele, Peak, the Miners' Ultimatum and the Fragmented Diary, children who broke the lamp, the mine cart operators with «Ночь в Большой шахте» I–III (`hsr-carts`).
- **Rivet Town** (`u_rivet`, 41×52, traced from the original minimap, `rivet` land: a dead trading town under snow at night, lit windows, snow caps, pipes over the streets, the hanging workshop sign) and **the orphanage hill** (`u_rivet2`, 26×25): the entrance bridge over the ravine (anchor «Вход», the notices under the bridge), the south plaza with the crimson Calyx Bud of Erudition, the terrace with the view of the orphanage and the window of the blind girl, the barricade on the main road, the south-west street past the restaurant, the market with empty stalls (anchor «Заброшенный рынок», the Tempering Workshop door and sign, a Magflow cache), the north plaza (the Stagnant Shadow «Форма ветра» with the Stormbringer), the trading street round the inner block (Ice Out of Space, the Warp Trotter, a Magflow safe), the north-east avenue to the Robot Settlement, the upper street to the orphanage and the north-west cliff path. The hill holds the lit orphanage with festoon lights, the playground (swing, slide, crates), anchor «Сиротский приют» and the long stairs down to the cliff path. «Прошлое накроет снежной лавиной» follows the original: the vantage point, monsters, the restaurant and the workshop, the empty market crates, Eric and Bronya's medal, the trading street, the orphanage, Ice Out of Space, the slide, the swing and the brooch, the rubbing alcohol and Bronya's toy, Clara and Perkins at the bottom of the stairs, the shared painkillers. The barricade opens after the mission, Perkins clears the cliff path. Natasha takes the three boxes in «Лежащие среди ржавчин». The Hook chain marks stay on Rivet Town, the orphanage trigger moved to the hill.
- **Robot Settlement** (`u_settle`, 34×62, upper level, and `u_settle1`, 50×24, lower level, traced from the two original minimaps, `settle` land: a snowy cave plaza, iron railings, vagrant tents, dark pillars with gold Geomarrow crystals): the Vagrant Camp (anchor «Лагерь бродяг», Eunice, Eric, the scout robots, vagrant pairs whose overheard lines give the quiz answers), the narrow road with two iron gates (anchor «База Сварога», the lift down), the south hall, Svarog's mansion room with Clara, Svarog, Screwllum's Message and the letter of the Geomarrow group, and the south-west pit. The lower level holds the Energy Conversion Station (anchor, the energy supply device, the crimson Calyx Bud of Harmony) and the east cave (Magflow cache, a Formidable Foe). Rivet Town's north-east avenue leads here once Hook and Sampo take the team.
- **Silvermane Guard Restricted Zone** (`j_restricted`, 50×42, `city` land, laid out after the original minimap): the outpost plaza with Franz's gate (anchor «Пост стражи», Dunn's booth, the oath, the Cavern of Corrosion), the gear bridge over a round pit in the middle (ent kind `gbridge`: each bridge cell belongs to the `ns` or `ew` arm and is solid unless the map's `gearFlag` matches, terminals turn it), the frontline in the west (anchor «Передовая Запретной зоны», Gepard, the gate to the Fragmentum and the Corridor, the Stagnant Shadow «Форма пламени») and the southern compound with the Mechanism Energy Hub behind a key gate (anchor «Энергетический центр»). The Administrative District gets curfew patrols on the way from the alley to Serval's workshop: triggers with `sight: true` are drawn as red cells, stepping on one sends the team back to the alley.
- **Corridor of Fading Echoes** (`j_fissures`, 38×68, `corridor` land, laid out after the original minimap): the frozen ruins of the old Belobog border fort in the Fragmentum. From the Restricted Zone gate the road climbs past Bronya's snow globe to the mechanism gate (anchor «Укреплённая зона»); hub 0 at the entrance and three guarded hubs open it, the third one only after a controller turns the gear bridge to the east (`gbridge` with the map's own `gearFlag: 'cor_ew'` and a pale `gearPit`). Beyond the gate: the command post with the readables (anchor «Командный центр»), the Guardian Shadow in front of the fort gate, the octagon plaza with Cocolia's echo at the stairs (anchor «Древнее поле битвы: Передовая») and the exit to Everwinter Hill. Echo NPCs (`echo: true`) are drawn semi-transparent. Two Stagnant Shadows, one Cavern of Corrosion, two Magflow caches, twelve treasures, three Formidable Foes and a Warp Trotter.
- **Everwinter Hill** (`j_everwinter`, 46×41, `hill` land, laid out after the original minimap): a spiral path climbs clockwise from the Corridor exit (anchor «Древнее поле битвы в Снежных полях») up the outer ridge, over a stepped causeway under the broken hand of the colossus (decor `colossus`) to the north-east shelf and down a stair onto the square summit (anchor «Платформа созидания», the Stellaron Imprisonment Device, the Echo of War, the Section 6 rift). An inner arc with the Cavern of Corrosion «Путь провидения» and the Warp Trotter joins the summit from the west. Frozen Voidrangers (`voidranger`) line the path; two of them can be inspected. The Path Space (`j_pathspace`, 34×22, `pathspace` land) is a set of amber platforms over a starry void with four memories (`echo` NPCs) and the Guardians' Will; it is visited once inside «Возвращение». Battles: `dawn_waves` has three waves and an encounter `field` (the Engine of Creation strikes all allies on the first two enemy turns of each wave), `cocolia1` ends at half HP.
- **Pillars of Creation** (`j_pillars`, 60×50, `pillars` land, warm low sun, laid out after the original minimap): one road from the north-west canyon (exit to the Corridor, anchor «Столп творения: Вход») along a paved passage under leaning ruined skyscrapers (decor `tower`) and a wide curve down to the construction site (anchor «Столп творения: Строительная площадка»). East of the site a fenced pit holds the broken Engine of Creation in scaffolding (`enginewreck`); the unstable long bridge (`longbridge`) crosses a ravine to the west. No enemies. Soldier Kyle in the Corridor (`fm_kyle`) leads there any time after the story. The Rivet Town outskirts (`u_rivet3`, 36×30) is the stealth excursion of «Рынок будущего II»: IPC posts with red sight cells (`sight` triggers send the team back), gates that open after a stealth bomb prop, and the cliffs where Svarog and Topaz wait.
- **Belobog.** The town hub: Qlipoth Fort gate, the Monument plaza with Pela's tour, Hotel Goethe (the porter starts the night of the arrest), the shop, Sampo, the cordoned alley that leads to the Underworld and Serval's workshop.
- **Boulder Town.** The underworld hub: Natasha's clinic, Hook and the Moles, Wildfire, the flea market and the exits to the Great Mine (opens after Seele vouches for the team) and Rivet Town (opens after the night at the hotel).
- **Great Mine.** Rails and crystals lead down to the Geomarrow vein where Svarog and Clara stand.
- **Rivet Town.** Ruined houses, the old orphanage and the factory yard in front of Svarog's settlement.
- **Vagrant camp.** Behind the east drift of the Great Mine (opens with Hook's first mission). The entrance where Fersman is attacked, Fersman's tent, Swetta, the cook's pot, the supply crates in the east, the auction table under the north wall and the machine graveyard in the south-east corner.
- **Corridor of Fissures, Everwinter Hill.** Ice mazes that climb to the Stellaron.

## Official site

`http://hsr.hoyoverse.com` is the game's site in the in-system Internet Explorer: `js/apps/hsr-site.js` (`window.hsrSite`) and `css/apps/hsr-site.css` (classes `hsrs-*`). IE routes any address with `hoyoverse`, `starrail98` or `mihoyo` to it (checked first in `identifyPageKey`), and it is in Favorites, the search results and the Яndex catalog. It is the one exception to the Steam-only rule in AGENTS.md.

**Look.** A fixed 780 px table on a tiled star field, painted in the game's own palette: navy window fills, brass double frames, gold serif headings. The header is a carriage window: a 320×240 canvas that plays the title scene (`H.TitleScene.draw`) at about 12 frames per second and stops when the page is left or when reduced motion is on. The navigation is a Star Rail with a diamond per station; the current page has the filled diamond. Pictures are canvases with `data-art` specs that `paintArt` draws from the game art after render: `fig:<id>:<scale>` and `figf:trail:<scale>` (figures), `path:<path>:<size>`, `item:<id>:<size>`, `foe:<id>:<size>`, `still:<id>` (`H.Cine.still` backdrops), `eid:<id>` (Eidolon pictures), `title` (the live window).

**Pages** (all derived from the URL in `parseUrl`): `/` (introduction, news, ticket, current Warp banners), `/download` (ticket with mirrors, install steps, requirements, Steam alternative), `/news` and `/news?id=<id>` (11 dated items from 26.04.1999 to 27.11.2000, the newest is the version 1.5 article `v15`), `/characters` (grouped by Path) and `/characters?id=<id>` (figure, stats, kit, Warp line, Fusions, Eidolon picture with its six fragments; the Trailblazer page shows both Paths), `/worlds` (areas from `H.WORLDS` with backdrops, enemies and bosses), `/play` (battle rules, elements, Paths, Fusions, Warp rules and controls), `/gallery` and `/gallery?pic=<n>` (2 screenshots, 20 places, 20 Eidolon pictures), `/support` (FAQ, `support@hoyoverse.com`, which answers in Outlook), `/guestbook` (seed entries plus visitor entries in `win98_hsrsite_guestbook_v1`). Unknown addresses show «Станция не найдена». Numbers on the site (Skill Points, Warp cost, pity, Fusions, break effects, banners, rumors) are read from the game data, so they follow the game.

**Скрепыш.** `AppClippy.hsrSiteReaction` comments once per page kind per session and stays silent on re-renders of the same address. On the home and download pages his buttons follow the download state (mirror tip and Moscow download, install, play); he notes the finished download (`onHsrDownloaded`, called by the site), meets Pom-Pom in the setup wizard, has lines for 33 characters (the Warp pity sums are computed from `TUNING`), signs the guestbook once and opens Outlook for `support@hoyoverse.com`. «Я сам разберусь» silences him on the site until the next session. The help index answers «Где скачать Honkai: Star Rail 98».

**Download.** The ticket on `/download` follows the state: no installer, download running, `StarRail98_Setup.exe` in `C:\My Documents\Downloads`, game installed. The mirror (Москва 5,1 КБ/с, Франкфурт 3,9, Шанхай 2,2) sets the download time (simulated, 6 to 30 s). The download window is styled as the IE 5 «Сохранение» dialog: progress, time left, rate, «Закрыть окно по окончании загрузки», and «Открыть» / «Открыть папку» when done; closing it cancels the download and saves nothing. The installer (`app: 'hsrsetup'`, opened from Explorer, the desktop or the site) runs the site's own setup wizard (Pom-Pom on the side panel): welcome, license (Next stays off until it is accepted), folder, copying, finish with «Запустить» and «Открыть README.TXT». Cancel asks first. Finishing adds `hsr98` to `purchased` and `downloaded` and calls `gameManager.finishInstallation`, so the desktop icon, Start menu, `C:\Games\StarRail98` and the Steam library all see the game. Running the installer again offers to play. The catalog entry has price 0, so Steam gives the game for free too.

## Fan site «Ковчег»

`http://www.kovcheg98.ru` is a players' guide site for HSR 98 in the in-system Internet Explorer: tier list, character dossiers, squads, Forgotten Hall by stage, Simulated Universe, bosses, articles, references, a team builder, a guestbook and «Мой экипаж». The staged plan, the decisions and every stage report are in `KOVCHEG.md`. The site opened on 12.10.1999 and was last updated on 20.11.2000 in its own timeline.

**How players find it.** `identifyPageKey` checks `kovcheg98` first. The site is in the «Поиск и порталы» catalog and answers search words such as ковчег, гайд, тир-лист, кого крутить, Зал забвения. It is not in Favorites: `new-sites-stage1` keeps exactly three portals there. The official site has a «Фан-сайты» box on its home page, GamerZone has the thread «Тир-лист Ковчега: кто согласен?» (thread 48, dated 1–3 December 1998 because the forum lives in 1998), Outlook has the editor `bocman@kovcheg98.ru`, and Clippy comments on the tier list, the builder and the guestbook (`kovchegReaction`).

**Modules** (loaded in this order after `hsr-site.js`; the order is tested):

| File | Content |
|---|---|
| `css/apps/kovcheg.css` | All styles, classes `kv-*`, only the 216 web-safe colours |
| `js/apps/kovcheg-data.js` | `window.kovchegData`: host, dates, crew, games «в доке», log, poll, hatches, guestbook seed, mail |
| `js/apps/kovcheg-hsr.js` | `window.kovchegHsr`: columns, modes, tiers, tags, build presets, standard cones, free characters, editorial shifts (`ADJUST`), bot notes, squads (`TEAMS`, `ARCHETYPES`), boss notes |
| `js/apps/kovcheg-hsr-bench.js` | `window.kovchegBench`, generated by the bench, never edited by hand |
| `js/apps/kovcheg-hsr-guides.js` | `window.kovchegGuides`: texts of all 53 dossiers |
| `js/apps/kovcheg-hsr-articles.js` | `window.kovchegArticles`: four articles |
| `js/apps/kovcheg-site.js` | `KovchegSite` (`window.kovchegSite`): routing, mast, home, hub, tier list, crew, dossiers, squads, text tokens, art, events |
| `js/apps/kovcheg-site-ref.js` | Hall, Universe, bosses, articles, cones, relics, Fusions, banners, about |
| `js/apps/kovcheg-sim.js` | `window.kovchegSim.create(H, K)`: the battle runner shared by the bench and the builder |
| `js/apps/kovcheg-site-builder.js` | `/hsr98/builder` |
| `js/apps/kovcheg-site-guest.js` | `/guestbook` |
| `js/apps/kovcheg-site-mine.js` | `/hsr98/mycrew` and the «own crew» marks |

The last four site modules add methods to `Object.getPrototypeOf(window.kovchegSite)`; `builder`, `guest` and `mine` wrap `attachEvents` and call the previous one first.

**Pages.** `/` home, `/hsr98/` deck, `/hsr98/tier?mode=hall|boss|su|start&cone=sig|std&rar=&path=&free=1&mine=1`, `/hsr98/crew`, `/hsr98/char?id=`, `/hsr98/teams`, `/hsr98/team?id=`, `/hsr98/hall?stage=1..15`, `/hsr98/su?world=1..4`, `/hsr98/bosses[?id=<boss enemy>]`, `/hsr98/guide[?id=newbie|math|pulls|free]`, `/hsr98/cones`, `/hsr98/relics`, `/hsr98/fusions`, `/hsr98/banners`, `/hsr98/builder?team=a,b,c,d&goal=h12-2|b-<encounter>&cone=std`, `/hsr98/mycrew`, `/guestbook`, `/about`. An unknown character, squad, boss or article id gives «Такого на борту нет».

**Numbers come from the bench.** `node tools/kovcheg-bench.cjs --jobs=20` (about three minutes) runs the real HSR 98 engine headless and writes `js/apps/kovcheg-hsr-bench.js`: per-mode, per-cone best squads for every fighter, uplift for the other columns, sustain survival, skill-point balance, squad logs, Forgotten Hall picks for all 15 stages and the tiers. Characters stand at the enemy level with +9 relics and the build from their dossier; every fight runs 12 times. `--check` prints the data fingerprint and the control fight. The test `bench results are fresh` fails when kits, tuning, enemies, encounters, cones, relics, Fusions or the site's columns, builds, squads or tiers change: run the bench again then. The builder calls the same `kovcheg-sim.js` with the same seeds, so its numbers match the bench exactly (tested on every hall pick).

**Texts.** Guides and articles use tokens resolved by `renderText`: `[[n:id]]` (link, nominative only), `[[k:id.ability.fx.field]]`, `[[s:id]]`, `[[b:mode.id]]`, `[[bs:mode.id]]`, `[[u:mode.id]]`, `[[us:mode.id]]`, `[[w:id]]`, `[[c:cone]]`, `[[f:fusion]]`, `[[t:id.col.mode]]`, `[[tm:team]]`, `[[tmb:team.mode]]`, `[[alt:team.slot.id.field]]`, `[[base:id.stat]]`, `[[tu:key]]` and `[[tup:key]]` (raw `TUNING` value and percent). An unknown token shows `[[?…]]` and fails the test.

**Site rules** (all tested): Web 1.0 look (tables, no border-radius, shadows, rgba, gradients, blur, transitions or opacity), web-safe colours, no emoji (the star is `gif('star')`), no em dash in site text (`esc` turns one from game data into a hyphen), Microslop/Win naming, zero comments.

**Saves for «Мой экипаж».** `writeSave` in `hsr-ui.js` also writes every save into `C:\Games\StarRail98` as `SAVE1.SAV`, `SAVE2.SAV`, `SAVE3.SAV` and `AUTOSAVE.SAV` (JSON with `date`, `summary`, `state`). On start the UI writes the existing slots that have no file yet. Without the game folder (or outside Win 98) nothing is written. The site opens the file through `window.fileDialog`, reads characters, levels, Eidolons, the Trailblazer's current Path and the Light Cones, and uses signature-cone numbers only when the save has the signature cones of every member.

**Storage keys.** `win98_kovcheg_hits_v1` (visit counter), `win98_kovcheg_poll_v1`, `win98_kovcheg_guestbook_v1` (up to 50 entries), `win98_kovcheg_crew_v1` (the loaded crew).

**Tests.** `node tests/kovcheg-site.test.js` (38 tests, part of `npm test`). Screenshots: `node tools/site-shots.cjs <outDir> url:http://www.kovcheg98.ru/hsr98/tier wait:900 shot:tier`.

**Real-web copy.** `node tools/web98-kovcheg.cjs` (`npm run build:web98-kovcheg`) writes `web98-site/kovcheg/` for https://pennygachi.men/web98/kovcheg/. Run `node tools/web98-sr98.cjs` first: the copy loads the game scripts from `../sr98/play/game/` instead of carrying its own. The tool copies the `kovcheg-*.js` modules in `index.html` order into `site/`, renames Honkai: Star Rail 98 / HSR 98 to Star Rail 98 / SR98, swaps the Steam lines for the browser game, extracts the `gif-*` icons of `js/icons.js` into `gifs.js` and fills `tools/web98-kovcheg/index.html`. `host.js` stands in for IE: hash routes (`#/hsr98/tier?mode=hall`), `data-url` clicks (hsr.hoyoverse.com goes to `../sr98/`, other in-system sites show a note), `gameManager` titles, and «Мой экипаж» reads the browser game's saves (`localStorage` `hsr98.save.<slot>`, same origin) or a .SAV file from disk through its own Open dialog. The tool refuses to wipe a folder it did not write (`<meta name="generator" content="web98-kovcheg">`). The sr98 site links to it from the home page box «Ковчег» and the footer.

## Section 6 shots

`warpsig`, `warpsigyanagi`, `warpamp`, `battles6` (env `KEYS`, `^`/`v` are ↑/↓), `skillsmiyabi`, `skillsyanagi`, `sigc<best>_<sec>` (the TV cinematic), `sigv_<id>_<rar>_<sec>[_h]` (one reveal, `_h` starts with the hop), `sigrack<best>`, `foe_ethereal`, `foe_etherhound`, `foe_mimic`. `charsheet` takes env `LOOK` (JSON merged into the look) for quick art experiments.

**Guild: Kazuma's party (version 1.3).** A collaboration with *KonoSuba*, merged into 1.3 and sold only on the «Гильдия» Warp tab. Kazuma is a 4★, Aqua, Megumin and Darkness are 5★, all with `faction: 'axel'`. Every kit is built around a flaw from the source that another member of the party covers: Darkness only hits enemies that stand still, Megumin collapses after her Explosion, Aqua sometimes fails and cries, and Kazuma turns the failures of his party into Luck.
- *Казума* (Nihility, Wind, 4★; Elation since patch 1.6, see «Path of Elation»). Basic «Прицельная стрельба». The Skill has three modes, picked with ↑/↓ while the Skill is picked (`kit.skills`, `flags.skillSel`, the «Навык Казумы» list above the cast plate takes clicks): «Кража» (1.2×, takes one buff or the shield, copies the buff to Kazuma for 2 turns, keeps part of a stolen shield, and rolls for loot: 35% plus his CRIT Rate, a relic one time in ten), «Связывание» (0.6×; a normal enemy loses its next turn, an elite or boss is only pushed back by 30%; after that the enemy cannot be tied until its next turn) and «Иссушающее касание» (targets another ally: drains 0.8× from the enemy with the most HP, heals Kazuma for half of it, gives the ally 20 energy and puts a collapsed Megumin back on her feet). Ultimate «Великая кража» (3.0× plus 0.35× per Luck): removes every buff and shield, cancels a charge or lock-on, ATK −20% for 2 turns, loot is certain, Luck is spent. Talent: CRIT Rate +10%; a miss of Darkness, a failure of Aqua or an effect that an enemy resists gives 1 Luck (up to 5: +4% CRIT Rate and +6% Effect Hit Rate each). Technique «Скрытность»: the party moves 25% sooner and Kazuma starts with 2 Luck.
- *Аква* (Abundance, Ice). Basic «Удар богини» 1.0×, 2.5× against the unholy (`H.UNHOLY`: Voidrangers, Shadewalkers, Frigid Prowler, Ethereals, the Doomsday Beast, the Mimic) and it strips one of their buffs. Skill «Исцеление» heals and cleanses one ally; aimed at a fallen ally it is «Воскрешение» (25% HP, energy 0, costs 2 Skill Points; the battle UI lets ←/→ and clicks pick fallen allies and refuses with a message when 2 points are missing). Ultimate «Священное создание воды»: heals everyone, 1.2× to all foes (×2.5 for the unholy) and soaks them: the next Ice hit on a soaked enemy freezes it (normal enemies always, elites and bosses with 50%). Talent: 20% of her Basic ATK and Skill fail at 60% power; she cries and gets a Tear (up to 3), and her next good action heals 20% more and hits the unholy 50% harder per Tear. The unholy aim at Aqua twice as often and deal 30% less damage to her. Technique «Трюк для вечеринок»: the party heals 10% and every enemy that is not a boss gets one point of Joy for Contact.
- *Мегумин* (Erudition, Fire). Basic «Удар посохом» 0.8× Physical. Skill «Заклинание взрыва» deals no damage, adds one chant (up to 3) and 20 extra energy, and shows a line of the incantation. Ultimate «Взрыв!» (energy 140): 4.5× to all, +35% and +10 Toughness damage per chant, and it takes Toughness from every enemy even without a Fire weakness (`anyTough`). Then she lies «Без сил» for 2 of her turns (DEF −20%, enemies aim at her more often) and cannot cast it again; Drain Touch ends that at once. Every kill by the Explosion gives 10 energy and +5% battle EXP (up to +20%). Technique «Ежедневный взрыв»: the battle opens with a 1.8× Explosion and Megumin lies down for one turn.
- *Даркнесс* (Preservation, Physical). Basic «Боковой удар» (1.8× ATK plus 1.0× DEF, +12% per Ecstasy) hits only an enemy that is broken, frozen or bound; against a moving enemy it misses («Промах!»), the enemy must attack her on its next action, she gains 1 Ecstasy and Kazuma gains Luck. The damage preview says «Промах» before the swing. Every enemy hit on her adds Ecstasy (up to 6, +5% DEF each); at 6 the next Basic is «Случайно попала!», a blast that hits anything and spends the stacks. Skill «Приманка»: taunt for 2 turns, −30% damage taken, a shield. Ultimate «Крестоносец прикроет»: for 2 turns half of every enemy hit on another ally goes to her («Прикрытие»), she heals 20%, gains 3 Ecstasy and taunts. Effect RES +40%. Technique «В бой первой!»: a taunt for the first turn and 3 Ecstasy.
- *Fusions*: «Ледяная ловушка» (Kazuma + Aqua: Ice and Wind on all, soak and freeze, 2 Luck; 4 Punchline since 1.6), «Взрыв на мне!» (Megumin + Darkness: a Fire Explosion on any Toughness with Darkness at the centre, she loses 10% HP, gets full Ecstasy and taunts, Megumin stays up) and «Да будет благословенен этот прекрасный мир!» (all four: Aqua heals 20% and cleanses, a 4.0× Fire Explosion on any Toughness, Darkness taunts for 2 turns, Kazuma gets 5 Luck). The Fusion cutscene of four members opens on four slanted strips (`quadIn`) and shows four lines (`swaps`); the battle banner spreads all four busts.
- *Contact*: Darkness's «Попросить ударить сильнее» uses the new tag `weird` («странное»): most families get scared, automatons do not understand, Ethereals get angry and Trotters like it.
- *Banner* «Гильдия» (between Convene and the standard banner): one channel «Набор в отряд» (`BANNERS.guild`, `party: true`), ticket «Манатит высшей пробы» (`mana`), else 160 jade. 5★ rate 1.2% with hard pity at 60; the chosen heroine with 50% (`guildFeatured`); a miss (`partyPick`) gives another heroine of the party or a standard 5★ character half and half (`guildParty`), so the split is 50 / 25 / 25, and after any miss the next 5★ is the chosen heroine. Kazuma is the only featured 4★ (50% and a guarantee after a miss). State `pityGuild`, `warpNGuild`, `guildStar` in `BANNER_STATE`; log entries `ban: 'guild'`, the history marks misses «другой член отряда», «гость из стандарта» or for 4★ «не Казума», each with «· дальше гарант». Faucet: the 1.3 letter (10 manatites). On every other banner (standard, event, cones, Signal Search, Convene) each 4★ result has a 1% chance (`kazumaCameo`) to be Kazuma instead (`warpPick` returns `cameo: true`); the roll comes before the featured 4★ roll, so the 4★ guarantee is neither spent nor set, and the history marks it «гость из гильдии». In the normal Warp his reveal is `WarpIntro.SHOWS.kazuma` (4.8 s, longer than other 4★ intros): he falls into the parlor car, shows Pom-Pom his adventurer's card instead of a ticket (Luck bar runs off the card), the guild «Кража!» beat (`ExplosionKit.beat`, which runs any guild cut beat outside the guild show), Steal grabs March 7th's camera, and the Warp ticket falls into his hand by luck; In the Convene (resonator and weapon channels) he has his own cut `CUTS.kazuma` in hsr-convene.js (6.5 s): washed up on the night shore, a resonance scan where only Luck runs off the panel («РЕЗОНАТОР?»), a giant toad rises from the tide as an echo, the guild «Кража!» beat, and Steal turns the toad into a tiny echo toad that rides on his head into the pose (`ExplosionKit.toad`); shots `convv_kazuma_4_<sec>`. Signal Search shows him with its generic 4★ reveal. Each place has its own line: Warp «Это точно не Аксель…» (`SHOWS.kazuma.line`), Signal Search «Меня засосало в телевизор?» (`SignalKit.QUOTES`), Convene «Меня что, опять куда-то призвали?» (`ConveneKit.QUOTES`), Guild «У тебя отряд нормальный?» (`ExplosionKit.QUOTES`).
- *Wiz shop* «Лавка Виз» (`BANNERS.wiz`, second channel of the «Гильдия» tab, Tab or the «Набор» / «Лавка Виз» buttons at the bottom of the board): the signature Light Cones of the party, bought with the same manatite (or 160 jade). 5★ «Номер на бис» (`hagoromo`, Abundance, Aqua: HP +16%, healing +16%, a healed ally gets effect RES +20% for 2 turns, a failure no longer cuts her healing), «Взрыв на сегодня» (`manastaff`, Erudition, Megumin: ATK +24%, every chant adds 10% more to the Explosion, «Без сил» after the Ultimate lasts one turn less) and «Молитва о друге» (`adamantite`, Preservation, Darkness: DEF +28%, team damage +2% per Ecstasy of the wearer up to 12%, the whole team gets a shield for 2 turns when her Cover ends); the only featured 4★ is «Это был трактор» (`chunchunmaru`, Elation since 1.6, Kazuma: CRIT Rate +8%, EHR +12%, +3% damage per Luck, the attack after a successful Steal or Bind deals 20% more). Effects live in `hsr-battle` (`axelConeDmg`, `axelAfter`, the chant factor in `axelMult`, the collapse length, the Cover end in `endTurn`, the hagoromo branch of `heal`). Rules: cone pity 50 and rate 1.6%, the chosen cone with 75% (`coneFeatured`), a miss gives another heroine's cone or a standard 5★ cone (`partyPick` maps the stars to their cones, `guildParty` splits the miss) and the next 5★ is the chosen cone; 4★ Chunchunmaru with 50% (`wizFeatured4`) and a guarantee after a miss; no Kazuma cameo (the channel is a party banner). State `pityWiz`, `warpNWiz`, `wizStar`; history notes «конус другой героини» / «конус из стандарта» / «не «Это был трактор»». The screen is Wiz's shop: shelves of potions, a sign «Магические товары Виз», the chosen cone on the counter with a paper tag in the shop's voice (`stars[id].tag`), the shared manatite gauge (`manaGauge`, label shows the 75/25 split) and the cone picker; buttons read «Купить». A purchase plays the same `hsr-explosion` scene, where Kazuma steals every cone out of the smoke. The 1.3 letter and the site news mention the shop. Balance check (`bal_wiz.cjs` in the session scratchpad, level 30, 40 seeded battles, actions to win on gepard2 / svarog / cocolia1): Megumin with the staff 21.4 / 21.5 / 26.2 against Night on the Milky Way 24.0 / 20.6 / 27.9, Darkness with the armour 23.1 / 23.0 / 31.2 against Moment of Victory 24.8 / 23.6 / 33.7, Aqua with the mantle equal to the 4★ Post-Op Conversation on speed (her bonus is survival), Kazuma with Chunchunmaru within one action of Good Night.
- *Screen*: a guild hall wall with a cork quest board; the poster «Набор в отряд» of the chosen heroine with her portrait and a line in her voice, her figure in front of the board, a manatite crystal on the left that fills toward the hard pity and shows «Гарант», the rules and the featured 4★ on the right, and the three heroine cards at the right edge (↑/↓ or a click).
- *Scene*: `hsr-explosion` (see Modules). Heroes from outside the party have own Guild scenes in `CUTS` (Luna reads their Adventurer's Card, two or three gags with the KonoSuba cast, then a pose on their own backdrop in `poseBack`; `LINES`, `QUOTES`, `BEAT_SFX`, `RIM`, `BRIEF` for repeat pulls). The 4★ of version 1.4 get theirs one by one with approval: Lynx (card «следопыт» stamped «В РАБОЧИЕ ЧАСЫ» with a Survival bar; she makes camp in the guild stable where Kazuma and Aqua freeze in the hay, with a stove and cans; she tosses a chocolate energy bar to the collapsed Megumin, who jumps up; pose in the snowy night with the aurora, the tent and cans from `H.FoursScenes`; helpers `stableBack`, `choco`); Luka (card «кулачный боец» stamped «ОВОЩИ НЕ ЕСТ» with a Stamina bar; he punches flying cabbages out of the air with his mechanical fist, a combo counter on top; Darkness begs him to arm-wrestle harder until the tavern table breaks, «Ничья?»; pose in a boxing ring with ropes from `H.FoursScenes`); Sushang (card «рыцарь-новобранец» stamped «ДОБРОЕ ДЕЛО: 1/1» with a Kindness bar; her daily good deed is to cut the giant toad so it spits Aqua out; she listens starry-eyed while Kazuma inflates his duel with a Demon King general, the thought cloud shows a tiny Kazuma toppling a headless knight, Megumin adds «…»; pose in Xianzhou with red lanterns and her greatsword planted); Qingque (card «прорицатель» stamped «НОЛЬ ИНИЦИАТИВЫ» with a Gambling bar and falling jade tiles; at the tavern table she deals «Небесный нефрит» to Aqua and Darkness and wins, Aqua cries; Kazuma wins with his very first hand, the tiles glow gold, «Удача!», and the shocked Qingque loses her coins; pose in teal Xianzhou with a ring of floating tiles over a jade table; helper `tilesTable`); Tingyun (card «посредник» stamped «СДЕЛКА ЗАКЛЮЧЕНА» with an Eloquence bar and drifting fans; she talks Kazuma's 340 000 000 eris debt down to half until Aqua pops up and confesses the flooded guild cellar, the debt jumps to 510 000 000; her fan lightning doubles Megumin's Explosion, «×2», then Megumin lies flat as usual; pose at a Xianzhou harbour with sailing skiffs, red lanterns and a giant jade fan); Yukong (card «воздушный ас» stamped «ШЕСТЬ РАЗ НА КРАСНЫЙ» with a Piloting bar and drifting kites; she drives the guild cart behind a running lizard at full speed, Kazuma flies up on a bump while Darkness, tied behind, is dragged over the stones in bliss; the wind lifts Aqua by her kite, Yukong shoots the kite down and Aqua lands head first in a haystack; pose on the Xianzhou pier with a skiff and memorial kites; helper `cart`); Hanya (card «судья» stamped «ВСЁ ЗАПИСАНО» with a Strictness bar and falling talismans; she writes Kazuma's record line by line on a scroll, the Steal of Chris's underwear included, and stamps «ВИНА» while he protests in tears; her brush seal of Burden chains Darkness to two iron balls, Darkness blushes and asks for one more; pose in a karma hall with hanging talismans, candles and a gold brush ring); Xueyi (card «ловчая» stamped «ТЕЛО: КУКЛА» with a Grip bar and falling gears; under Kazuma's wanted poster she finds him hiding in a barrel despite his Lurk skill and drags him out on her chain; Darkness swings at a training dummy and hits Xueyi instead, gears burst out of the puppet body, Aqua's Heal does nothing, «ИММУНИТЕТ»; pose in the House of Shackles with chains from the corners and purple glow; helper `gear`); Guinaifen (card «уличная артистка» stamped «ИДЁТ ЭФИР» with a Charisma bar and fireworks; Aqua jumps into her guild stream with a party trick of water and potted flowers, the viewer count rockets to 4096, the chat cheers for Aqua and the tips fly to her; Darkness stands at a target board begging for closer throws while flaming knives land around her and a fire hoop flies past; pose on a night street with red lanterns, fireworks and a big fire hoop). The three 5★ girls open their scenes with the Adventurer's Card too (`advCard` takes `bigMax`, the length of the big bar in bar widths, default 3.6): Aqua «архижрица» with huge Strength, Dexterity and Magic, Intelligence near zero and a Luck bar that barely moves, stamped «УДАЧА: НИЖЕ НЕКУДА», water drops falling, Luna: «Магия и сила под потолок! А вот интеллект...». Megumin «архимаг»: Magic and Intelligence at the top, Strength and Dexterity at the bottom, the Explosion bar runs off the card, stamped «НАВЫКИ: ТОЛЬКО ВЗРЫВ», red sparks rising, Luna: «Магия и интеллект зашкаливают! Архимаг, не меньше!»; `lift` raises the bust so her hat fits. Darkness «крестоносец»: huge Strength, Dexterity almost zero, the Defence bar runs off the card, stamped «ПОПАДАНИЙ: 0», hearts drifting up, Luna: «Сила и защита огромные. А ловкость... почти ноль.». Duplicates still start from their short beats, so the cards play only on a new pull. March 7th also has her own Guild scene: card «лучница» stamped «ПРОШЛОЕ: НЕ ПОМНЮ» with an Optimism bar; she frames Kazuma, Megumin and Darkness for a group photo with a 3-2-1 countdown, Aqua jumps in front at the flash and the polaroid shows only Aqua; on a snowy field her ice arrow is absorbed by the Winter Shogun, «Поглощено!», and Kazuma drags her down to bow flat until the general leaves; pose among ice crystals with floating photos and snow (helpers `polaroid`, `winterShogun`). Dan Heng's Guild scene: card «копейщик» stamped «О ПРОШЛОМ МОЛЧИТ» with a Composure bar; he files the party records into a cabinet, the drawers «Казума: кражи», «Мегумин: взрывы», «Даркнесс: ...» slide shut, then «Аква: долги» bursts and buries him in paper; his Vidyadhara horns show as his water dragon swirls, Aqua answers with her own water column and the guild floods, Kazuma up to the chin; pose by a night sea under the moon with a water dragon trail. F8 «Сцены» has a seventh mode «Набор в отряд: появление».
- *Content*: Ultimate cutscenes (Steal of the enemy's weapon, the flood that freezes, the circles and the Explosion with a fall, the crusader under a rain of blows), Eidolon pictures («Гильдия Акселя», «Озеро и клетка», «Холм у старого замка», «Капустная страда») and first information pages from the KonoSuba wiki. All four looks are redesigned from the KonoSuba character art (see «Character redesign»). Light Cones of the Guild and a story chain are planned for later.
- *Balance check* (40 seeded auto battles, level 28, Gepard at the front line, Svarog, Cocolia; player actions to win): Megumin with March 7th, Natasha and Asta 29.8 / 27.0 / 43.1 against Himeko 28.3 / 27.2 / 38.7; Darkness 26.5 / 26.3 / 35.2 against Gepard 27.7 / 25.9 / 37.4; Aqua lands beside Bailu; Kazuma 29.7 / 35.6 / 53.7 against Pela 33.3 / 28.0 / 51.1; the whole party 30.5 / 28.0 / 38.0 against Himeko, Gepard, Bailu and Asta 30.8 / 25.9 / 34.3. Auto battle does not use Fusions, so the party Fusion is extra for a human player.

## KonoSuba x HSR 98: «Обмен отрядами» (event, version 1.4)

A story event with Kazuma's party. The design document and the stage log are in `HSR-KONOSUBA.md`.

- *Access.* The event opens after the Jarilo-VI chapter (`jarilo_done`). Once the player has boarded the Express, the Events tab «KonoSuba x HSR 98» offers early access (`ks_early`) that skips the rest of the story. The letter `v14_konosuba` (300 Jade, 3 manatites) arrives with either flag, and the 1.4 news on the official site describes the event.
- *Story.* Pom-Pom catches a signal, the Express lands on a meadow outside Axel in the Kingdom of Belzerg, and Kazuma talks the Trailblazer into swapping parties for one day. The Trailblazer leads Aqua, Megumin and Darkness (party level never below 40 in Belzerg). They take jobs from the Guild board: toads, the daily Explosion, the lake cage. Interludes put Kazuma in the Express party: porter on the train, the goblin camp, Keele Dungeon, where Herta's doll wakes the Destroyer Prototype. Kazuma takes his party back for the gate fight. After the epilogue in the Guild and dinner at the mansion, everyone sees the Express off. The clip `k0n0SuBa3Ed` plays instead of credits.
- *Mechanics.* The toads swallow allies, Aqua purifies the lake from a cage the enemies attack, Megumin rides on the leader's back after the Explosion, the Explosion is rated on three scales, and the Adventurer's Card teaches Steal, Bind and Drain Touch. Herta's reports count the misses of both parties, and the final report adds a «Прикрытие» column. The Prototype fight has three phases: Aqua breaks the barrier, Megumin breaks the legs, Kazuma steals the core.
- *Rewards.* Four event milestones: the swap, three board jobs, Keele Dungeon, and the finale, which gives Kazuma for free.
- *After the story.* Kazuma writes in Messages (`ks_kazuma_after`), Kazuma signs the guest book, the photo wall gets the meadow photo, the party lives in the mansion, and the Events tab replays the clip credits.

## Кон-Тон (Cosplay Convention Crisis, version 1.4)

A banner collab with the heroines of Cosplay Convention Crisis. The design document and the stage log are in `HSR-CCC.md`. Facts and lines come from the scripts in `content/CCC` (Thursday, Friday, Saturday); scenes that are not for all ages are left out.

- *Heroines.* Макото Майн (Hunt, Imaginary) steals part of the enemy's turn and leaves through a portal to come back with a guaranteed crit. Аманда Миллер (Preservation, Wind) turns into the Pink Defender without spending the turn and covers allies with Heart Cocoons that burst into Wind damage. Таллея из Авии (Remembrance, Quantum) puts Shackles on enemies and fights with a gynoid squad; if she falls once, the gynoids carry her to the recovery capsule.
- *Banners.* The «Кон-Тон» Warp tab has the main stage (`con`, featured heroine 50%, 5★ by pull 60) and the artists' alley (`alley`, cone rules). Both spend badges or Jade. Kazuma can appear with a 1% cameo and his own cut.
- *Badges.* Registration at the desk gives the first badge, the event «Четыре дня Кон-Тона» gives 10 over four game days, and the letter `v14_conton` «Кризис на Косплейном Конвенте» gives 10.
- *Music.* The banner screen plays the intro of the minus track; the pull scene plays the minus, and each heroine reveal plays her theme (`content/music/ccc-*.mp3`).
- *Standard 5★ cuts.* Welt, Himeko, Bronya, Gepard, Clara, Yanqing and Bailu play their own four-bar convention scene in the Spotlight show (minus track, no theme, jury card on the pose beat, one beat on a repeat). The lines come from their own voice tables.
- *4★ cuts.* The 19 original 4★ (March 7th to Guinaifen; collab heroes excluded) play a two-bar convention scene: setup bar, punch half-bar, pose half-bar with the jury card; a repeat plays the punch beat.
- *Extras.* Each heroine signs the guest book once she is on the roster. The official site has a paragraph in the 1.4 notes, and Скрепыш has a line for each heroine.

## «Хитрые зайцы» (Signal Search A agents, version 1.4)

Three A-rank agents of Zenless Zone Zero come as 4★ and are the featured A agents of both Signal Search banners (Miyabi and Yanagi); Dan Heng left Signal Search. The design document and the stage log are in `HSR-HARES.md`.

- *Agents.* Билли Кид (Erudition, Physical) builds the С колена stance with every Basic in a row (+25% damage a level, two levels); the Skill, a skipped turn or a heavy hit resets it. Ally Chain Attacks and follow-ups give him Star Knight charges for the Ultimate. Николь Демара (Nihility, Quantum) opens the Energy Field: enemy DEF −20% and every foe counts as a neighbour of the target, so blast attacks hit everyone; two case shots after her Skill or Ultimate hit as a blast and keep the field up. Энби Демара (Hunt, Lightning) counts a Series of three Basics and then strikes with Гром; her Skill right after the third hit deals 64% more Toughness damage.
- *Chain Attack.* While Anby is in the team, every break stops the battle in the `chain` phase (`b.chainReady()`, `b.chain(ally | null)`, events `chainAsk`, `chainSkip`, `chainEnd`). The player picks a partner (not the breaker, each ally once per blow) who hits the broken foe and its neighbours with their own element. A splash that breaks another foe asks again. The chain comes before Persona's One More; the auto battle picks the ally with the highest ATK.
- *Squad link.* Each hare's extra ability works only with an ally of the same element or another hare (card mark: a double link).
- *Banner and economy.* Featured A 50% with a guarantee after a miss; their amplifiers («Звёздные рыцари не проигрывают», «Как купить солнце», «Трек без названия») are the featured 4★ cones of the amplifier channel. The letter `v14_hares` «Хитрые зайцы на связи» brings 10 Encrypted Master Tapes. The hares drop nowhere else.
- *Signal Search scene.* Like every other agent: the stage reveal, then the close-up with the bust in front of the TV that switches the hero's channel (`SHOWS.anby`, `SHOWS.nicole`, `SHOWS.billy`, three programmes each, from the public image to a private moment) and the agent's official line.
- *Art.* New look keys in `hsr-art`: coat `volt`, `gyaru`, `kid`; back `twinwave`; face `visor` (`paintVisor`, a black faceplate with eye slits); figure field `legGap` (spreads the legs by whole pixels); accessories `headphones`, `voltblade`, `stockband`, `garter2`, `tagbelt`, `tiebows`, `furhem`, `ringbelt`, `bunnybag`, `mechcase`, `rips`, `tallspikes`, `chainbelt`, `kneeskull`, `sneakers`, `pantseam`, `revolvers`.
- *Extras.* Ultimates, Fusion scenes («Хитрые зайцы» в деле, Главная тема «Звёздного рыцаря», Катана в залог), Eidolon pictures named after the Mindscapes, «Информация» pages, arcana (VII, X, XVIII), Rating Pistol scores, Скрепыш lines and three paragraphs in the 1.4 notes on the official site.

## Character information

The sixth section of the character screen. The panel is wider than the others (x 388, 244 px) and holds three pages: the file (full name, other names, faction, home world, Path and element, «О персонаже», «Знакомство» with crew notes), the story (parts I to V) and the voice (talk lines, then battle lines). All pages are one scrolling list of rows of 14 px built every frame by `infoRows`; headings, cyan questions, lavender quotes and dim lock rows are row styles. Story parts unlock by character level (1, 20, 30, 40, 50), lines about other characters need them in the roster, crew lines need `express_intro`, the Eidolon line needs E1, the max level line needs level 60.

Writing rules: texts come from the official Russian character pages (fandom `<Имя>/Лор`: «Знакомство с персонажем», «О персонаже», «Истории персонажа», «Голос»), edited by `game-human-style.md` (no em or en dashes, dialogue dashes turned into «quotes» with the speech tag after a comma; names follow the game spelling, for example Химико, not the wiki's Химеко), with nothing invented. Lines and story parts about places and events this game does not reach (March 7th's sword lessons with Yanqing, Penacony, Amphoreus, later forms of characters) are left out. Characters are filled one per step with user approval; done: March 7th, Dan Heng, Trailblazer (two story parts only: the others are Penacony and Amphoreus), Himeko, Welt, Natasha, Serval, Asta, Arlan, Herta, Gepard, Bronya, Seele, Sampo, Hook, Pela, Ruan Mei, Bailu, Yanqing, Clara, Miyabi, Yanagi, Phrolova, Scar (not playable in Wuthering Waves, so no official stories or voice lines exist: his dossier, stories and lines are translated from the official Data Bank entry, the «Sacrifice» token and his own lines in the quest «Ominous Star»; the line «О Фроловой» was written for this game on user request, answering Phrolova's official line about him). All characters are done. ZZZ characters: the dossier comes from the RU zenless-zone-zero wiki (`<Имя>/Досье`: details, intel, impressions, cooperation notes), voice lines are translated from the EN `Hoshimi Miyabi/Voice-Overs` page because the RU wiki has none; ZZZ has no talk lines, so «Общение» holds idle, Lost Void, Quality Time and promoter lines. Shots: `info_<key>_<page>_<top row>_<level>[_end]` (`_end` sets the story flags the unlocks use).

Guild heroes (KonoSuba) have no game dossier and no voice table. Their pages are built from the KonoSuba fandom wiki (RU article sections Внешний вид, Личность, Экипировка, Характеристики, Навыки, Предыстория, Взаимоотношения, Интересные факты; EN `<Name>/History` for checks): the dossier adds `extra` blocks for stats, skills and equipment, the five story parts retell the wiki in order, and «Голос» uses only the wiki quotes plus the lines the hero already says in this game (gacha intro and cuts, Warp cameo, Ultimate, Fusions), so nothing is invented. Lines about another party member are gated with `need: { char }`.

## Supply (Снабжение)

The phone tile «Снабжение» took the slot of «Управление»; Controls moved to the side panel under Settings. Supply opens after `express_intro`, like the Warp. No real money and no Oneiric Shards: the outfit shop sells for Stellar Jade.

- Currencies (`state.starlight`, `state.embers`) come from Warps only, with the numbers of the original: a duplicate 4★/5★ character gives 8/40 Undying Starlight and an Eidolon, a duplicate with all six Eidolons gives 20/100 Starlight (result `max`, it replaced the old +3000 credits; old `credits` log entries still read), a 4★/5★ Light Cone gives 8/40 Starlight, a 3★ Light Cone gives 20 Undying Embers. `addChar` pays Starlight for duplicates from any source (Warp, shop, story join). Warp results carry `glow {cur, n}`, the log entry keeps `glow`; `ui.resultTag(x, y, r, freshLines)` is shared by all six Warp shows and adds the gain line, the standard rack sums the gains. `H.GLOW`, `H.charGlow(res, rar)`.
- Old saves without `state.shop` get back pay for their logged Warps (`fixGlow`); the first Supply visit shows a notice that explains both currencies and the back pay (`shop.intro`, `shop.back`).
- The monthly limits of the original refresh every real hour here (`shop.hour`, `shop.bought`, `Game.now()` so tests inject a clock); the 4★ pair in the Starlight shop changes every hour too. Starlight: Special Pass 20 and Star Rail Pass 20 without limit, three standard 5★ cones 600 (one each per hour), the 4★ pair 140 (one each, refused at Eidolon VI). Embers: Special Pass and Star Rail Pass 90 (was 160, five each), «Журнал приключений» ×2 for 6 (80), «Путеводитель путешественника» 18 (30), credits ×10 000 for 12 (80). Tracks of Destiny, Condensed Aether, relic EXP and ascension materials are left out: the game has no traces, cone levels or materials for them.
- Outfit classes (plan `HSR-OUTFITS.md`): the class of an outfit follows what it changes, not the class of the hero. A 4★ outfit (`H.OUTFIT_TIER[4]`, 840 Stellar Jade, kind «Наряд») changes only the hero: field, battle, menus, phone, cards, the cut-ins of ultimates and Fusions, scenes and Warps, with the usual staging. A 5★ outfit (1680, «Особый наряд») also brings its own cutscenes of the ultimate, the Skill, every Fusion of the hero and the Warp. `rarity`, `price` and `kind` are not written in the outfit entries: hsr-shop sets them from `cuts` (`cuts: true` only when the outfit has the full cutscene set). «Алый урок» (`cuts: true`, 1680) is the first 5★; the test demands the full set (Ultimate, Skill, Warp and a cut for every Fusion of the hero) for every outfit with `cuts`. The showcase of a 5★ outfit has the button «Катсцены» (V): `openOutfitCuts(id)` plays `outfitCutSteps(id)` in order (Ultimate, Skill, Fusions, Warp) with demo foes; Enter or Esc goes to the next one, Backspace closes. Letter `v16_outfits` (no gift, after `express_intro`), site patch `v16h`, the wiki outfit article shows the row «Катсцены» and the section «Катсцены облика» (`cines` in hsr-wiki-data). The showcase, the purchase window and the «Наряды» panel show the tier line `OUTFIT_TIER[r].perk`, cards use `G.RARITY_BG`. Old saves (`state.otv` missing) get 840 jade back for each owned outfit of `H.OUTFIT_REFUND` (the seven that cost 1680; not March 7th's letter gift and not «Алый урок»), the sum waits in `shop.outfitBack` for `outfitBackNotice` when Supply opens. Scenes: the `lookFor` cache in hsr-cine keys string ids by the worn outfit too, and the ults of Makoto, Talleah, Billy, Nicole and Anby draw `P.look`. Site patch `v16o` «Патч 1.6: Облики 4★ и 5★».
- Outfits: `state.outfits` (owned ids), `roster[k].outfit` (worn id). `G.lookOf(id, game)` returns the worn outfit look, so the outfit shows on the phone, map, battle portraits, ultimates, Fusions and cutscenes that pass the game; calls without a game keep the default look. «Зарождение весны» (Nascent Spring) for March 7th is an outfit. Art: coat `frost` (bare shoulders, petal collar, ribbon choker `bow`, halter straps, bodice fading to `fade`, flower `flower`/`flowerCore`), keys `tiara`, `skirtfade`, `snowflakes`, `detsleeves` with `detAt` (where the detached sleeve starts on the arm). Reference: fandom `Character March 7th (Preservation) Nascent Spring Game.png`. «Ученица Алого народа» for Megumin is her pink school uniform from «Kono Subarashii Sekai ni Bakuen wo!»: short bob, coat `shirt` with `tie`, `bigbelt`, yellow `capelet` with a red cord (`capeletFur`), black `witchcape` with red `capeIn` and an orange zigzag hem (`capeZig`, also on the back sprite), black `bellsleeves` with red `cuff`, brown knee `boots`, no eyepatch or staff. «Письмо с цветами сливы» (Plumblossom Letter) for Ruan Mei: teal qipao with the high slit (`frontpanel`, `tails` with dark `tailsLining`), white lace off-shoulder bodice (new key `laceband`, bust/figure/sprite), puff sleeves, white lace collar with a pink bow, white plum branch embroidery down the front (new key `plumbranch`, petal centres `plumCore`), a long teal glove on her left arm only (`opera1` with `operaSide: 'r'`) and a white glove on the other hand, plum blossoms in the hair, pearl garter. Reference: fandom `Character Ruan Mei Plumblossom Letter Game.png`. «Айдол-дебют не удался» for Welt, the idol stage costume from «My Idol Debut Comedy Is Wrong»: grey `jacket` with grey `puff` shoulders and white `sleeve`, white shirt with `ruffles` and `buttons`, red `bow`, headset (`headset`), black layered `skirt` with grey `ruffle3` tiers, light `hem`, white `petti` and white crosses (`snowflakes`), `bigbelt`, white knee `socks`, pink shoes with white `heelbow`; glasses stay. «Невеста Дастинесс» for Darkness, the wedding dress from KonoSuba season 3 episode 11: hair in a side `bun` updo, silver `tiara` with a blue gem, red `xclips`, long `veil` (new key, drawn behind the hair in `bustBackHair`/`figBackHair` and on every sprite side), strapless coat `bridal` (sweetheart bodice with lace edge), `pearls`, white `longgloves`, new skirt `low: 'ballgown'` (floor-length bell skirt with ruffle tiers and white roses from `roseCol`). «Спортивный костюм» for Kazuma : his green tracksuit from the first episode (green `jacket` with black `sleeve`, `zip`, `piping`, `hemband`, black V shirt, green pants with outer white stripes `pantstripe` + new flag `stripeOut`, white shoes with maroon `soles`). It is also free: the KonoSuba finale step `give` and the quest «ks_q7» reward carry `outfit: 'kazuma_jersey'` (`Game.give` handles `outfit`, the Missions card shows it), and `gift: 'ks_done'` in the outfit makes `ownsOutfit` true for saves that finished the story earlier; `giftNote` is shown on the locked card. «Пляжная богиня» for Aqua, her navy bikini from the season 3 acrylic stand art: new coat `bikini` (halter cups with a centre tie and gold beads from `bead`, low side-tie bottoms with white `trim` bows, bare arms and midriff; bust, figure and sprite), `low: 'none'`, `barefoot`, the `hairloop` bun stays, new key `hairtassel` (navy hair ties with gold beads: on the side hair in the bust, on the back hair beside the thighs in the figure via `figBodyAcc`, because the head pen of the figure sets `p.fig`). «Модная зайка» for Nicole , her pink and teal street look from the user's reference art: new coat `bunnyvest` (pink crop top `inner` with a white `print`, cropped teal denim vest `top` with white fur armholes `lining`, popped collar, gold `chain`, bare midriff; bust, figure and sprite), denim `shorts` with `furhem` and `ringbelt`, yellow `tiebows`, gold `armband`, pink `detsleeves` from `detAt` 0.66 with white gloves, green `bunnybag`, new key `cattail` (leopard tail with `spot` colour, figure in `figBackAcc` and every sprite side), black `garter` with a green bow, pink `boots` to the calf with `bootband` and `soles`, white `mechcase` (new optional `caseBand` for the case stripes). «Благородное цветение» (Dignified Blossom) for Miyabi: new coat `haori` (black haori slipped off the shoulders with white `lining` and gold `trim`, long side panels to the knee, white bodice `inner` with a high gold-banded collar and gold V, black obi `obiCol` with a gold shell fan and two tassels, black front apron with gold edges and a gold four-petal crest, gold ginkgo leaves `leaf` on the white knee `skirt`; bust, figure and sprite), black `bellsleeves` with white `cuff`, white `hairblossom`, black ankle boots `ankle` + gold `anklerim`; the fox ears stay, the sheath strap is dropped because it crossed the obi. «Косплей Чоколы» for Makoto: new coat `maid` (dark dress `top` with pink `trim`, white blouse `inner` with a gathered neckline and pink lacing, dark corset with pink buttons, bare midriff, high dark collar with a pink bow and heart, white `apron` with a pink inner border, scallops and pink corner bows, big white back bow; bust, figure and sprite), new head key `maidcap` (white frill cap with a `capRibbon` line and bows and light blue `capTail` ribbons, also on the sprite), `catband` ears with pink `neon` insides, white `puff` sleeves and `ruffcuff`, `cattail` (brown, `spot` close to the tail colour), dark `stockings` with new flag `stockcats` (cat ear points on the stocking tops). Each outfit names its showcase backdrop in `scene` (`snow`, `blast`, `plum`, `stage`, `chapel`, `street`, `beach`, `boutique`, `ginkgo`, `netcafe`, `mine`, `walk`, `eclipse`, `trashking`; painters in `SCENES` of hsr-ui-shop); the outfit list on the right shows two cards and follows the selection. «Старший бригадир шахты» for Hook (scene `mine`) is an original outfit drawn after the Underworld workers of the original game (fandom `NPC Senior Mine Cart Operator.png`, `NPC Apprentice Mine Cart Operator.png`, `NPC Balaway the Miner's Lamp.png`): they wear no hard hats, so Hook gets the apprentice fur cap (new key `minecap`, navy crown, `capFur` brim, brass headlamp, `molebadge` on the side), a beige shirt with white arm bands (`straps`) and cuffs, and dark navy bib overalls (new coat `overalls`: bib from the chest, leather `strap` braces with metal clasps, white side tabs `tab`, badge on the bib) over `low: 'pants'` tucked into boots, plus an oil lamp on the belt (new key `beltlamp`, `lampGlow`; the key `lantern` is a chest emblem of another character). The `mine` backdrop follows the Great Mine shots: dark rock pillars with snow caps, golden-orange geomarrow (not blue), a rusty truss gantry, hanging oil lamps, rails and an ore cart. «Выгул особого назначения» for Arlan (scene `walk`) is an original outfit about Peppy, drawn after the original Peppy (fandom `NPC Peppy.png`, `Light Cone Woof! Walk Time! Artwork.png`, `Profile Picture Peppy.png`): Peppy is cream, not brown, with tall pointed ears that are pink inside, a fluffy chest and tail, and a staff pass on a blue lanyard with a bone clip. Arlan gets a warm cream hoodie (new coat `hoodie`: kangaroo pocket, hem band, drawstrings `cord`, optional key `pawprint` on the chest) with the hood up (new key `doghood`, Peppy ears with `earIn`; `cathood` stays Lynx's), his blue trousers, white sneakers with blue `soles`, the security `armband` and a pass on a blue `lanyard`; the gauntlet, pauldron, harness and boots are dropped. The hoodie is warmer than his white hair, or the hood and hair merge. The `walk` backdrop is a Herta Station corridor (dark blue wall panels, a wood strip, grey dotted floor, a window with a planet) with Peppy sitting by the stand with his own blue leash in his mouth. Outfit card names shrink from 11 to 9 px when they do not fit the card. «Алый урок» for Himeko (scene `eclipse`) is the Valkyrie battlesuit Vermilion Knight: Eclipse of Murata Himeko from Honkai Impact 3rd, the world Welt comes from, drawn after the honkaiimpact3.fandom.com renders (`Vermilion Knight - Eclipse (Front).jpg`, `Vermilion Knight - Eclipse.png`, `Vermilion Knight - Eclipse Chibi.png`): bare shoulders and a high black collar with an orange gem, a black bodysuit with red plates and glowing orange crescents (new coat `valk`, colours `top` plates, `inner` suit, `glow`), black fur on one shoulder (`fur`), red gauntlets with black bands (`gauntlets` + new option `gauntletBand`, default stays white), black thigh stockings and red knee boots, long dark translucent coat tails to the ankles (`tails`, `tailsTo: 'ankle'`), a black and orange flame ornament in the hair (new key `flamehorn`) and the red greatsword with an orange edge on the back (new key `greatsword`: bust, figure and field sprite). The `eclipse` backdrop is a red sky with a black eclipse, burning ruins, embers and glowing cracks. «Властелин Мусорного Царства» for the Trailblazer (scene `trashking`) puts Caelus or Stelle inside a Lordly Trashcan from Penacony (fandom `Enemy Lordly Trashcan.png`, `Profile Picture Trashcan.png`, `Dreamscape Pass Sticker Lordly Trashcan.png`): a silver can with vertical ribs and dark bands up to the chin (new coat `trashcan`; the figure draws the can in `figBodyAcc` over the arms, with dark side holes and forearms in `sleeve` coming out of them), the domed ribbed lid with an arch handle worn askew (new key `trashlid`) and the baseball bat in the right hand (new key `bat`). The backdrop copies the trashcan painting: a sunburst, clouds and rows of silver cans with one golden can. Trailblazer outfits: `char: 'trail'`; `H.outfitLook(id, gender)` builds on `G.trailLook(gender)` (cached per gender, optional `lookF` for Stelle only), `wornLook` passes `state.gender`, and `G.lookOf` for `trail`, `trail_p` and `@` returns the worn outfit first. UI calls pass `state.gender`; the «Обычный наряд» preview uses `G.trailLook`. Long showcase titles shrink from 18 to 14 px.

One outfit is granted and put on when the character is in the crew (`Game.debugOutfit(id, wear)`); «Все наряды игры» only adds every outfit to the collection.
