## Pets (Питомцы)

Supply has a third section «Питомцы» (after the outfit shop). A pet costs Stellar Jade, has no effect on battle or stats and walks next to the party leader in the open world. The first pet is Chomusuke from the KonoSuba crossover (1200 jade); her look follows the official anime art (konosuba.fandom «Chomusuke», file `Chomusuke Anime.png`): dark plum fur, pale yellow oval eyes, a red cross on the forehead, small bat wings, a big round head and short legs.

- State: `state.pets` (owned ids), `state.pet` (the pet that goes along or null), `state.petLog[id] = {steps, pets}` (steps walked together, times petted). The walker itself (`game.pet`) is not saved: it appears again on the next frame.
- Following: every tile the leader leaves goes into the pet's trail; the pet walks the trail and stops one tile behind (two behind when the leader is in `fears`, Aqua for Chomusuke). The pet speeds up with the backlog and flies (Chomusuke has `fly`) when three or more tiles behind; more than nine tiles away (teleports, map changes) it appears again next to the leader. It never stops on solid tiles, exits or triggers. The pet stays out of the Simulated Universe and out of its `home` map (the KonoSuba mansion, where Chomusuke already sits by the fireplace).
- Idle: after a stop the pet turns to the leader and sits; every 3.5 to 8 s it grooms, yawns, looks around, wanders to a tile one or two steps from the leader or (with `fire`) sneezes a puff of flame. With a liked leader (Megumin, Kazuma) it sometimes purrs with hearts; with a feared one (Aqua) it hisses. After 24 s without moving it curls up and sleeps (9 s with a `lazy` leader: Kazuma), until the party moves or it is petted.
- Space on the tile in front of a sitting pet pets it (purr, hearts, a short bubble) or wakes it up; with Aqua leading, Chomusuke hisses instead. Every third petting shows a line of the party leader if that leader has one (Megumin, Kazuma, Aqua, Darkness). The HUD prompt reads «Погладить: Чомусуке».
- Supply section: a stage (Kazuma's mansion living room) runs a 16 s demo of the pet (walk in, sit, groom, sneeze, purr, sleep, fly away), the description and price or counters on the left, the habits on the right, one button: «Купить» → purchase window → «Взять с собой» / «Позже»; owned pets toggle «Взять с собой» / «Оставить на Экспрессе».
- Sounds: `meow`, `purr`, `hiss`, `sneeze` in hsr-audio. Menu icon `pet` (paw).
- Adding a pet: an entry in `H.PETS` with `look` colours for the cat painter (or a new painter in hsr-ui-pets if it is not a cat), texts without dashes, `home` if the pet lives on a map.
- Shots: `pets` (PT=seconds picks the demo moment), `petsowned`, `petfield` (PA=act, PL=party), `petsheet` (every frame at 4× and 2×).

## Express Supply Pass and Contracts (Снабжение экспресса, Контракты)

Supply now has five sections in the order of the original: «Снабжение экспресса», «Магазин», «Магазин обликов», «Контракты», «Питомцы». Supply still opens on «Магазин»; `H.SUPPLY_SECTIONS` lists the section ids. Sources: EN fandom «Express Supply Pass», «Contract Shop» and the pages of every Herta contract; official Russian names from the screenshots of the original.

- *Express Supply Pass* (`hsr-pass.js`, `hsr-ui-pass.js`, `H.PASS`, `H.PASS_RULES`). The only item of the game bought for (fake) real money: $4.99, paid through the in-system Steam client like a game. The game has no Oneiric Shards, so the 300 shards of the original become 300 Stellar Jade at once; then 90 jade on every day the player enters the game for 30 days (day starts at 04:00, `H.passDayNo` reuses `H.dayKey`). A day without a visit is lost, as in the original. Each purchase adds 30 days, at most 180 days in total (`passCanBuy` refuses when more than 150 are left; a late order is cut at 180). State `state.pass = { end, last, orders, bought, got }` (day numbers; `orders` keeps the Steam order ids so one order is delivered once). Game API: `passLeft` (days including today), `passOwed` (claims still to come), `passReady`, `passClaim`, `passGrant(orderId)`.
- *Steam bridge.* `hsr98-app.js` passes `store: { buy(offer), take(skus) }` to `H.UI`. `buy` calls `appSteam.openInGameBuy('hsr98', offer, onPaid)`: no Steam client → `gameManager.showInSteam` (steampowered.com) and `'nosteam'`; otherwise the window `steam_txn_<sku>` «Покупка в игре» with the card form of the game Buy window (card autofill, no promo codes). Payment writes an order into the Steam ledger `settings.orders` in `win98_steam` (`recordOrder`), and `takeOrders(game, skus)` hands undelivered orders to the game once. The game takes orders in `storeSync` (after the payment callback and every second in the field through `passTick`, so an order paid while the game was closed arrives on the next load), grants them, writes the autosave and shows «Оплата прошла, пропуск активен». The standalone and APK editions have no store: the button explains that the pass is sold only in the Steam version. Steam news «Покупки в играх» (20.11.00).
- *Daily reward.* `passTick` (hook in `UI.update`, field only) claims the day when the field is idle and shows a punched ticket with +90 and the days left. The section shows the medallion (painted in `paintMedal`), today's state, «Осталось N дн.», the total received, the two reward columns, the gold button «Купить/Обновить: 4,99 US$» and «Правила» (R).
- *Contracts* (`hsr-contracts.js`, `hsr-ui-contracts.js`, `H.CONTRACTS`). Seven Herta contracts of the original Contract Shop, paid with Stellar Jade at the original Oneiric Shard prices. Limits: `period: 'week'` refreshes every real hour, `period: 'version'` every six real hours (orange «Ещё …» tag), `period: null` is once per save. Credits are a fifth of the original, Sparse/Refined/Condensed Aether map to Встречи/Журнал/Путеводитель as in the conductor rewards, Lost Crystal (relic EXP, not in the game) is replaced by Traveler's Guides.
  - «Великая Герта: Контракт стратегического сотрудничества» 2680, 5 per version: 10 Special Passes + 3 of 4: Self-Modeling Resin ×2, Relic Remains ×1600, Tears of Dreams ×480, «Путеводитель путешественника» ×200.
  - «Великая Герта: Контракт стратегической поддержки» 720, 5 per version: 2 of 4: Resin ×1, Remains ×800, Tears ×240, Путеводитель ×100.
  - «Герта»: Контракт на снабжение Первопроходцев 880, once: Путеводитель ×60, 24 000 credits. На восполнение топлива 240, 1 per week: Fuel ×2, 3000 credits. На совместные приключения 180, 2 per week: Журнал ×25, Встречи ×60, 5000 credits. На поставки в путешествиях 330, 8 per version: Путеводитель ×20, Журнал ×15, 12 000 credits. На снабжение материалами 2 660, 5 per version: Tears ×150, 12 000 credits.
  - State `state.contracts[id] = { slot, n }`; API `contractList`, `contractView`, `contractBuy(id, picks)` (picks must be `pick` distinct option indexes). Screen: 4×2 grid of cards (chest art per `look`, painted in `paintChest`; tags «Доступно», «Ещё …»/«⟳ …», «Достигнут лимит»), an info strip with the contents, and a purchase window where ←→ and Enter pick the options and ↓ jumps to the buttons.
- *Tears of Dreams* (`tears`, «Слёзы мечтаний», 4★, hsr-traces). They replace missing Path materials in Traces at the original rates 1/3/9 for 2★/3★/4★ (`H.TEAR_RATE`). `traceNode` returns `tears` (how many would be spent); `trace` spends the Path materials the player has and covers the rest with tears. The cost panel shows a tears row, covered Path rows turn gold, and a line says how many tears will be used.
- Icons: menu `pass`, `contract`; item `tears`.
- Shots: `supplypass`, `supplypassactive`, `supplypassmax`, `passrules`, `passgot`, `passdaily`, `contracts`, `contractbuy`, `contractbuy2`, `tracetears`. The whole OS flow (Steam installed, game window, payment, delivery, autosave) was checked with `MP_PAGE=index.html MP_READY='!!(window.winManager && window.appSteam)' node tools/maxpayne-shots.cjs <out> <steps.cjs>`.

## Pom-Pom: conductor, level rewards and Express missions

Sources: fandom `Pom-Pom` (dialogue in the Parlor Car), `Trailblaze Level` (reward table), `Messages/Pom-Pom` and the Russian pages `Пом-Пом`, `Сообщения/Пом-Пом`, `Награды проводника`, `В поисках записи` for the official Russian lines. The project writes Pom-Pom in the masculine, so feminine forms of the Russian wiki are changed; Химеко becomes Химико.

- Levels: characters now level up to 80 (`MAX_LVL`); the «Макс. уровень» voice lines unlock at 80. Trailblaze Level is a separate account level, `state.tl {lvl, exp}`, cap 70 (`H.TL_MAX`), its own curve `H.tlExpToNext` (the old 40·lvl + 2·lvl² curve). Characters use the original EXP table `H.CHAR_EXP` (`H.expToNext`, 5 797 920 EXP from 1 to 80); battle EXP is counted in Trailblaze units and converted per character with `H.charExp(n, lvl)`, so the pace per level stays as before. Exp books give the original 1000/5000/20000. Old saves scale the EXP inside the current level once (`state.xpv`, `fixExpScale`). It gains the full battle EXP of every win, 200 per Trailblaze mission and 300/150 per finished Companion/Adventure mission (`H.TL_QUEST_EXP`). `Game.trailLevel()`, `addTrailExp(n)` (emits `{t: 'tl'}`, the UI toasts it), `setTrailLevel(lvl)`. Old saves take the level from the Trailblazer's level, capped at 70 (`fixTrailLevel`); a state without `tl` is treated as old, so tests that set `roster.trail.lvl` before `new H.Game` still work, and later changes go through `setTrailLevel`. Message thresholds (`lvl` in chat threads), Trailblazer's Will and the phone card read the Trailblaze Level.
- Level rewards (`hsr-conductor.js`, `H.TL_REWARDS`, levels 2-70): the original table mapped to this game's items. Credits are a fifth of the original; Travel Encounters and Sparse Aether become «Встречи в путешествиях», Adventure Log and Refined Aether «Журнал приключений», Traveler's Guide «Путеводитель путешественника», Energy Drink ×10 three «Термос Среброгривых», Quid Pro Quo the 4★ Abundance cone «Беседа после операции»; Star Rail Pass, Fuel and Stellar Jade stay as they are; relic gifts keep their sets and slots (Knight 3★ set at 17, Passerby 4★ at 26-27, Knight 4★ at 32-35, 5★ at 44 and 47; star count maps to relic level 3/6/9). Tracks of Destiny, ascension and trace materials and Ectostella are left out because the game has no use for them. Claims: `tlRewardState(lvl)` (`got`/`ready`/`locked`), `tlReadyLevels()`, `claimTl(lvl)`, `claimAllTl()`, `state.tlGot`. Rewards open only after the mission «Награды проводника» (`pc_rewards_done`); every level reached before it waits.
- Screen «Награды проводника» (`hsr-ui-conductor.js`, pending `{ui: 'tlrewards'}`): Pom-Pom's booth on the left (level, EXP, a line from Pom-Pom, «Получить всё» = F), a ticket book on the right. Each level is a ticket with a stub; a claimed ticket is punched and stamped «Погашен». ↑↓ move, ←→ page, Enter claims, wheel and scroll bar scroll. On the Express a red badge hangs over Pom-Pom while tickets wait.
- Talking to Pom-Pom: one random ambient line from the original Parlor Car set, then a menu: «Как там мой уровень Освоения?» (after the mission), «Расскажи о пассажирах.», «Что ты здесь делаешь, проводник?» (once, after the Jarilo-VI story), «Что у тебя есть в продаже?» (the old shop), «Мне пора.». «О пассажирах» lists Dan Heng, Himeko, March 7th, Welt, Pom-Pom and the Express; each pick tells the next untold fact of the 13 «About» topics (`H.POM_FACTS`, `state.pomFacts`), then the name gets «(всё рассказано)» (`pf_<who>`) and picks repeat a random fact. Script step `{lines: 'method', arg}` splices the lines a Game method returns.
- Adventure missions on the Express: «Награды проводника» (`pc_rewards`, message `pompom_miss` at level 12 after `plains_met`, the official text; ends with a tutorial and the reward screen), «Не забывай о доме» (`pc_home`, message at level 17), «В поисках записи» (`pc_disc`, from Pom-Pom's menu; Serval in the Neverwinter workshop answers even when she is in the party, then Pom-Pom writes about the record), «Старые друзья, новые знакомые» (`pc_guests`, message at level 25 after the Jarilo-VI story, read the guest book). «Мимолётные огни» (level 21) was already in the game. Pom-Pom's signature is the official «Приходите ко мне за наградами Первопроходца!»; three short praise messages arrive at levels 30, 45 and 60. With no Trailblaze mission, a Companion mission is tracked before an Adventure mission.
- Debug: F8 → «Предметы» has a row «Ур. Освоения» and a button «Уровень Освоения».
- Shots: `tlrewards`, `tlrewardsfirst`, `tlrewardsend`, `pomtalk`, `pomfield`, `debugitems`.

## Version 1.5

- Title and every version label read 1.5: `BRAND.disc` in hsr-core («CD-ROM v1.5», standalone «v1.5»), the Steam catalog size note, the IE search snippet and the site (header note, ticket «1.5 от 27.11.2000», download page, Steam paragraph, worlds intro, setup wizard). The Ковчег fan site keeps «версия 1.4»: its pages are dated before the update.
- Letter `v15_supply` «Версия 1.5: Снабжение и проводник Пом-Пом» covers Supply, the interface fixes (Controls on the side panel, 12 squads, the scrolling picker, favourites) and the Pom-Pom rework. It gives 300 Stellar Jade and the outfit `march_spring` («Зарождение весны»); `give.outfit` only adds it to the collection, March 7th does not put it on. Mail attachments draw outfits with `ui.outfitCell` (shared with the Missions reward row).
- Site article `v15` (27.11.2000) describes the same update with the numbers of the game: Starlight per duplicate (`H.GLOW`), the Supply cones and prices (`H.SUPPLY`), every outfit with its price (`H.OUTFITS`), the Trailblaze Level cap (`H.TL_MAX`) and the four Express missions; `tests/hsr-site.test.js` now loads hsr-shop and hsr-conductor to check them.
- The setup wizard draws Pom-Pom with `G.figureCanvas(SPEAKERS.pompom.look, 2)`; the old call `H.PomPom.figure` stopped existing after the 2D redraw.
- Shots: `mail15`, `mail15got`.

## Version 1.6: nine 5★ of versions 1.x

Plan and status: `HSR-16.md`. Jing Yuan, Luocha, Dan Heng: Imbibitor Lunae (`imbibitor`), Fu Xuan, Jingliu, Topaz and Numby (`topaz`), Huohuo, Argenti and Dr. Ratio join the event banner (`BANNERS.ev.stars`, 4★ trios of the first original run) and the event cone banner (`BANNERS.cone.stars`, signature cones). They never drop from the standard pool.

- Modules: `hsr-fives.js` (kits, signature cones, Fusions, banners, letter `v16_fives`, Battle mixin `fives*`), `hsr-fives-scenes.js` (Ultimates, Fusion cutscenes, Warp intros including Dan Heng's first one, Eidolon pictures, banner backdrops `H.FivesScenes.BACKDROPS`, cone art; shared art `lordGiant`, `waterDragon`, `divRing`, `tailSpirit` and more), `hsr-fives-info.js` (information pages), `hsr-ui-fives.js` (battle UI). Loaded after the matching `hsr-hares*` file in both pages and the tests. `H.WarpIntro.KIT` exports the intro helpers (`pose`, `stand`, `hand`, `fly`, `zoom`, `cuts`).
- Engine hooks in hsr-battle: `fivesStart/Tech/Fx/Preview/Ability/Pay/UltReady/Spend/Ult/Turn/End/Share/Hit/Dmg/CritD/Vuln/After/Follow/Summon/Leave/HoldUlt/Auto/UltTarget`, `fivesWave` on a new wave, `basicLocked`, and an action counter `actN` bumped by every `act` event (once-per-action effects use it).
- Summons: the Lightning-Lord (`id: 'lord'`, SPD 60 + 10 per hit over 3, 3 to 10 follow-up hits) and Numby (`id: 'numby'`, SPD 80) live in `b.summons` like Hecate; UI events `lord` and `numby`, spots `fivesPos` (Numby stands next to Topaz's card), rail icons `fivesIcon`.
- Verbs: Jing Yuan charges the Lord; Luocha's prayer triggers by itself at half HP (2 Luocha turns cooldown) and 2 Abyss Flowers open the zone that heals on every ally action against a foe; Imbibitor Lunae raises the Basic ATK tier with a free turn-keeping Skill and pays 1 to 3 SP with the attack (Squama first); Fu Xuan's Matrix moves 65% of a hit to her through her own defence (`fivesShare`), Knowledge uses `hpAdd`; Jingliu's Spectral Transmigration locks the Basic ATK (`basicLocked`, the UI picks the Skill) and drains 4% HP of teammates per attack; Topaz's Proof of Debt is a follow-up vulnerability (`fivesVuln`) and follow-ups on the debtor advance Numby once per action; Huohuo gives teammates 20% of max energy (`ult.support` keeps the auto battle from waiting for low HP); Argenti has 180 energy and two Ultimates (90 and 180); Dr. Ratio's follow-ups wait in `fivesQueue` and resolve in `followUps()`.
- Banner picker: more than five heroes scroll (`PICK_VIS`), arrows with hidden counts, wheel and ↑/↓.
- Version 1.6: letter `v16_fives` (300 jade, 10 Special Passes), «CD-ROM v1.6», site article `v16` (18.12.2000) and every 1.5 label on the site, Steam note and IE; Clippy lines for the nine; Rating Pistol scores from the original.
- Bot (scratch `bal.cjs`, level 30): each hero sits between Kafka/Blade and Seele; Fu Xuan needs a healer such as Bailu or Luocha against Cocolia.
- Art: all nine looks redrawn from the official renders and reference sheets, one hero per step with approval (stage 6 of `HSR-16.md`). New look keys in hsr-art.js, each drawn on the bust, the figure and the sprite: `gemclip`, `ipcfront` (Topaz), `jyarmor`, `lionpad` (Jing Yuan), `blindfold`, `lunarbodice` (Jingliu), `talismancap`, `hhcharm`, `ghosttail` (Huohuo), `fxhairpin`, `starorb` (Fu Xuan), `lcdrape`, `lctassels` (Luocha), `ilfront`, `ilsash` (Imbibitor Lunae), `agarmor` (Argenti), `laurel`, `ratoga`, `racape`, `rasandal` (Dr. Ratio). New look fields: `bangsR` mirrors the bangs, `streakShape: 'sidel'` is a long left streak, `tuck` draws boots over pants.

## Traces and Eidolons (patch 1.6)

Plan, decisions, numbers and the report of every stage: `HSR-TRACES.md` (Russian). Sources: Mar-7th/StarRailRes (`content/hsr-traces/extract.json`, written by `node tools/hsr-traces-import.cjs fetch`) and the EN fandom wiki.

- *Traces.* Every kit has the original tree: Basic ATK 1–6, Skill, Ultimate and Talent 1–10, the Technique, bonus abilities A2/A4/A6 and ten stat nodes linked as in the original. There is no ascension: phase N of the original is character level 10 + 10N, the last two stat nodes open at 75 and 80. Caps per phase follow the original (Skill 1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 10). State: `roster[k].tr[kitId] = { lv, on }`; the Trailblazer has one entry per Path.
- *Costs* are the original items and counts; credits are `T.trCredit` (0.2) of the original. Trailblazers pay the 4★ table.
- *Battle.* Party, trial, guest, Simulated Universe and Forgotten Hall specs carry `tr` and `eid`. With `T.trLevels` the damage, heal and shield multipliers of Basic ATK, Skill and Ultimate follow the original level curves (talents follow them only after a kit is reworked). Foes facing a team with traces get the phase curve `T.trFoeHp`/`T.trFoeAtk`, so a team with traces at the level cap fights the story bosses as before; units without `tr` fight at level 10 as before.
- *Eidolons.* A kit without `eidFx` keeps +4% base HP, ATK and DEF per Eidolon; with `eidFx` the flat bonus goes away, Eidolons III and V add ability levels (up to 12 and 7) and the kit code reads `eidOn(u, n)`. The Trailblazer keeps one Eidolon count per Path (`eidBy`, swapped in `setPath`) and raises it with Shadows of Destruction or Preservation.
- *Materials and sources.* Path lines from the crimson buds (new: Destruction in the Storage Zone, Preservation in the Supply Zone, Remembrance in the Simulator only), foe lines from battles by enemy family and level and from the golden buds, Echo of War materials (four Echoes, three rewards per real hour on all of them), Tracks of Destiny (level rewards 41–66, Supply, Simulated Universe worlds 3 and 4), Shadows (level rewards 19 and 30, shops with stock, story), the Omni-Synthesizer (3 → 1 synthesis, 2 → 1 exchange), the Embers shop for the Xianzhou lines, the letter `v16_traces`. Old saves get the traces up to the level cap and back pay for what they already passed (`state.trBack`, notice once).
- *Economy model.* `node tools/hsr-trace-econ.cjs` (`balance`, `economy`; `probe(lvl)` from code): a first full 5★ takes about 0.9 hours of Trailblaze Power (600 per hour).
- *Echoes of War.* «Семя бессмертия» (Phantylia the Undying, Abundance Lotus in phase one, Destruction Lotuses with Havoc on the locked target in phase two) and «Старые кошмары червоточины» (Skaracabaz in two phases, Multiply spawns a Gnaw Sting per hit and a break during Multiply breaks the swarm), `hsr-echoes.js`.
- *Debug.* F8 «Персонажи» has the row «Следы» (до потолка уровня, все узлы, сбросить), applied with «Применить» and «Выдать всех»; F8 «Предметы» grants every material.
- *Shots:* `maticons`, `chartraces`, `chartracesstat`, `chartracesbonus`, `chartracestrail`, `chartracesnew`, `charactions`, `invmats`, `synth`, `synthx`, `simdrops`, `simecho`, `traceback`, `echophantylia`, `echophantylia2`, `echoswarm`.
- *Kits.* Each kit is reworked one at a time (stage 8 of `HSR-TRACES.md`): its own level tables (`H.TRACES[kit].curves`), bonus abilities behind `hasBonus`, real Eidolons and `eidFx: true`. The effects live in `hsr-trace-fx.js` (`H.TRACE_FX[kit]` with `start`, `break`, `kill`, `hit`, `crit`, `dmg`), called through `kitFx` (events also `act`, `after`, `aggro`, `shieldTurns`, `freezeChance`, `froze`, `allyTurn`). Reworked: Trailblazer (Destruction), March 7th, Dan Heng, Himeko, Welt, Herta, Asta, Arlan, Trailblazer (Preservation), Bronya, Seele, Gepard, Clara, Yanqing, Bailu, Natasha, Serval, Sampo, Hook, Pela, Ruan Mei, Kafka, Silver Wolf, Blade, Lynx, Luka, Sushang, Xueyi, Tingyun, Hanya, Qingque, Yukong, Guinaifen, Jing Yuan, Luocha, Imbibitor Lunae, Fu Xuan, Jingliu, Topaz, Huohuo, Argenti, Dr. Ratio, Miyabi, Yanagi, Billy, Nicole, Anby, Phrolova, Scar, Kazuma, Aqua, Megumin, Darkness, Yu, Yosuke, Chie, Yukiko, Kanji, Rise, Makoto, Amanda, Talleah.

## Interastral Guide and Synthesis (patch 1.6)

Plan, decisions and the report of every stage: `HSR-GUIDE.md` (Russian). Sources: the author's screenshots of the original and the EN/RU fandom wikis (Interastral Peace Guide, Daily Training, Survival Index, Omni-Synthesizer, Relic Remains, Synthesis Materials).

- *Phone.* Tile 7 «Путеводитель» (icon `ipg`, after `express_intro`) and tile 8 «Синтез» took the places of Save and Load; Save and Load moved to the side panel right under the Settings gear. A disabled panel item is dim, says why at the bottom and toasts on Enter.
- *Synthesis* (`hsr-ui-synth.js`, `openSynth(close, tab, { resin })`): tabs Q/E «Расходные предметы», «Синтез материалов», «Обмен материалами», «Реликвии»; a 3 × 5 rarity grid with a filter (F), a brass medallion, «Необходимые материалы», a quantity slider (A/D, Home/End, mouse). Material synthesis 3 → 1 and exchange 2 → 1 stay free; the exchange takes its source from a picker (Enter, Tab) or «Автодобавление» (R). The inventory button, the Party car device and `{ui:'synth'}` open it.
- *Consumables* (`hsr-synth.js`): ten synthesis materials of the original (`kind: 'synmat'`, rarity 2 and 3, field `src`) from broken crates by world, battles in the Storage Zone, Robot Settlement and Backwater Pass, golden Calyxes and shops; `H.RECIPES` for the twelve dishes (cooking is always cheaper than buying). A recipe is known from the first copy of the dish or from a `rcp_<dish>` item (shops of the Express, Belobog, Boulder Town and robot No. 123, three chests); known recipes leave the shops, a second copy pays 300 credits.
- *Relics.* Selling a relic became salvage: 150 credits as before plus Relic Remains (`remains`) 10 + 4 × level; mass salvage by level from the inventory (R). Crafting (Trailblaze Level 40 or after meeting the Forgotten Hall) costs 100 Remains for any cavern or planar set and slot; Self-Modeling Resin picks the main stat; 800 Remains buy a resin twice per real hour. The old Forgotten Hall maker is gone: its shop opens this tab.
- *Interastral Guide* (`hsr-guide.js`, `hsr-guide-data.js`, `hsr-ui-guide.js`, `openIpg(tab)`): a light brochure with five binder tabs (Q/E) and «Правила» (I).
  - Ежедневная тренировка: resets every real hour; four fixed tasks (log in, spend 120 Power, a successful Contact, a Simulated Universe run) and three from a pool (kills, Weakness Breaks, Synthesis, Traces, Technique, a consumable, a Fusion); points up to 500 by «Получить», five steps with the original rewards by Equilibrium Level (credits × `T.dtCredit`, synthesis materials and «Журнал приключений» for the missing items). Counters are prototype wrappers (`dailyCount`), battle events through `startBattle`.
  - Индекс выживаемости: the development target (missing Trace materials, EXP books and credits with their sources, recommended sets from «Ковчег», a relic score S–C) and every domain of the Simulator by category with «Войти». «Войти» follows the teleport rule of the map (anchor or safe map), picks the anchor or landing nearest to the entrance or enters directly when the zone has none, then opens the domain window or runs the machine script.
  - Космический раздор: the four Simulated Universe worlds with rewards and «Перейти к испытанию».
  - Озарённые светом сокровища: the Forgotten Hall with stars, the 15 reward tiers (F), the weekly Echo, the shop refresh, the shop (B) and «Перейти к испытанию» to the mirror.
  - Первопроходческий опыт: the acts and chapters of the Trailblaze missions with the Express photo wall pictures and a chapter page; chapters say «Акт».
- *Caverns and Shadows* drop a pair of sets (`sets` in `H.CALYX`, `rollRelic(lvl, sets)`): Fist and Spike Champion + Thief, Drifting Passerby + Musketeer, Providence Genius + Knight, Rime and Gust Glacial + Eagle, Scorch and Blaze Firesmith + Wastelander, Fulmination and Mirage Knight + Thunder; the Simulator's plain Cavern stays random.
- *Badges:* `alerts().guide`, `guide_daily`, `guide_treasure`, `guide_cosmic`.
- *Shots:* `phonesave`, `phoneguide`, `synth_mat`, `synth_swap`, `synth_swapsrc`, `synth_pick`, `synth_food`, `synth_foodlock`, `inv_synmat`, `synth_relic`, `synth_relicplanar`, `synth_craft`, `synth_crafted`, `inv_salvage`, `synth_lock`, `guide_daily`, `guide_daily_full`, `guide_daily_steps`, `guide_index`, `guide_index_echo`, `guide_index_orn`, `guide_index_cavern`, `guide_index_target`, `guide_target`, `guide_target_sets`, `guide_target_none`, `guide_target_pick`, `guide_su`, `guide_su_locked`, `guide_fh`, `guide_story`, `guide_prologue`, `guide_chapter`, `guide_rules`, `guide_locked`.

## Fuel Vouchers, domain series and the build target (patch 1.6)

Plan, decisions and the report of every stage: `HSR-GUIDE-2.md` (Russian). Sources: the author's screenshots of the original and the EN fandom wiki (Fuel Vouchers, Tutorial/Fuel Voucher Details, Calyx, Stagnant Shadow, Cavern of Corrosion, Echo of War, Reserved Trailblaze Power, Interastral Peace Guide/Survival Index, Version/3.0, 3.2, 3.4).

- *Fuel Voucher* (`hsr-domain.js`): item `fuelv` «Топливный купон» (4 stars, key item, `note`), cap `H.FUELV_MAX` 1000 through a `give` wrapper. For now the only source is Pom-Pom's letter `fuelv_gift` (100, after `express_intro`); the vouchers never expire.
- *Runs* (`startCalyx(id, tier, n)`, `H.CALYX` kinds by `H.guideCalyxKind`): a Calyx runs 1 to 24 waves of three foes in one battle (10 power and one `calyxReward` per wave); a lost or fled run still pays and rewards the cleared waves, a retry continues with the waves left. A Stagnant Shadow runs up to 8 battles in a row and a Cavern up to 6 (`calyxRun`: each win pays and rewards at once; `calyxNext`, `calyxStop`, `calyxRepeat`); an Echo stays one battle. The whole run must be affordable at the start.
- *Quick power recovery* (`powerAuto`, `state.guide.autoPower`, on by default): the shortfall comes from the reserve only, never from Express Fuel or jade; without enough reserve the power window opens with the shortfall picked (`openPower(need)`).
- *Quick Farm* (`quickFarm(id, tier, n, { target })`): Calyxes (one voucher per 10 power) and Shadows (three) only, 1 to 10 tries, power on hand only (no reserve), not in battle, the Simulated Universe, the Forgotten Hall or a script. A try is one `calyxReward` plus the Trailblaze EXP of an average fight of that domain (`domainExp`); no character EXP, no enemy loot, no kills, so manual fights stay the better yield. It counts toward the daily power task. When the domain feeds the build target, the tries stop once the target has enough (`stopped: 'target'`, `left`).
- *Domain window* (`hsr-ui-domain.js`, `openDomain(id, close, { fromList, enter })`): difficulties I–IV on a brass rail with a pixel picture of the domain, description and «Быстрое получение» on the left; place, name, difficulty plate, possible enemies with weaknesses and levels, drops, the recovery toggle and the run slider on the right; «⚡×cost ┊ Начать испытание». Count and difficulty are remembered per domain (`state.guide.runs`). The Simulator list opens it with Enter; one-domain calls (map props, the guide) open it directly; `enter` turns it into a view with «Войти».
- *Battle* (wrappers of `drawBattle`, `drawResult`, `battleKey`, `updateBattle`): plate «Волна k / n» or «Испытание k / n»; the domain victory window shows the loot grid, a 3 s countdown with «Дальше» / «Завершить» inside a series, «Итоги серии» at the end and «Ещё раз» (R). The defeat window says how many waves or wins paid out.
- *Quick Farm window* (`openQuickFarm(id, tier, { target })`, `openQuickResult`): the question with domain and difficulty, tries with −/+/maximum, the cost band, «Отменить» / «Подтвердить» (or «Пополнить», «Нет купонов»), then «Получено» with the loot, or «Материалы для цели собраны» with «Завершить» / «Продолжить».
- *Survival Index* (`guideIndexList`, `guideTargetIds`): rows that feed the target carry its bust and come first; a voucher button (F) on Calyx and Shadow rows; a loupe (L) opens the domain view without travel; «Сменить цель развития» with the target bust under the categories; voucher buttons on missing-material rows; «Цель достигнута» with a one-time toast (`state.guide.done`).
- *Build target*: the catalog lists own event-Warp 5-star heroes, the crew, then every hero of the event, collab and standard Warps not in the crew (`warpSources`, `guideWarpInfo`, «Прыжок» and «Стандарт» plates with the banner name); a stranger counts from level 1 and empty Traces. The target stands first in «Персонажи» with a crosshair mark. «Только рекомендуемые Следы» (R, `state.guide.recOnly`, own rule): Skill, Ultimate, Talent and the bonus abilities only.
- *Traces*: a voucher button on a missing Calyx or Shadow material, R on the node; for a hero who is not the target it asks «Сделать целью» / «Только получить».
- *Texts*: site news `v16d` with a Clippy reaction, F1 help `hsr-fuel`, AstralWiki article `sys:sim_express_fuel`, the «Ковчег» relic page, the guide patch letter and the Index rules, the Controls screen («Испытание», new guide keys).
- *Shots:* `mail_fuelv`, `inv_fuelv`, `domain_calyx`, `domain_crimson`, `domain_shadow`, `domain_cavern`, `domain_echo`, `domain_locked`, `sim_list`, `battle_waves`, `series_next`, `series_total`, `win_again`, `lose_waves`, `quick_farm`, `quick_got`, `quick_power`, `quick_target`, `guide_index_quick`, `guide_shadow_quick`, `guide_target_btn`, `guide_target_quick`, `guide_target_done`, `guide_view`, `guide_target_pick_warp`, `guide_target_warp`, `chars_target`, `guide_target_rec`, `traces_quick`, `traces_ask`.
- *Economy*: `node tools/hsr-domain-econ.cjs` compares manual fights with Quick Farm per 10 power.

## Trailblaze Power (patch 1.6)

The player asked for 600 maximum power, a full refill in one real hour, a 2400 reserve that fills in four real hours as in the original, and an exchange.

- *Numbers* (`H.TUNING`): `powerMax` 600, `powerRegenSec` 6 (600 per real hour), `powerReserveMax` 2400 (four hours at the same rate), `powerTop` 3000 (no source can raise power higher), `powerPack` 60 (one fuel or one jade refill), `powerJade` [50, 50, 75, 75, 100, 100, 150, 150] (jade refills per real hour, the price rises with each one). The jade limit resets every real hour, like the Daily Training and the Supply shops.
- *Real time* (`hsr-game.js`): state `power`, `powerAt` (time stamp of the last whole point), `powerRes`, `powerBuy {hour, n}`. `powerSync()` counts whole 6 s steps from `powerAt` with `Game.now()`, so power also comes back while the game is closed or in battle. Below the maximum the steps fill power; at or above the maximum they go into the reserve until 2400. A clock that goes back resets `powerAt` and gives nothing. `update()` calls it every frame; old saves lose `powerAcc` in `fixPower()` and start the clock on load.
- *Exchange* (`powerFromReserve(n)` 1 to 1, `powerFromFuel(k)`, `powerFromJade(k)`, `powerJadeCost(k)`, `powerInfo()`): all of them may go over 600 up to `powerTop`; over the maximum new power still goes to the reserve. The fuel item from the bag uses the same limit.
- *UI* (`hsr-ui-menu.js`): `ui.powerChip(x, y)` is the counter with a «+» box and a violet reserve line under it (phone, inventory, Simulator, Simulated Universe terminal, Interastral Guide); a click opens `ui.openPower()`. The window shows power with the next point and the time to full, the reserve with its time to full, and three sources (reserve, Express fuel, Stellar Jade) with −/+/«Макс»; keys ↑↓ source, ←→ amount (60 for the reserve, 1 for fuel and jade), Tab maximum, Enter exchange, Esc close. The Simulator opens it with P, and when a domain or a Simulated Universe reward needs more power than the player has.
- *Shots:* `powerx`, `powerjade`, `calyx`, `items`.

## Item icons (patch 1.6)

The staged plan and every stage report are in `HSR-ICONS.md`. Reference icons come from the fandom wiki and StarRailRes (`tools/hsr-icon-refs.cjs` downloads them to `%TEMP%/hsr-icon-refs` and builds comparison sheets); no original image is in the repository.

- Native size: `G.itemCanvas(id, set, size)` draws the 32-unit art with `G.pen(g, size / 32)` at the size the screen asks for (cached per size), so 18, 40 and 64 px icons keep square pixels. `itemIcon` and `itemSilhouette` use it.
- Halos and rays are dotted (`halo`, `rays` in `G.iconHelp`): they are recorded as marks and painted as a checker pattern under the icon without outline; translucent `rgba` below 0.5 is not used in item art (tests check `RELIC_PIECE`, `MAT_ART` and the redrawn items). Silhouettes skip marks. `fine(p, fn)` draws details only at 32 px and above.
- Relics: every Cavern set has its own four pieces and every Planar set its own sphere and rope (`hsr-relic-art.js`). The inventory grid shows a slot badge in the corner of each relic (`G.slotBadge`). Cavern sets use the official Russian set and piece names (`RELIC_SETS[set].pieces`).
- Materials: the 14 Trace material lines (`MAT_ART`), Echo of War, Tracks of Destiny, Shadows (`shadowOrb`), Relic Remains, Self-Modeling Resin (art in `hsr-ui-memory.js`) and the 10 synthesis materials follow the original items. Tier 1 has no halo, tier 2 a dotted halo, tier 3 a halo with rays and sparkles.
- Consumables keep the game's own dishes, drawn in the style of the original food icons; the energy items keep the lightning badge. Recipe tickets are cream paper with a rarity stripe and the dish drawn by its own art at 0.4 scale.
- Exp books: «Встречи в путешествиях» 1000, «Журнал приключений» 5000, «Путеводитель путешественника» 20 000 (official names and amounts). Characters use the original EXP table `H.CHAR_EXP`; see «Pom-Pom: conductor, level rewards and Express missions» for the Trailblaze Level curve.
- Review: shots `relicsheet(g)`, `planarsheet(g)`, `matsheet`, `foesheet`, `echosheet`, `synmatsheet`, `foodsheet(g)`, `expsheet`, `shoprecipe`; `iconcheck` writes a JSON report (relic silhouette overlap per slot, tier growth, small icons, similar dish silhouettes).
- The official site article «Патч 1.6: Межзвёздный путеводитель и Синтез» (`v16g`) has a paragraph about the new icons, names and EXP.

## Path of Elation (patch 1.6)

Plan, decisions and the report of every stage: `HSR-ELATION.md` (Russian). Sources: the EN fandom wiki through its MediaWiki API (Path, Aha Instant, Punchline, Certified Banger, Elation DMG, the Elation stat, Merrymake, the Elation characters, the cones), Russian names from the RU wiki.

- *Path.* `H.PATHS.elation` («Радость», aggro 100), the chalice emblem, the Warp sign (pink rings and rainbow confetti), arcana 0, the material line `elat` (the three issues of «Пушистый скиталец»), the crimson bud `cx_elation` (opens with an Elation hero, like the Bud of Remembrance), the guide preset `elation` (own element set, Vonwacq, speed and crit).
- *Battle rules.* Punchline is shared by the team: 1 per Elation hero at the start and after each Instant, more from abilities and from failures (a resisted effect, Darkness missing, Aqua failing, Megumin collapsing, being swallowed by a toad; 2 each, once per action, 6 between Instants). With Punchline on the team Aha joins the action bar as an untargetable summon, SPD 80 + S1/5 + S2/10 + S3/20 + S4/40 of the fastest Elation heroes. The Aha Instant dispels freeze, stun and swallow from Elation heroes, runs every Elation Skill by `elSkill.order`, shows the damage card and turns all Punchline into Certified Banger (2 turns of its owner per portion; 20 at the start). Elation DMG = level base (`elationK` × break base) × ability multiplier × crit × (1 + Elation) × Punchline multiplier 1 + 5P/(P+240) × (1 + Merrymake) × DEF × RES × vulnerability × broken × reduction; ATK and DMG boosts do not count. With no Elation Skill alive Aha casts «Да будет смех»: 10 random hits, 1 DMG and 2 Energy to allies, never downs anyone. Punchline and Aha keep through waves.
- *Kazuma.* Elation, Wind, 4★. Each Skill mode +3 Punchline; Steal and Bind add 20% Elation DMG with Certified Banger; the Great Heist +5 Punchline, Aha 15% closer (40% at E4), +40% Elation DMG with Banger. Elation Skill «Камень, ножницы, бумага!»: 40% to all, then 6 rounds of 15% on random enemies, a won round (50%, 75% with A6) hits twice and gives 1 Punchline (9 rounds and 2 Punchline at E6); it follows the Talent level. Technique: the team acts first, +3 Punchline. Luck is gone; Fusions give Punchline (Ice Trap 4, the party Fusion 10). Traces: A2 +3 Punchline, A4 as before, A6 double roll; three Elation Boost nodes 4/6/8%. Chunchunmaru is an Elation cone (CRIT Rate, Elation, Elation DMG ignores 10% DEF). Auto battle keeps the Ultimate until 10 Punchline or until Aha acts next, at most two turns.
- *Cones.* «Благоприятный день» (4★, ref 21065, «Выбор»: CRIT Rate +12–20%, after an Elation Skill Elation +12–20% up to twice) and «Насмешка» (3★, ref 20023, «Разгулье»: Elation +16–32% during the Instant), in the standard Warp pool, data in the `HSR-CONES.md` contract.
- *UI.* The Punchline counter (mask, value, multiplier) sits to the right of the Skill Points and opens the rules card; Aha has its mask on the rail; Elation heroes show «Хит N» under the portrait and «Радость» instead of break effect on the details page; Elation DMG numbers are rainbow coloured. Cut-ins follow the Ultimate cinematic setting. The enemy Contact mood «Радость» is now «Довольство».
- *Texts.* Letter `v16_elation` from Pom-Pom with Elation materials, news «Патч 1.6: Путь Радости» on the official site, the Path role on the site, the wiki and the Ковчег guide.
- *Balance.* Bot on Gepard, Svarog and Cocolia (boss HP ×6, ATK ×1.5, 16 seeds, actions to win, old → new): Kazuma with March 7th, Natasha and Asta 18.4 / 14.4 / 43.4 → 17.1 / 14.6 / 42.9; the Axel party 7.2 / 4.9 / 14.0 → 7.3 / 5.3 / 14.6; Kazuma with Himeko, March 7th and Natasha 9.2 / 7.4 / 16.7 → 8.8 / 6.9 / 17.4. An Instant comes about every 1.3 Kazuma turns.
- *Shots:* `elhud`, `elinstant`, `elrps`, `elcard`, `elhelp`, `elaha`, `elahaend`, `ellaugh`, `elkazuma`, `eldetails`, `elskills`, `eltraces`, `eleid`, `elcutkazuma`, `elcutlaugh`, `eldebug`, `elcone`, `elmail`, `elsign`, `elcalyx`, `matsheet`.

## Squads and favourites

- The Team screen keeps 12 squads. `state.party` is always the active squad; `state.squads[i]` holds the other squads and `state.squad` is the active index. `Game.squadOf(i)`, `setSquad(i)` (refused while guests are in the team, in the Simulated Universe, or for an empty squad) and `startSquad(i, key)` (the first member makes an empty squad active).
- Number tabs 1-12 sit at the top (click or Q/E). A green diamond marks the active squad. An empty squad can be viewed; the battle party stays until a character is added to it.
- The window button at the bottom left (or Tab) opens «Обзор отрядов»: all 12 squads with portraits and levels, scroll bar, wheel, ↑↓ + Enter or a click picks a squad.
- The character picker shows 5 rows of 4 cards inside its window, scrolls with keys, wheel and scroll bar, and names fit their cards.
- Favourites: `roster[k].fav`, `Game.isFav/toggleFav`. The Characters screen has a star toggle under the sections (click or F). `rosterKeys()` sorts favourites first, then the active party, then catalog order, so favourites lead the roster strip, the picker and every other roster list. Favourites carry a gold star in the corner of their portraits.
- Shots: `team`, `teamsquad`, `teamempty`, `teamover`, `teampick`, `teampickend`, `charfav`.

## Verification

- `node tests/hsr-site.test.js` (`npm run test:hsr-site`) checks the site: routing, every page with clean text, every canvas spec against the game art, facts against the game data, news order, the whole download and setup flow, cancelling both, the guestbook and the wiring in Explorer, the desktop, Outlook and AGENTS.md.
- `node tests/hsr98.test.js` checks the data, validates every map (row widths, defined marks, reachability from the spawn point), checks every script reference, tests the battle engine, runs boss win rates with the expected party and plays the full story from the opening to the ending, including the Simulator and a save/restore. The mission part checks the Trailblaze catalog against the story flags, the rewards shown against the rewards given, the quest conditions, the old-save fix, tracking, and plays Hook's whole chain (the wrong and the right suspect, both auction outcomes); the UI part plays the mission screen. The Simulated Universe part checks the data (Paths, blessings, curios, worlds, domain maps, events), blessing and curio effects against the damage formula, the Resonance of every Path, the quest from Herta's letter through the tutorial run and the report, repeat rewards, waiting claims, world locks, a failed run, every event with its first three answers and a 4-run balance check per world. The UI part plays the terminal, a run with the Resonance, the shop with a blessing bought over it, enhancement, curios, an event, the summary and a lost run on the stub canvas.
- Browser check: `node tools/hsr98-shots.cjs <outDir> [scenes]` starts the server and headless Edge, builds a mid-game save and writes PNGs of any scene (`title, express, dialog, menu, char, charcone, charrelic, skills, eidolon, team, items, invcones, invrelics, warp, warpres, warp1, shop, rumors, calyx, archive, saves, battle, boss, contact, result, defeat, busts, figures, sprites, icons, coneart`; Simulated Universe: `suterm, supath, supathlock, suteam, sufield, suportal, surespite, suframe, suevent, suaeon, subless, subless2, sucurio, sushop, suenh, suend, suendfail, surun, surun2, subattle, sures, sulose, suicons`; station and quests: `office, control, questhud, questmission, questnav`; Messages: `chats, chatpick, chatphoto, chatsticker, chatlist, chatempty, chatnote`; Missions: `missions, missionstrail, missionscomp, missionsadv, missionstrack, hudhook, campfight, campask, campcrates, campauction, rivetdig, rivetjunk, boulderhook`). Warp scenes: `warpc<3|4|5>_<sec>` renders the cinematic at a time for that door colour (for example `warpc5_10.3` is Pom-Pom's leap), `warpv_<id>_<rar>_<sec>` renders a reveal of a character or Light Cone (`warpv_bronya_5_0.6` is the star prelude), `warprack<rar>` renders the results rack; `title_<sec>` renders the title scene at a given time (the comet crosses at 0–1.4 s of every 13 s); `warphist`, `warphist5` (5★ filter), `warphistp` (fifth page), `warphist0` (empty log) and `archwarp` render the warp history. `at_<map>_<x>_<y>` renders the field with the player on a given tile (`at_express_4_4` shows Pom-Pom next to the Warp terminal). `cine_<id>_<shot>_<sec>[_m|_f]` renders one moment of a cinematic (`cine_c_nanook_2_1.5` is Nanook's eye opening), `cg_<id>` a dialogue backdrop and `dlg_<script>` the first line of a script. `ZOOM=arlan,trail_f:fig` with the `zoom` scene enlarges busts or figures. Enemy and map scenes: `foes1`–`foes4` (battle sprite, icon and field symbol of every enemy), `foeicons`, `foe_<id>`, `bgs` (all battle backdrops), `map_<id>` (the field view of a map), `full_<id>` (a whole map scaled to the screen), `cg_<id>`. Navigation scenes: `nav_<map>` (the area chart, all anchors active), `nav_<map>__<kind>` (the card of the first marker of that kind, for example `nav_j_belobog__exit`; `goalmark` selects the objective without the card), `navz_<map>` (two zoom steps closer), `navarea_<map>__<area>` (the map opened on another area), `starmap[_<map>]`, `hud_<map>` (field HUD with the minimap) and `mission`. Energy scenes: `hudenergy` (party cards with empty, partial and full bars), `hudcharge` (the fill flash right after Искра геоядра and Сбитень), `useenergy` (the target list of Термос Среброгривых). Version 1.1 scenes: `phonerail` (the side panel in focus), `evnotice`, `events` (day 3, day 1 claimed), `events7`, `eventsdone`, `will`, `willdone`, `mail`, `mailgot`, `mail0` (empty), `maildel` (the confirm dialog), `warpev`, `warpstd`, `warphistev` and `charsheet` (bust, figures, sprites and portraits of `CHAR`, Ruan Mei by default). Version 1.2 scenes: `warpcone`, `warpconeseele` (the Light Cone event banner), `warpseele` (the event banner with Seele chosen), `charbailu`, `charyanqing`, `charclara`, `skillsbailu`, `skillsyanqing`, `skillsclara` and `battlenew` (Clara, Yanqing, Bailu and March 7th in battle). Battle input scenes: `battlepick` (Skill picked on a blast target), `battleboard` (after several turns, damage board filled), `battleheal` (keys from env `KEYS`, default `E`; `KEYS="  E"` shows March 7th's shield preview on an ally), `bosspick` (preview on the boss bar); `hsrShots.battlePlay(enemies, bg, party, keys, boss)` presses the keys one per player turn (space casts, `>` moves the target, letters are `Key<letter>`). Ultimate cutscene scenes: `ult_<id>_<beat>_<sec>[_all|_one|_none]` renders one moment of a character's cutscene (beat 0 cut-in, 1 action, 2 outcome; the targets default to the Ultimate's own), `ultlive_<id>_<sec>` casts the Ultimate in a real battle and renders the screen after that many seconds, `settings` shows the Settings window on the cutscene row. Fusion cutscene scenes: `fus_<fusion id>_<beat>_<sec>[_all|_one|_none]` renders one moment (beat 0 cut-in, 1 action, 2 hit, 3 team beat; `fus_f_landau_1_1.1` is Serval's second chord), `fuslive_<sec>` casts «Поезд отправляется!» in a real battle and renders the screen after that many seconds. Eidolon picture scenes: `eid_<char>_<n>[_<star>]` (the Eidolons section with n Eidolons, a star in focus), `eidv_<char>_<n>` (the full-screen picture), `eida_<char>_<from>_<to>_<sec>` (the assembly of the pieces after `from` up to `to` at that time) and `eidsheet` / `eidsheetg` (all 20 pictures in colour or in black and white on one sheet).
- The MIDI checks parse every file of a MIDI track, run the player against a stub audio context over two loops (the intro plays once, notes are cut at the loop end, every channel builds voices) and check which fights use which track: the theme of every boss, `Game.battleTheme()` on every map, a real field encounter on `j_plains` and `hs_base`, and that every theme started by a cinematic with `keepMusic` or by a `{music}` step reaches its battle unchanged.
- Manual browser work goes through `window.hsrui`: `newGame()`, `handleKey(k, code)`, `game.loadMap(id, mark)` and `game.startBattle({ encId })`.

## Path and element emblems

The nine Path emblems (`PATH_ART`) and seven element emblems (`ELEM_ART`) in `hsr-icons` follow the in-game icons (reference: the HSR wiki, biligame `<path>-大.png` and `<element>.png` files): Destruction is a tower in flames, the Hunt a stag head over crossed arrows, Erudition a four-point star with an eye in front of book spines, Harmony a three-blade swirl in a broken ring, Nihility a spiked eye-sun in two crescents, Preservation a shield with a rose stone, Abundance a laurel and wheat wreath, Remembrance a crescent moon with a candle, Elation a chalice under a sparkle; Physical is two crossed swords in a rhombus, Fire a layered flame, Ice a six-point star with a snowflake, Lightning a jagged crown with a bolt, Wind a spiral with a tail, Quantum a split oval, Imaginary a golden tree.

- Both tables are drawn in a 32-unit box through `emblemPen` (`fill`/`line` take SVG-like paths with M, L, Q, C, Z; `ribbon` is a stroke of varying width; `turn`, `spin` and `mirror` transform around the centre; colour `null` cuts a hole) and pixelated by `G.pixelArt`.
- Every art gets a detail tier: 0 below 15 px (one bold silhouette, no decoration), 1 up to 23 px, 2 from 24 px (secondary shapes in the dark tone, small cuts and highlights). Check new shapes at 11, 14, 20, 32 and 64 px.
- `pathIcon(ctx, path, x, y, size, col)` paints the main shape in `col` and secondary shapes in a 42% darker tone, with no outline. `elemIcon(ctx, elem, cx, cy, r)` paints the element colours (`col`, `dark`, lighter and deeper shades) inside a box of `2r + 3` px centred on the point, with the dark pixel outline, so it reads on light and dark panels.
- `pathShapes(path)` records the contours of the tier-2 Path art in 32-unit space; the Warp intro (`hsr-warp-intro` `trace`) draws its constellation lines along them.
- `node tools/hsr98-shots.cjs <dir> glyphs` renders every emblem at the sizes the UI uses, on dark and light backgrounds.
